~~~python
        st['d'][x] = st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x] if cp is None else min(st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x], cp)
        if m.kind == 'HOMB':
            st['Hm'][x] = (1 - m['nu']) * st['Hm'][x]
    if m.kind == 'N12':
        for x in (0, 1):
            if st['brk'][x] == 1 and x in pres:
                st['H'][x] += m['rhoH'] * P
    return Y, tmin

def n12_rest(m, st, r):
    for _ in range(r):
        for x in (0, 1):
            st['d'][x] = (1 - m['lam'][x]) * st['d'][x]
            if m.kind == 'N12':
                st['H'][x] = (1 - m['muH']) * st['H'][x]
            if m.kind == 'HOMB' and st['brk'][x] == 1:
                st['Hm'][x] = st['Hm'][x] + m['kH'] * (m['Hbar'] - st['Hm'][x])

def n12_run(m, st0, T, trials):
    """trials = [(P, pres, r, block, unblock), ...]"""
    st = {'d': list(st0['d']), 'H': list(st0['H']), 'brk': list(st0['brk']), 'Hm': list(st0['Hm']), 'events': []}
    out = []
    for k, (P, pres, r, block, unblock) in enumerate(trials):
        out.append(n12_trial(m, st, T, P, pres, block, unblock, k + 1))
        n12_rest(m, st, r)
    return out, st
~~~

A.3  t16d\_ext.py  Extension library: general trials with blockade (L₁), the combination HSP, exact tools for N5 recovery, the N12 breakthrough time

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Appendix extension library of The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0: a general trial with blockade (comparison component library L₁), the combination HSP, exact tools for N5 recovery, and the N12 breakthrough time.
Standard library and Fraction only.
  trial_blk: a blocked path does not open; the presentation set is passed unchanged to the post-passage update.
  HSP: the crosstalk increment is gated by the presentation 1[o ∈ Pres] of the path the crosstalk enters (c2, valid under the strict reading).
  eventual_sign, delta_R, unique_exact: the eventual sign, the effective depth and the exact first-opening-step comparison for N5 recovery; breach_trial: the breakthrough time with an initial head.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import math
import t16b_core as tb
import t16c_ext as tc
from t16b_core import g, open_step, BOTH
F = tb.F

def trial_blk(m, st, T, P, u=0, pres=BOTH, blocked=frozenset()):
    P = F(P)
    eff = frozenset(x for x in pres if x not in blocked)
    if m.kind == 'FS':
        return tb.fs_trial(m, st, T, P, eff)
    if m.kind == 'LIF':
        return tb.lif_trial(m, st, T, P, eff) if eff else _lif_noeff(m, st, P, pres)
    if m.kind == 'HSP':
        taus = [open_step(tb.g(m['g0'][x], m['c'][x], st['d'][x]), Fr(0), P, T) if x in eff else None for x in (0, 1)]
        fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
        Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if fin and taus[x] == min(fin)) if fin else frozenset()
        new = list(st['d'])
        for x in Y:
            new[x] = tb.clip(new[x] + m['alpha'][x], tb.cap_of(m, x))
        for x in Y:
            o = 1 - x
            if o in pres:                      # c2: increment κ multiplied by the gate 1[o ∈ Pres]
                new[o] = tb.clip(new[o] + m['kappa'], tb.cap_of(m, o))
        st['d'] = new
        return Y, (min(fin) if fin else None)
    h = st['h'] if m.kind == 'N3' else Fr(0)
    taus = [open_step(tb.theta(m, st, x), h, P, T) if x in eff else None for x in (0, 1)]
    fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
    Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if fin and taus[x] == min(fin)) if fin else frozenset()
    tb.passage(m, st, Y, u, P, pres)
    return Y, (min(fin) if fin else None)

def _lif_noeff(m, st, P, pres):
    if pres:
        st['V'] = m['rhoV'] * (st['V'] + P)
    return frozenset(), None

def rest_any(m, st, r):
    if m.kind == 'HSP':
        for _ in range(r):
            for x in (0, 1):
                st['d'][x] = (1 - m['lam'][x]) * st['d'][x]
    else:
        tb.rest(m, st, r)

def run_blk(m, st0, T, trials):
    """trials = [(P, u, pres, r, blocked), ...]"""
    st = tb.copy_state(st0)
    out = []
    for (P, u, pres, r, blk) in trials:
        out.append(trial_blk(m, st, T, P, u, pres, blk))
        rest_any(m, st, r)
    return out, st

# ---------------- Exact tools for N5 recovery ----------------
def eventual_sign(s0, L0, E0, mus, muL, muE):
    """Eventual sign of the untruncated value s0·ρs^R + L0·ρL^R − E0·ρE^R of δ_R (ρ = 1 − μ): merge coefficients by decay factor and take the sign of the nonzero merged coefficient at the largest decay factor (slowest decay).
    Returns +1, −1 or 0 (all merged coefficients 0). Terms with ρ = 0 act only at R = 0 and do not enter the eventual sign."""
    terms = {}
    for coef, mu in ((F(s0), F(mus)), (F(L0), F(muL)), (-F(E0), F(muE))):
        r = 1 - mu
        if coef != 0 and r > 0:
            terms[r] = terms.get(r, Fr(0)) + coef
    for r in sorted(terms, reverse=True):
        if terms[r] != 0:
            return 1 if terms[r] > 0 else -1
    return 0

def delta_R(s0, L0, E0, mus, muL, muE, R):
    v = F(s0) * (1 - F(mus)) ** R + F(L0) * (1 - F(muL)) ** R - F(E0) * (1 - F(muE)) ** R
    return v if v > 0 else Fr(0)

def unique_exact(g0, c, delta, P, T):
    """Two paths with the same threshold mechanism, path b with effective depth 0: compare the first-opening steps ⌈θ·T/P⌉ of the two paths (both reachable when θ ≤ g0 ≤ 1 ≤ P); returns (τ_a, τ_b, a sole lead?)"""
    ta = math.ceil(F(g0) / (1 + F(c) * delta) * T / F(P))
    tbb = math.ceil(F(g0) * T / F(P))
    return ta, tbb, ta < tbb

# ---------------- N12 breakthrough time ----------------
def breach_trial(Bbar, H0, rhoH, P):
    """Rest 0, blockade set once in trial 1, the blocked path presented in every trial thereafter, P constant, ρ_H·P > 0: the trial in which the breakthrough occurs (counting from 1)."""
    x = F(Bbar) - F(H0)
    if x <= 0:
        return 1
    return 1 + math.ceil(x / (F(rhoH) * F(P)))
~~~

A.4  t16e\_ext.py  Extension library: the components added in C\_cmp v0.2 (VST, BCM, CMP, DAM, PF, ERO, DIS, THR) and N13

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Appendix extension library of The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0: the components added in comparison class C_cmp v0.2, and N13.
Standard library and Fraction only.
Unified state: d, brk (blockade), H (accumulation), cnt (count of trials blocked and presented), ub (low-pass drive), thM (BCM sliding threshold).
Unified trial (execution contract D2.4):
  ①′ the protocol sets or lifts the blockade; self-releasing components check their conditions (PF: H ≥ B̄; ERO: cnt ≥ K_b; DIS: u = 1 in this trial; THR: P ≥ P_rel in this trial; DAM: as PF, plus scour);
  ①② thresholds and the global first-opened set Y are computed only for presented, unblocked paths;
  ③ K1/K3 → CMP (a path that did not pass loses κ_c) → VST (presented paths write according to drive strength) → BCM → PF/DAM/ERO accumulation;
  ④ rest: K2; H ← (1 − μ_H)H; ub ← (1 − μ_u)ub; thM ← (1 − μ_M)thM.
A component is switched on or off by whether its parameters are given; kind is only a label.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import math
import t16b_core as tb
from t16b_core import g, open_step, BOTH
F = tb.F

def new(d=(0, 0), H=(0, 0)):
    return {'d': [F(d[0]), F(d[1])], 'brk': [0, 0], 'H': [F(H[0]), F(H[1])], 'cnt': [0, 0],
            'ub': [Fr(0), Fr(0)], 'thM': [Fr(0), Fr(0)], 'events': []}

def cp(st):
    return {k: (list(v) if isinstance(v, list) else v) for k, v in st.items()}

def has(m, k):
    return m.get(k) is not None

def trial(m, st, T, P, u=0, pres=BOTH, block=(), unblock=(), k=0):
    P = F(P)
    # ① blockade setting, lifting and self-release
    for x in block:
        st['brk'][x] = 1
    for x in (0, 1):
        if st['brk'][x] != 1:
            continue
        release = x in unblock
        why = 'release' if release else None
        if not release and has(m, 'Bbar') and st['H'][x] >= m['Bbar'][x]:
            release, why = True, 'pressure'
        if not release and has(m, 'Kb') and st['cnt'][x] >= m['Kb']:
            release, why = True, 'erosion'
        if not release and has(m, 'dis') and u == 1:
            release, why = True, 'disinhibition'
        if not release and has(m, 'Prel') and P >= m['Prel']:   # THR: release as soon as this trial's drive exceeds the threshold (overload type)
            release, why = True, 'overload'
        if release:
            st['brk'][x] = 0
            if has(m, 'ks'):                       # DAM: on release, scour according to the head
                nd = st['d'][x] + m['ks'] * st['H'][x]
                cpx = tb.cap_of(m, x)
                st['d'][x] = nd if cpx is None else min(nd, cpx)
            st['events'].append((k, why, x))
            st['H'][x] = Fr(0); st['cnt'][x] = 0
    # ② global first-opened set
    taus = []
    for x in (0, 1):
        if x in pres and st['brk'][x] == 0:
            taus.append(open_step(g(m['g0'][x], m['c'][x], st['d'][x]), Fr(0), P, T))
        else:
            taus.append(None)
    fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
    Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if fin and taus[x] == min(fin)) if fin else frozenset()
    # ③ post-passage update (gating reads the pre-trial state: here each component reads only this trial's Y, P, Pres, Brk)
    pre_brk = list(st['brk'])
    for x in Y:
        st['d'][x] = tb.clip(st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x], tb.cap_of(m, x))
    if has(m, 'kc'):                               # CMP: heterosynaptic resource competition (N-13, Triesch type) instantiated on W: when x passes, the path that did not pass loses κ_c
        for x in Y:
            o = 1 - x
            if o not in Y:
                st['d'][o] = max(Fr(0), st['d'][o] - m['kc'])
    if has(m, 'Ap'):                               # VST: the voltage type (Clopath type) instantiated on W
        for x in (0, 1):
            if x in pres:
                gate = 1 if not has(m, 'vst_blk_only') else pre_brk[x]
                st['ub'][x] = (1 - m['muu']) * st['ub'][x] + m['muu'] * P
                ltp = m['Ap'] * max(Fr(0), P - m['thp']) * max(Fr(0), st['ub'][x] - m['thm'])
                st['d'][x] = tb.clip(st['d'][x] + gate * ltp, tb.cap_of(m, x))
    if has(m, 'eta'):                              # BCM: y = P·1[x ∈ Y]
        for x in (0, 1):
            y = P if x in Y else Fr(0)
            dd = m['eta'] * y * (y - st['thM'][x])
            st['d'][x] = tb.clip(max(Fr(0), st['d'][x] + dd), tb.cap_of(m, x))
            st['thM'][x] = (1 - m['muM']) * st['thM'][x] + m['muM'] * y * y
    for x in (0, 1):
        if st['brk'][x] == 1 and x in pres:
            if has(m, 'rhoH'):
                st['H'][x] += m['rhoH'] * P
            st['cnt'][x] += 1
    return Y, (min(fin) if fin else None)

def rest(m, st, r):
    for _ in range(r):
        for x in (0, 1):
            st['d'][x] = (1 - m['lam'][x]) * st['d'][x]
            if has(m, 'muH'):
                st['H'][x] = (1 - m['muH']) * st['H'][x]
            if has(m, 'muu'):
                st['ub'][x] = (1 - m['muu']) * st['ub'][x]
            if has(m, 'muM'):
                st['thM'][x] = (1 - m['muM']) * st['thM'][x]

def run(m, st0, T, trials):
    """trials = [(P, u, pres, r, block, unblock), ...]"""
    st = cp(st0)
    out = []
    for k, (P, u, pres, r, b, ub) in enumerate(trials):
        out.append(trial(m, st, T, P, u, pres, b, ub, k + 1))
        rest(m, st, r)
    return out, st

# ---------------- N13 (flow division and share-proportional write) ----------------
def n13_run(m, d0, T, trials):
    """The two paths share the entry flow pressure p_t = t·P/T; a presented path receives the divided pressure w_x·p_t, w_x = (1 + d_x)^ν / Σ_{y presented} (1 + d_y)^ν (distribution by deformation potential, ν a positive integer);
    x opens when w_x·p_t ≥ θ₀ (θ₀ is the same on both paths and does not vary with depth: depth acts only through the divided pressure); after passage d_x += α·w_x (potential configuration: scour deepening follows the divided pressure received); at rest d ← (1 − λ)d.
    trials = [(P, pres, r), ...]"""
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    out = []
    for (P, pres, r) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        ws = {x: (1 + d[x]) ** m['nu'] for x in pres}
        tot = sum(ws.values())
        w = {x: ws[x] / tot for x in pres}
        taus = []
        for x in (0, 1):
            if x in pres:
                t = math.ceil(m['th0'] * T / (w[x] * P))
                taus.append(t if t <= T else None)
            else:
                taus.append(None)
        fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
        Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if fin and taus[x] == min(fin)) if fin else frozenset()
        for x in Y:
            d[x] += m['alpha'] * w[x]
        for _ in range(r):
            d = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in d]
        out.append((Y, min(fin) if fin else None))
    return out, d
~~~

A.5  t16f\_ext.py  Extension library: KIR and DNC of C\_cmp v0.3, N14 on W16-E″ and its comparison members

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Appendix extension library of The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0: KIR and DNC of comparison class C_cmp v0.3, and N14 on W16-E″ with its comparison members.
Standard library and Fraction only.
1. KIR (reinforcement under parallel flow division): pool = the paths presented and unblocked in this trial; D_x = (1 + d_x)^ν; s_x = D_x/Σ_pool D_y;
  x opens at the first grid step with s_x·p_t ≥ θ₀, τ_x = ⌈θ₀·T/(s_x·P)⌉ (when ≤ T); with gateY = 0 every path in the pool gets d_x += α·s_x^μ, with gateY = 1 only x ∈ Y; at rest d ← (1 − λ)d.
2. DNC (C16 plus a divisive-normalization threshold term): R_x = D_x^n/(σ^n + Σ_pool D_y^n), D_x = 1 + d_x; θ_x = g(d_x) + θ_N·(1/R_x − 1); passage by K1; rest by K2.
3. N14 and the comparison members of q14 (affective input e ∈ {−1, 0, +1}).
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import math
import t16b_core as tb
from t16b_core import g, open_step, BOTH
F = tb.F

def _argmin(taus):
    fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
    if not fin:
        return frozenset(), None
    t = min(fin)
    return frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if taus[x] == t), t

# ---------------- KIR ----------------
def kir_run(m, d0, T, trials, gateY=1):
    """m: nu, th0, alpha, mu (positive integer), lam; trials = [(P, pres, r, block), ...] (block is the set of paths blocked in this trial)"""
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    out = []
    for (P, pres, r, blk) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        pool = [x for x in (0, 1) if x in pres and x not in blk]
        s = {}
        if pool:
            D = {x: (1 + d[x]) ** int(m['nu']) for x in pool}
            tot = sum(D.values())
            s = {x: D[x] / tot for x in pool}
        taus = []
        for x in (0, 1):
            if x in s:
                t = math.ceil(m['th0'] * T / (s[x] * P))
                taus.append(t if t <= T else None)
            else:
                taus.append(None)
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus)
        for x in pool:
            if (not gateY) or (x in Y):
                d[x] = d[x] + m['alpha'] * s[x] ** int(m['mu'])
        for _ in range(r):
            d = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in d]
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, d

# ---------------- DNC ----------------
def dnc_run(m, d0, T, trials):
    """c1(C16 v0.2, DN): m: g0, c, alpha, lam (pairs), n, sigma, thN; trials = [(P, pres, r), ...]"""
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    out = []
    for (P, pres, r) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        pool = [x for x in (0, 1) if x in pres]
        n = int(m['n'])
        tot = m['sigma'] ** n + sum((1 + d[y]) ** n for y in pool)
        taus = []
        for x in (0, 1):
            if x in pool:
                R = (1 + d[x]) ** n / tot
                th = g(m['g0'][x], m['c'][x], d[x]) + m['thN'] * (1 / R - 1)
                taus.append(open_step(th, Fr(0), P, T))
            else:
                taus.append(None)
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus)
        for x in Y:
            d[x] = d[x] + m['alpha'][x]
        for _ in range(r):
            d = [(1 - m['lam'][x]) * d[x] for x in (0, 1)]
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, d

# ---------------- N14 and the comparison members of q14 ----------------
def clip(v, lo, hi):
    return lo if v < lo else (hi if v > hi else v)

def e_run(m, st0, T, trials):
    """Unified implementation of N14 and the comparison members. State: d (depths of the two paths), c (the Coloring attached to the Imagery, shared by the two paths), F (shared facilitation), V (per-path value).
    trials = [(P, pres, r, e), ...]. A component is switched on or off by whether its parameters are given:
      Core: θ_x = g(d_x)·(1 − F) (F ≡ 0 without STF); on passage d_x += α·factor (below), then clipped to [0, D̄]; at rest d ← (1 − λ)d.
      N14: factor = 1 + κ·c; after a trial with nonempty Y, c ← clip(c + β·e, −c̄, c̄); at rest c ← (1 − μ_c)c.
      TF3e: on passage, add α_e·e as well (signed three-factor; writes only the passing path).
      RWe: on passage d_x ← d_x + η(e·V̄ − d_x) (signed RW; writes only the passing path).
      HS: when x passes, the other path += κ_h; HSg: the same but multiplied by 1[e = +1] (c2 under the strict reading: cross-path write gated by e).
      STF: after a trial in which some path is presented, F ← F + κ_F(F̄ − F); STFg: κ_F multiplied by 1[e = +1] (c2 under the strict reading: shared state gated by e).
      X3: per-path value V_x ← V_x + η_v(e − V_x) on passage; the K1 increment of any path y is multiplied by clip(a₀ + b₁·V_{oth}, 0, 1) (c2 under the broad reading: gating by the other path's state).
    """
    st = {'d': [F(st0['d'][0]), F(st0['d'][1])], 'c': F(st0.get('c', 0)), 'F': Fr(0), 'V': [Fr(0), Fr(0)]}
    out = []
    has = lambda k: m.get(k) is not None
    for (P, pres, r, e) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        taus = []
        for x in (0, 1):
            if x in pres:
                taus.append(open_step(g(m['g0'], m['cc'], st['d'][x]) * (1 - st['F']), Fr(0), P, T))
            else:
                taus.append(None)
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus)
        new = list(st['d'])
        for x in Y:
            fac = Fr(1)
            if has('kappa'):
                fac = 1 + m['kappa'] * st['c']
            if has('a0'):
                fac = fac * clip(m['a0'] + m['b1'] * st['V'][1 - x], Fr(0), Fr(1))
            v = new[x] + m['alpha'] * fac
            if has('alpha_e'):
                v += m['alpha_e'] * e
            if has('eta'):
                v = v + m['eta'] * (e * m['Vbar'] - v)
            new[x] = v
        if has('kh'):
            for x in Y:
                if (1 - x) not in Y:
                    gate = 1 if not has('hs_gate') else (1 if e == 1 else 0)
                    new[1 - x] = new[1 - x] + gate * m['kh']
        cap = m.get('cap')
        st['d'] = [clip(v, Fr(0), v if cap is None else cap) for v in new]
        if has('a0'):
            for x in Y:
                st['V'][x] = st['V'][x] + m['eta_v'] * (e - st['V'][x])
        if has('kF') and pres:
            gate = 1 if not has('stf_gate') else (1 if e == 1 else 0)
            st['F'] = st['F'] + gate * m['kF'] * (m['Fbar'] - st['F'])
        if has('kappa') and Y:
            st['c'] = clip(st['c'] + m['beta'] * e, -m['cbar'], m['cbar'])
        for _ in range(r):
            st['d'] = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in st['d']]
            if has('kappa'):
                st['c'] = (1 - m['muc']) * st['c']
            if has('kF'):
                st['F'] = (1 - m['muF']) * st['F']
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, st
~~~

A.6  t16g\_ext.py  Extension library: the c4 clipping-switch control; IZH, IZHc, TON and PIT of C\_cmp v0.4; N15 on W16-F, with GNG, GWR and MSP

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Appendix extension library of The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0: the control model for the c4 clipping switch, the neuromodulation and value components of C_cmp v0.4, N15 and the generation components on W16-F.
Standard library and Fraction only.
1. c4_example: a's clipping event switches b's write law via c4 (a control outside the non-interference class).
2. m_run (W16-E″): IZH (shared neuromodulator level D; after a trial with an opening, D ← clip(D + ε_D·e, −D̄, D̄); immediate eligibility or a cross-trial eligibility trace; write α_I·c_x·D);
  IZHc (eligibility trace, decaying trial by trial); TON (shared average reward R, acting on the threshold); PIT (shared Imagery value V, acting on the threshold).
3. s_run (W16-F: paths a, b and the latent Structural Position ℓ on the edge of a): N15, GNG, c4(GNG@full), GWR, MSP.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import math
import t16b_core as tb
from t16b_core import g, open_step
F = tb.F

def clip(v, lo, hi):
    return lo if v < lo else (hi if v > hi else v)

def _argmin(taus, keys):
    fin = [taus[k] for k in keys if taus[k] is not None]
    if not fin:
        return frozenset(), None
    t = min(fin)
    return frozenset(k for k in keys if taus[k] == t), t

# ---------------- 1. c4 clipping-switch control ----------------
def c4_example(e, T=100, n=2):
    """Pretraining: 1 trial presenting only a (P = 1, g0 = 1): a's TF3e increment e, capacity 1/2; c4 uses a's “bottom-reaching” event to switch b's write law from α_b = 0 to α_b = 1/4.
    Then b alone is tested n times (e = 0). Returns (pretraining Y, list of b's first-opening steps)."""
    da, db, mode = Fr(0), Fr(0), False
    th_a = g(1, 1, da)
    Ypre = frozenset((0,)) if open_step(th_a, Fr(0), Fr(1), T) is not None else frozenset()
    raw = da + e
    da = min(Fr(1, 2), max(Fr(0), raw))
    if raw > Fr(1, 2):
        mode = True                      # a's clipping event (affected by e)
    out = []
    for _ in range(n):
        out.append(open_step(g(1, 1, db), Fr(0), Fr(1), T))
        if mode:
            db += Fr(1, 4)
    return Ypre, out

# ---------------- 2. Neuromodulation and value components ----------------
def m_run(m, d0, T, trials):
    """trials = [(P, pres, r, e), ...]"""
    has = lambda k: m.get(k) is not None
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    D = Fr(0); R = Fr(0); V = Fr(0); el = [Fr(0), Fr(0)]
    out = []
    for (P, pres, r, e) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        fac = Fr(1)
        if has('psiT'):
            fac -= m['psiT'] * max(Fr(0), R)
        if has('psiV'):
            fac -= m['psiV'] * V
        fac = clip(fac, Fr(0), Fr(1))
        taus = {x: (open_step(g(m['g0'], m['cc'], d[x]) * fac, Fr(0), P, T) if x in pres else None) for x in (0, 1)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, (0, 1))
        new = list(d)
        for x in Y:
            new[x] = new[x] + m['alpha']
        if has('alphaI'):
            for x in (0, 1):
                if has('mu_el'):
                    el[x] = (1 - m['mu_el']) * el[x] + (1 if x in Y else 0)
                else:
                    el[x] = Fr(1) if x in Y else Fr(0)
                new[x] = new[x] + m['alphaI'] * el[x] * D
        d = [max(Fr(0), v) if m.get('cap') is None else clip(v, Fr(0), m['cap']) for v in new]
        if Y:
            if has('alphaI'):
                D = clip(D + m['epsD'] * e, -m['Dbar'], m['Dbar'])
            if has('etaR'):
                R = R + m['etaR'] * (max(0, e) - R)
            if has('etaV'):
                V = V + m['etaV'] * (e - V)
        if has('alphaI') and has('da_per_trial'):
            D = (1 - m['muD']) * D
        for _ in range(r):
            d = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in d]
            if has('alphaI'):
                D = (1 - m['muD']) * D
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, {'d': d, 'D': D, 'R': R, 'V': V}

# ---------------- 3. W16-F and N15 ----------------
L = 2   # index of the latent Structural Position ℓ

def s_run(m, st0, T, trials):
    """W16-F: paths a(0), b(1); ℓ(2) is a latent Structural Position on the edge of a and does not exist before generation; after generation it is a path, presented if and only if a is presented (the Information of Edge-Seepage closely correlates with the Information of the Domain core),
    and has its own blockade flag (blocking a does not block ℓ). trials = [(P, pres, r, block)], block being the set of paths blocked in this trial (for this trial only).
    Generation laws (determined by the parameters):
      N15: when a passes and was already full before passage (d_a = D̄_a), the increment α_a enters the generation amount s of ℓ; when s ≥ σ, ℓ is generated, d_ℓ = s − σ, s ← 0; at rest s ← (1 − μ_s)s.
      GNG (W reading of Fritzke 1995): the error err_x of each path increases by θ_x² when x passes (the pre-trial threshold, read as quantization error); every λ trials in which some path is presented, if the path with the largest error is a and ℓ has not been generated,
        ℓ is inserted between a and its neighbor b: d_ℓ = (d_a + d_b)/2, err_a and err_b are multiplied by α_G, err_ℓ = the new err_a; at the end of every trial all errors are multiplied by d_G.
        When gng_after_full is given, this is the c4 combination: the insertion law is enabled only after a's “bottom-reaching” event, and the count starts from that event.
      GWR (W reading of Marsland, Shapiro and Nehmzow 2002): when a passes, a's firing count h (initial value 1) takes one discrete step of Stanley habituation, h ← max(0, h + (α_h(1 − h) − 1)/τ_h);
        mismatch is read as “entering although already full before passage”; on a mismatch with h < h_T, ℓ is inserted on the edge of a, d_ℓ = d_a/2 (W reading of the literature formula w_r = (w_s + x)/2, with x read as 0).
      MSP (W reading of Butz–van Ooyen 2013; the linear growth curve follows the common formulation, and no equation-by-equation match with the original has been established): a's activity A ← (1 − μ_A)A + μ_A·1[a ∈ Y];
        free structural elements on the edge of a: z ← max(0, z + ν_M(1 − A/ε_M)); when z ≥ 1, ℓ is generated, d_ℓ = 0.
    After generation ℓ follows C16: threshold g(d_ℓ), on passage d_ℓ += α_ℓ (clipped at D̄_ℓ), shallowing at rest."""
    st = {'d': [F(st0['d'][0]), F(st0['d'][1]), Fr(0)], 'E': 0, 's': Fr(0), 'err': [Fr(0)] * 3, 'cnt': 0, 'armed': 0,
          'A': Fr(0), 'z': Fr(0), 'gen_at': None}
    has = lambda k: m.get(k) is not None
    cap = [m['capa'], m['capb'], m['capl']]
    out = []
    for k, (P, pres, r, blk) in enumerate(trials, 1):
        P = F(P)
        live = [0, 1] + ([L] if st['E'] else [])
        prs = set(pres) | ({L} if (st['E'] and 0 in pres) else set())
        th = {x: g(m['g0'], m['cc'], st['d'][x]) for x in live}
        taus = {x: (open_step(th[x], Fr(0), P, T) if (x in prs and x not in blk) else None) for x in (0, 1, L)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, live)
        full_before = st['d'][0] >= cap[0]
        reached = False
        for x in Y:
            nv = st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x]
            if nv >= cap[x] and not (st['d'][x] >= cap[x]):
                reached = True if x == 0 else reached
            st['d'][x] = min(nv, cap[x])
        if 0 in Y and full_before:
            reached = True
        if has('sigma') and 0 in Y and full_before and not st['E']:
            st['s'] += m['alpha'][0]
            if st['s'] >= m['sigma']:
                st['E'] = 1; st['d'][L] = st['s'] - m['sigma']; st['s'] = Fr(0); st['gen_at'] = k
        if has('lamG'):
            for x in Y:
                st['err'][x] += th[x] ** 2
            if has('gng_after_full') and reached and not st['armed']:
                st['armed'] = 1; st['cnt'] = 0
            if prs and (not has('gng_after_full') or st['armed']):
                st['cnt'] += 1
                if st['cnt'] % int(m['lamG']) == 0 and not st['E']:
                    q = max((0, 1), key=lambda x: (st['err'][x], -x))
                    if q == 0 and st['err'][0] > 0:
                        st['E'] = 1; st['d'][L] = (st['d'][0] + st['d'][1]) / 2
                        st['err'][0] *= m['alphaG']; st['err'][1] *= m['alphaG']; st['err'][L] = st['err'][0]
                        st['gen_at'] = k
            st['err'] = [m['dG'] * v for v in st['err']]
        if has('hT'):                                   # GWR: a's firing count (habituation) and “mismatch” (entering although already full)
            if 0 in Y:
                st['h'] = max(Fr(0), st.get('h', Fr(1)) + (m['alh'] * (1 - st.get('h', Fr(1))) - 1) / m['tauh'])
            if 0 in Y and full_before and not st['E'] and st.get('h', Fr(1)) < m['hT']:
                st['E'] = 1; st['d'][L] = st['d'][0] / 2; st['gen_at'] = k
        if has('nuM') and not st['E']:
            st['A'] = (1 - m['muA']) * st['A'] + m['muA'] * (1 if 0 in Y else 0)
            st['z'] = max(Fr(0), st['z'] + m['nuM'] * (1 - st['A'] / m['epsM']))
            if st['z'] >= 1:
                st['E'] = 1; st['d'][L] = Fr(0); st['gen_at'] = k
        for _ in range(r):
            st['d'] = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in st['d']]
            if has('sigma'):
                st['s'] = (1 - m['mus']) * st['s']
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, st
~~~

A.7  t16h\_ext.py  Extension library: N15 full state and bookkeeping of the three events, the delayed-transfer control, BIM, an independent implementation of N14, IZD, Protocol C and the c4 mode model

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Appendix extension library of The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0. Standard library and Fraction only.
1. n15_run: full-state implementation of N15 on W16-F (an initial state of ℓ may be given; records the birth, the first entry into the readable pool and the first trial in which ℓ opens first).
2. delayed_gate: b's write is gated by a's state (c2 under the broad reading; a control outside the non-interference class).
3. bim_run: the broadcast-integrator representation M = 1 + κc of N14; the write reads the old M, then M is updated.
4. IZD and izd_run: a fixed discretization of the Izhikevich 2007 model (interface, sampling, time and parameters all fixed).
5. n14_run: an independent implementation of N14 (with shared-state reset); c4_run: the mode model in which a's clipping event switches b's write law (for the shared-state reset protocol).
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import math
import t16b_core as tb
from t16b_core import g, open_step
F = tb.F
INF = None

def _argmin(taus, keys):
    fin = [taus[k] for k in keys if taus[k] is not None]
    if not fin:
        return frozenset(), None
    t = min(fin)
    return frozenset(k for k in keys if taus[k] == t), t

# ---------------- 1. N15 full state ----------------
def n15_run(m, st0, T, trials):
    """st0 = {'d': (d_a, d_b, d_l), 'E': 0/1, 's': s}; trials = [(P, pres, r, block)]. Returns out, st; st['birth'], st['pool'], st['open'] are the trial numbers of the three events."""
    st = {'d': [F(v) for v in st0['d']], 'E': st0.get('E', 0), 's': F(st0.get('s', 0)),
          'birth': None, 'pool': None, 'open': None}
    cap = [m['capa'], m['capb'], m['capl']]
    out = []
    for k, (P, pres, r, blk) in enumerate(trials, 1):
        P = F(P)
        live = [0, 1] + ([2] if st['E'] else [])
        prs = set(pres) | ({2} if (st['E'] and 0 in pres) else set())
        pool = [x for x in live if x in prs and x not in blk]
        if st['E'] and 2 in pool and st['pool'] is None:
            st['pool'] = k
        taus = {x: (open_step(g(m['g0'], m['cc'], st['d'][x]), Fr(0), P, T) if x in pool else None) for x in (0, 1, 2)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, live)
        if 2 in Y and st['open'] is None:
            st['open'] = k
        full_before = st['d'][0] >= cap[0]
        for x in Y:
            st['d'][x] = min(st['d'][x] + m['alpha'][x], cap[x])
        if 0 in Y and full_before and not st['E']:
            st['s'] += m['alpha'][0]
            if m['sigma'] > 0 and st['s'] >= m['sigma']:
                st['E'] = 1; st['d'][2] = st['s'] - m['sigma']; st['s'] = Fr(0); st['birth'] = k
        for _ in range(r):
            st['d'] = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in st['d']]
            st['s'] = (1 - m['mus']) * st['s']
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, st

# ---------------- 2. Delayed-transfer gating control ----------------
def delayed_gate(e, T=100):
    """a uses TF3e: initial state 0, capacity 1/2; when a opens, d_a ← clip(d_a + e, 0, 1/2); b writes (1/2)·d_a only when b opens (c2 under the broad reading: b's K1 gated by a's state).
    Pretraining: 1 trial presenting only a; test: 2 trials presenting only b, e = 0, P = 1. Returns (d_b after pretraining, list of b's first-opening steps)."""
    da, db = Fr(0), Fr(0)
    if open_step(g(1, 1, da), Fr(0), Fr(1), T) is not None:
        da = max(Fr(0), min(Fr(1, 2), da + e))
    pre_b = db
    out = []
    for _ in range(2):
        out.append(open_step(g(1, 1, db), Fr(0), Fr(1), T))
        db = db + Fr(1, 2) * da
    return pre_b, out

# ---------------- 3. Broadcast-integrator reparameterization ----------------
def clip(v, lo, hi):
    return lo if v < lo else (hi if v > hi else v)

def bim_run(m, d0, T, trials):
    """m: g0, cc, alpha, lam, kappa, beta, cbar, muc (parameters with the same names as in N14); trials = [(P, pres, r, e)]"""
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    Mv = Fr(1)
    lo, hi = 1 - m['kappa'] * m['cbar'], 1 + m['kappa'] * m['cbar']
    out = []
    for (P, pres, r, e) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        taus = {x: (open_step(g(m['g0'], m['cc'], d[x]), Fr(0), P, T) if x in pres else None) for x in (0, 1)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, (0, 1))
        for x in Y:
            d[x] = max(Fr(0), d[x] + m['alpha'] * Mv)
        if Y:
            Mv = clip(Mv + m['kappa'] * m['beta'] * e, lo, hi)
        for _ in range(r):
            Mv = 1 + (1 - m['muc']) * (Mv - 1)
            d = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in d]
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, {'d': d, 'M': Mv}

# ---------------- 4. Fixed discretization of Izhikevich 2007 ----------------
IZD = dict(
    dt=Fr(1, 10),            # each window is 100 ms: one trial = one window; one rest beat = one window
    rho_c=Fr(9048, 10000),   # exp(−0.1 s / τ_c), τ_c = 1 s, four-digit rational approximation
    rho_d=Fr(6065, 10000),   # exp(−0.1 s / τ_d), τ_d = 0.2 s
    s0=Fr(1, 1000),          # tonic source 0.01 μM/s × 0.1 s
    jump=Fr(1, 2),           # reward steps DA up by 0.5 μM
    Ap=Fr(1),                # one “passage” (pre-before-post pairing) increases the eligibility trace by A₊ (normalized)
    kw=Fr(10),               # weight scale: w increases by 10 per μM·s per unit of eligibility (interface parameter, fixed)
    wbar=Fr(4),              # weight upper bound 4 (literature: 0–4 mV)
)

def izd_run(m, d0, T, trials, reset_at=None):
    """Depth d_x is the weight w_x; threshold g(w_x). Order within each window: ① threshold ② Y ③ E_x += A₊ for x ∈ Y; D += jump when there is an opening and e = +1 (the outcome follows the response; e = −1 has no counterpart and releases nothing)
    ④ w_x += kw·dt·E_x·D (all x, clipped to [0, w̄]) ⑤ E ← ρ_c·E, D ← ρ_d·D + s0. Rest beat: only ④⑤. The initial value of D is the tonic steady state s0/(1 − ρ_d).
    reset_at: reset D to the tonic steady state before the start of that trial (selective intervention on the shared state in Protocol C). trials = [(P, pres, r, e)]"""
    P_ = IZD
    w = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    E = [Fr(0), Fr(0)]
    Dstar = P_['s0'] / (1 - P_['rho_d'])
    D = Dstar
    out = []
    def tail():
        nonlocal D, E, w
        w = [clip(w[x] + P_['kw'] * P_['dt'] * E[x] * D, Fr(0), P_['wbar']) for x in (0, 1)]
        E = [P_['rho_c'] * v for v in E]
        D = P_['rho_d'] * D + P_['s0']
    for k, (P, pres, r, e) in enumerate(trials, 1):
        if reset_at is not None and k == reset_at:
            D = Dstar
        P = F(P)
        taus = {x: (open_step(g(m['g0'], m['cc'], w[x]), Fr(0), P, T) if x in pres else None) for x in (0, 1)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, (0, 1))
        for x in Y:
            E[x] += P_['Ap']
        if Y and e == 1:
            D += P_['jump']
        tail()
        for _ in range(r):
            tail()
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, {'w': w, 'E': E, 'D': D}

def n14_run(m, d0, T, trials, reset_at=None):
    """N14 (an independent implementation with the same law as t16f_ext.e_run, plus the Protocol C reset: c ← 0 before the start of trial reset_at). trials = [(P, pres, r, e)]"""
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    c = Fr(0)
    out = []
    for k, (P, pres, r, e) in enumerate(trials, 1):
        if reset_at is not None and k == reset_at:
            c = Fr(0)
        P = F(P)
        taus = {x: (open_step(g(m['g0'], m['cc'], d[x]), Fr(0), P, T) if x in pres else None) for x in (0, 1)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, (0, 1))
        for x in Y:
            d[x] = max(Fr(0), d[x] + m['alpha'] * (1 + m['kappa'] * c))
        if Y:
            c = clip(c + m['beta'] * e, -m['cbar'], m['cbar'])
        for _ in range(r):
            c = (1 - m['muc']) * c
            d = [(1 - m['lam']) * v for v in d]
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, {'d': d, 'c': c}

# ---------------- 5. c4 mode model ----------------
def c4_run(d0, T, trials, reset_at=None, cap_a=Fr(1, 2), alpha_on=Fr(1, 4)):
    """a: TF3e (on passage d_a ← clip(d_a + e, 0, cap_a)); b: K1 coefficient initially 0, switched by c4 to alpha_on after a's clipping event (the unclipped result of the increment exceeds cap_a).
    No shared neuromodulatory state; reset_at has no effect on it (it resets only the shared neuromodulation and does not touch the mode). trials = [(P, pres, r, e)]"""
    d = [F(d0[0]), F(d0[1])]
    mode = False
    out = []
    for (P, pres, r, e) in trials:
        P = F(P)
        taus = {x: (open_step(g(1, 1, d[x]), Fr(0), P, T) if x in pres else None) for x in (0, 1)}
        Y, tm = _argmin(taus, (0, 1))
        if 0 in Y:
            raw = d[0] + e
            d[0] = max(Fr(0), min(cap_a, raw))
            if raw > cap_a:
                mode = True
        if 1 in Y and mode:
            d[1] = d[1] + alpha_on
        out.append((Y, tm))
    return out, {'d': d, 'mode': mode}
~~~

A.8  snn\_core.py  Program of the frozen SNN instance s1 (verbatim identical to Appendix A.1 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* v1.0, its SHA-256 equal to the value recorded in Appendix B.1 of that paper; used by J1 in A.17)

~~~python
"""Frozen SNN instance s1: a replayable SNN instance (program host). Exact rational arithmetic, Python standard library, deterministic, replayable event by event.
Host instance χ_SNN = (P, L, B, W):
  P this program; L the algorithmic level (discrete time steps); B = {4 input neurons, 2 output LIF neurons, 8 synapses}; W the declared window.
  Input-neuron firing is fixed by an external stimulus sequence (out-of-boundary signal); the teaching current teach is injected into output 0 only during the formation phase (out-of-boundary signal).
  Output neurons: LIF, v ← v·LEAK + Σ w_ij·s_i + I_ext; when v ≥ θ the neuron fires and resets to 0.
  Plasticity: pair-based STDP (trace form): presynaptic traces x_i and postsynaptic traces y_j are multiplied by DEC each step and incremented by 1 on firing;
    when postsynaptic neuron j fires (via the backpropagated signal reaching the synapse), w_ij += A_PLUS·x_i; when presynaptic neuron i fires, w_ij -= A_MINUS·y_j;
    weights are clipped to [0, W_MAX]. Optional relaxation toward baseline (RELAX, default 0).
  Intervention switches: plastic (plasticity on/off); bap (whether postsynaptic firing propagates back to the synapse; when off, STDP does not see postsynaptic firing, i.e. the route is cut).
Trace-Response: above-baseline weight z_ij = w_ij − W0.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
import hashlib
N_IN, N_OUT = 4, 2
W0 = F(1, 4)
LEAK, THETA, DEC = F(3, 4), F(1), F(1, 2)
A_PLUS, A_MINUS, W_MAX = F(1, 8), F(1, 16), F(2)
PAT = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}

def init(w=None):
    return dict(w=[[W0 if w is None else w[i][j] for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)],
                v=[F(0)] * N_OUT, x=[F(0)] * N_IN, y=[F(0)] * N_OUT)

def step(st, inp, teach=(0, 0), plastic=True, bap=True, relax=F(0)):
    """One step: returns (new state, event record). The event record lists presynaptic firing, postsynaptic firing and the plasticity components of each synapse (positive, negative, relaxation)."""
    w = [row[:] for row in st['w']]
    s_in = PAT[inp]
    v = [st['v'][j] * LEAK + sum(w[i][j] * s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)) + teach[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    s_out = [1 if v[j] >= THETA else 0 for j in range(N_OUT)]
    v = [F(0) if s_out[j] else v[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    x = [st['x'][i] * DEC + s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)]
    y = [st['y'][j] * DEC + s_out[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    comp = {}
    for i in range(N_IN):
        for j in range(N_OUT):
            pos = A_PLUS * x[i] if (plastic and bap and s_out[j]) else F(0)
            neg = -A_MINUS * y[j] if (plastic and s_in[i] and bap) else F(0)
            rel = -relax * (w[i][j] - W0) if plastic else F(0)
            nw = min(W_MAX, max(F(0), w[i][j] + pos + neg + rel))
            comp[(i, j)] = (pos, neg, rel, nw - w[i][j])
            w[i][j] = nw
    ev = dict(inp=inp, teach=teach, s_in=s_in, s_out=tuple(s_out), comp=comp)
    return dict(w=w, v=v, x=x, y=y), ev

def run(hist, st=None, **kw):
    """hist: [(input name, teaching current)]. Returns (final state, event log)."""
    st = st or init()
    log = []
    for inp, teach in hist:
        st, ev = step(st, inp, teach, **kw)
        log.append(ev)
    return st, log

def log_hash(log):
    """Fingerprint of the frozen log: SHA-256 of the events serialized in a fixed order."""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for ev in log:
        h.update(repr((ev['inp'], ev['teach'], ev['s_in'], ev['s_out'],
                       sorted((k, tuple(str(c) for c in v)) for k, v in ev['comp'].items()))).encode())
    return h.hexdigest()

def z(st):
    return [[st['w'][i][j] - W0 for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)]
~~~

A.9  run16\_1.py  Run entry: sections R0–R6 (Propositions 4-1, 16-7, 4-3; N2; N3; N5; signature table; Tier B bounds of Misjudgment)

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_1.py (sections R1–R6).
Run: python3 run16_1.py. The output is deterministic. The program provides only finite verification, explicit witnesses and contrasts; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, math
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import t16b_core as tb
from t16b_core import M, new_state, run, tr, seqstr, ystr, BOTH
R = random.Random(16101)
A, B = frozenset((0,)), frozenset((1,))
here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def rq(lo, hi, den=12):
    a = math.ceil(Fr(lo) * den)
    b = math.floor(Fr(hi) * den)
    return Fr(R.randint(a, b), den)

def sha(p):
    with open(p, 'rb') as fh:
        return hashlib.sha256(fh.read()).hexdigest()

def rand_g():
    return (rq(Fr(1, 4), 1), rq(Fr(1, 4), 1)), (rq(Fr(1, 4), 2), rq(Fr(1, 4), 2))

def rand_C(kind='C', cap=True, same=False, **extra):
    if same:
        g0 = rq(Fr(1, 4), 1); c = rq(Fr(1, 4), 2)
        g0s, cs = (g0, g0), (c, c)
        a = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1); al = (a, a)
        cp = rq(Fr(1, 2), 3) if cap else None
        caps = (cp, cp)
    else:
        g0s, cs = rand_g()
        al = (rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1))
        caps = (rq(Fr(1, 2), 3), rq(Fr(1, 2), 3)) if cap else (None, None)
    lam = (rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), rq(Fr(1, 12), 1))
    return M(kind, g0=g0s, c=cs, alpha=al, lam=lam, cap=caps, **extra)

def rand_d(m, top=3):
    cp = m.get('cap')
    out = []
    for x in (0, 1):
        hi = top if (cp is None or cp[x] is None) else min(top, cp[x])
        out.append(rq(0, hi))
    return tuple(out)

def rand_trials(nmax=10, Pchoices=(Fr(1, 3), Fr(1, 2), 1, Fr(3, 2)), rest_max=3, allow_u=True, allow_pres=True):
    n = R.randint(1, nmax)
    out = []
    for _ in range(n):
        P = R.choice(Pchoices)
        u = R.randint(0, 1) if allow_u else 0
        pres = R.choice([BOTH, BOTH, BOTH, A, B]) if allow_pres else BOTH
        out.append((Fr(P), u, pres, R.randint(0, rest_max)))
    return out

print('The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_1.py')
print('=' * 72)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R0
print('\nR0  Version')
print('  t16b_core.py SHA-256: ' + sha(os.path.join(here, 't16b_core.py')))
print('  Threshold family G: g(d) = g0/(1 + c·d); W16-E grid p_t = h + t·P/T.')
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R1
print('\nR1  C16 v0.2 (K1 + K2(λ > 0) + K3 clipping + K6): I1, I2, ∅ absorption; two-path presentation protocols from symmetric initial states; presentation sets break the symmetry')
n_tot = 0; v1 = v2 = va = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    m = rand_C('C', cap=R.random() < 0.7)
    st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
    trials = rand_trials()
    out, _ = run(m, st, R.choice([3, 4, 7, 10]), trials)
    n_tot += 1
    v1 += tb.viol_I1(out, trials) is not None
    v2 += tb.viol_I2(out, trials) is not None
    va += tb.viol_absorb(out, trials) is not None
print('  %d random runs (with rest, presentation sets, reinforcement input, P ∈ {1/3, 1/2, 1, 3/2}): I1 violations %d, I2 violations %d, ∅-absorption violations %d' % (n_tot, v1, v2, va))
# symmetric initial state: m_C and m_B (N1b) from the symmetric zero initial state, λ = 1/10 (K2 requires λ > 0); exhaustive over protocols with both paths presented
mC = M('C', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(None, None))
mB = M('N1', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(None, None), phi=Fr(1, 2), mu=1, fcap=Fr(1, 2))
import itertools
cnt = diff = 0
for n in range(1, 6):
    for rests in itertools.product((0, 1, 3), repeat=n):
        for T in (3, 4, 10):
            for P in (Fr(1, 2), 1, Fr(3, 2)):
                trials = [(Fr(P), 0, BOTH, r) for r in rests]
                o1, _ = run(mC, new_state(mC), T, trials)
                o2, _ = run(mB, new_state(mB), T, trials)
                cnt += 1
                diff += [y for y, _ in o1] != [y for y, _ in o2]
print('  Symmetric initial state (λ = 1/10, both paths presented, n ≤ 5, rest ∈ {0,1,3}, T ∈ {3,4,10}, P ∈ {1/2,1,3/2}): %d protocols, %d with different Y sequences' % (cnt, diff))
trials = [(Fr(1), 0, A, 0), (Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)]
o1, _ = run(mC, new_state(mC), 4, trials)
o2, _ = run(mB, new_state(mB), 4, trials)
print('  Presentation-set protocol [present a only, P = 1] → [both paths presented, P = 1], T = 4: m_C: %s; m_B: %s (the symmetric blind spot is broken within Π₀ of W16-E)' % (seqstr(o1, True), seqstr(o2, True)))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R2
print('\nR2  Candidate N2 “Seep-Overflow”')
# (a) I2, ∅ absorption
v2 = va = v1 = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    m = rand_C('N2', cap=True)
    st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
    trials = rand_trials()
    out, _ = run(m, st, R.choice([3, 4, 7, 10]), trials)
    v2 += tb.viol_I2(out, trials) is not None
    va += tb.viol_absorb(out, trials) is not None
    v1 += tb.viol_I1(out, trials) is not None
print('  (a) 3000 random runs: I2 violations %d, ∅-absorption violations %d (Theorem N2-1: both hold on N2); I1 violations %d (I1 does not hold on N2)' % (v2, va, v1))
# (b) explicit witness
m = M('N2', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2))
out, st = run(m, new_state(m, d=(1, 0)), 10, tr(6))
mc = M('C', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2))
outc, stc = run(mc, new_state(mc, d=(1, 0)), 10, tr(6))
print('  (b) witness g = 1/(1+d), α = 1, D̄ = 2, initial state (1, 0), P = 1, T = 10, rest 0: N2: %s, final state %s; C16 v0.2 with the same parameters: %s, final state %s'
      % (seqstr(out, True), tuple(st['d']), seqstr(outc, True), tuple(stc['d'])))
# (c) Theorem N2-2: bound on first inclusion and terminal state on N2₌
amin, Dmax = Fr(1, 6), Fr(3)
Kb = 1 + 2 * math.ceil(Dmax / amin)
worst = 0; badK = badF = 0; nU = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    m = rand_C('N2', cap=True, same=True)
    if m['alpha'][0] < amin or m['cap'][0] > Dmax:
        continue
    st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
    T = R.choice([4, 10, 20, 50])
    out, _ = run(m, st, T, tr(Kb + 3))
    if len(out[0][0]) != 1:
        continue
    nU += 1
    x = next(iter(out[0][0])); o = 1 - x
    k1 = next((k for k, (Y, _) in enumerate(out) if o in Y), None)
    if k1 is None or k1 + 1 > Kb:
        badK += 1
    else:
        worst = max(worst, k1 + 1)
    K = 1 + 2 * math.ceil(m['cap'][0] / m['alpha'][0])
    if any(Y != BOTH for (Y, _) in out[K - 1:]):
        badF += 1
print('  (c) N2₌ (same threshold mechanism on both paths, same α ≥ 1/6, same capacity ≤ 3), %d runs with a sole lead on the first trial: first inclusion of the other path later than the bound 1 + 2⌈D̄_max/α_min⌉ = %d in %d, latest observed %d; '
      'not {a,b} from trial K = 1 + 2⌈D̄/α⌉ onward in %d' % (nU, Kb, badK, worst, badF))
# (d) Theorem N2-3: capacity takeover
m = M('N2', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(1, 3))
out, st = run(m, new_state(m, d=(Fr(1, 2), 0)), 20, tr(14))
print('  (d) capacity-takeover witness (D̄_a = 1 < D̄_b = 3, α = 1/2, initial state (1/2, 0), T = 20): %s, final state %s' % (seqstr(out), tuple(st['d'])))
bad = cnt = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    g0 = rq(Fr(1, 2), 1); c = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); a = rq(Fr(1, 6), 1)
    Da = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); Db = Da + rq(Fr(1, 2), 2)
    T = 200
    m = M('N2', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), alpha=(a, a), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(Da, Db))
    gap = g0 / (1 + c * Da) - g0 / (1 + c * Db)
    if not (Fr(1, T) < gap):
        continue
    st = new_state(m, d=(rq(0, Da), rq(0, Da)))
    out, stf = run(m, st, T, tr(1 + 2 * math.ceil(Db / a) + 2 * math.ceil(Da / a) + 4))
    if len(out[0][0]) != 1:
        continue
    cnt += 1
    if out[-1][0] != B:
        bad += 1
print('  (d′) %d random runs (D̄_a < D̄_b, P/T < g(D̄_a) − g(D̄_b), sole lead on the first trial): last trial not {b} in %d' % (cnt, bad))
# (e) Tier A (conditioned on the observable event U: sole lead on the first trial): N2₌ vs C16 v0.2
n = Kb
cC = cN = 0; badC = badN = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    for kind in ('C', 'N2'):
        m = rand_C(kind, cap=True, same=(kind == 'N2'))
        if kind == 'N2' and (m['alpha'][0] < amin or m['cap'][0] > Dmax):
            continue
        st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
        out, _ = run(m, st, R.choice([4, 10, 20]), tr(n))
        if len(out[0][0]) != 1:
            continue
        last2 = len(out[-1][0]) == 2
        if kind == 'C':
            cC += 1; badC += last2
        else:
            cN += 1; badN += (not last2)
print('  (e) protocol q_N2 (n = %d trials, P = 1, rest 0, both paths presented) on event U: of %d C16 v0.2 runs, both paths open simultaneously on the last trial in %d; of %d N2₌ runs, not both open simultaneously on the last trial in %d' % (n, cC, badC, cN, badN))
# (f) conjugacy with fill-and-spill (independent implementation)
same = tot = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    m = rand_C('N2', cap=True)
    d0 = rand_d(m)
    fsm = M('FS', g0=m['g0'], c=m['c'], V=m['alpha'], Cap=m['cap'], leak=m['lam'])
    trials = rand_trials(allow_u=False)
    T = R.choice([3, 4, 7, 10])
    o1, s1 = run(m, new_state(m, d=d0), T, trials)
    o2, s2 = run(fsm, tb.fs_state(S=d0), T, trials)
    tot += 1
    same += (o1 == o2 and s1['d'] == s2['S'])
print('  (f) N2 vs fill-and-spill∘first-opening competition (independent implementation, renamed d↔S, α↔V, D̄↔Cap, λ↔leak): %d random runs, readings and final states identical in %d' % (tot, same))
# (g) comparison with the c4 combination HSsat
eq_lat = tot_lat = eq_gen = tot_gen = eq_gen_read = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    m = rand_C('N2', cap=True, same=True)
    a = m['alpha'][0]; cp = m['cap'][0]
    hs = M('HSsat', g0=m['g0'], c=m['c'], alpha=m['alpha'], lam=m['lam'], cap=m['cap'])
    trials = rand_trials(allow_u=False)
    T = R.choice([4, 10])
    kmax = math.floor(cp / a)
    d_lat = (cp - R.randint(0, kmax) * a, cp - R.randint(0, kmax) * a)
    o1, s1 = run(m, new_state(m, d=d_lat), T, [(P, u, pr, 0) for (P, u, pr, r) in trials])
    o2, s2 = run(hs, new_state(hs, d=d_lat), T, [(P, u, pr, 0) for (P, u, pr, r) in trials])
    tot_lat += 1; eq_lat += (o1 == o2 and s1['d'] == s2['d'])
    d_gen = rand_d(m)
    o1, s1 = run(m, new_state(m, d=d_gen), T, [(P, u, pr, 0) for (P, u, pr, r) in trials])
    o2, s2 = run(hs, new_state(hs, d=d_gen), T, [(P, u, pr, 0) for (P, u, pr, r) in trials])
    tot_gen += 1; eq_gen += (o1 == o2 and s1['d'] == s2['d']); eq_gen_read += (o1 == o2)
print('  (g) N2 vs c4(CLIP→crosstalk) (HSsat, the crossing step does not split off the overflow excess): rest 0; of %d runs from grid initial states (D̄ − initial depth an integer multiple of α), readings and final states identical in %d; of %d runs from general initial states, fully identical in %d, readings alone identical in %d'
      % (tot_lat, eq_lat, tot_gen, eq_gen, eq_gen_read))
m = M('N2', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2))
hs = M('HSsat', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2))
o1, s1 = run(m, new_state(m, d=(Fr(3, 2), 0)), 20, tr(3))
o2, s2 = run(hs, new_state(hs, d=(Fr(3, 2), 0)), 20, tr(3))
print('      Crossing-step witness (initial state (3/2, 0), T = 20): N2 %s final state %s; HSsat %s final state %s' % (seqstr(o1, True), tuple(s1['d']), seqstr(o2, True), tuple(s2['d'])))
# (h) two-arm probe (two independent runs from the same initial state d = (0, 0)): arm 1 [present b only]; arm 2 [present a only × m, then present b only]; compare τ_b on the last trial
amin2, amax2, Dmin2, Dmax2 = Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)
g0min2, cmin2, cmax2 = Fr(3, 4), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2)
m_small = math.ceil(Dmin2 / amax2) - 1
dlt = Fr(1, 4)
m_large = math.ceil((Dmax2 + dlt) / amin2)
Tpr = 400
print('  (h) two-arm probe q_I4(m): class bounds α ∈ [%s, %s], D̄ ∈ [%s, %s], g0 ≥ %s, c ∈ [%s, %s]; m_small = %d (m_small·α_max < D̄_min), m_large = %d, T = %d'
      % (amin2, amax2, Dmin2, Dmax2, g0min2, cmin2, cmax2, m_small, m_large, Tpr))

def arm_tau_b(m, mm):
    o1, _ = run(m, new_state(m), Tpr, [(Fr(1), 0, B, 0)])
    o2, _ = run(m, new_state(m), Tpr, [(Fr(1), 0, A, 0)] * mm + [(Fr(1), 0, B, 0)])
    return o1[-1][1], o2[-1][1]

def rand_box(kind, **extra):
    g0 = (rq(g0min2, 1), rq(g0min2, 1)); c = (rq(cmin2, cmax2), rq(cmin2, cmax2))
    al = (rq(amin2, amax2), rq(amin2, amax2)); cp = (rq(Dmin2, Dmax2), rq(Dmin2, Dmax2))
    return M(kind, g0=g0, c=c, alpha=al, lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)), cap=cp, **extra)

rows = {}
for _ in range(400):
    for kind in ('C', 'N1', 'N3', 'HOM', 'TF3', 'N2', 'HS', 'STF'):
        if kind == 'N1':
            m = rand_box('N1', phi=rq(0, 1), mu=rq(0, 1))
        elif kind == 'N3':
            m = rand_box('N3', rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
        elif kind == 'HOM':
            m = rand_box('HOM', kH=rq(0, 1), Hbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), nu=rq(0, 1))
        elif kind == 'HS':
            m = rand_box('HS', kappa=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1))
        elif kind == 'STF':
            m = rand_box('STF', kF=rq(Fr(1, 4), 1), Fbar=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1))
        else:
            m = rand_box(kind)
        r = rows.setdefault(kind, [0, 0, 0])
        t1, t2 = arm_tau_b(m, m_small)
        u1, u2 = arm_tau_b(m, m_large)
        r[0] += 1; r[1] += (t2 != t1); r[2] += (u2 != u1)
for kind in ('C', 'N1', 'N3', 'HOM', 'TF3', 'N2', 'HS', 'STF'):
    r = rows[kind]
    print('      %-4s %d runs: τ_b changes at m_small in %d; τ_b changes at m_large in %d' % (kind, r[0], r[1], r[2]))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R3
print('\nR3  Candidate N3 “Gather and Wait, Join and Emerge”')
v1 = v1p = v2 = va = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    m = rand_C('N3', cap=R.random() < 0.7, rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
    st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
    trials = rand_trials()
    out, _ = run(m, st, R.choice([3, 4, 7, 10]), trials)
    v1 += tb.viol_I1(out, trials) is not None
    v1p += tb.viol_I1p(out, trials) is not None
    v2 += tb.viol_I2(out, trials) is not None
    va += tb.viol_absorb(out, trials) is not None
print('  (a) 3000 random runs (zero initial store): I1′ (h_k = 0 at the start of trial k: first trial, or previous trial non-∅) violations %d, I2 violations %d (Theorem N3-1: both hold on N3); ∅-absorption violations %d (does not hold on N3)' % (v1p, v2, va))
print('      I1 (without “previous trial non-∅”) violations %d: the store shifts the grid phase, so a sole lead can turn into simultaneous opening after the store is cleared (witness below)' % v1)
m = M('N3', g0=(Fr(19, 20), 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2), rho=Fr(1, 2), muh=0)
st = new_state(m); st['h'] = Fr(3, 50)
out, _ = run(m, st, 10, tr(2))
print('      Witness θ = (19/20, 1), α = 0, initial store h = 3/50, P = 1, T = 10: %s' % seqstr(out, True))
# (b) closed form of the first opening: the least j with P_lo(1 + ρ + … + ρ^{j−1}) ≥ θ_min
bad = cnt = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    m = rand_C('N3', cap=True, rho=rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), muh=0)
    d0 = rand_d(m)
    st = new_state(m, d=d0)
    thmin = min(tb.theta(m, st, 0), tb.theta(m, st, 1))
    Plo = rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2))
    if Plo >= thmin:
        continue
    rho = m['rho']
    jstar = None
    acc = Fr(0)
    for j in range(1, 200):
        acc += Plo * rho ** (j - 1)
        if acc >= thmin:
            jstar = j; break
    out, _ = run(m, st, 10, tr(200, P=Plo))
    first = next((k + 1 for k, (Y, _) in enumerate(out) if Y), None)
    cnt += 1
    bad += (first != jstar)
print('  (b) closed form for the first opening trial j* (rest 0, P_lo < θ_min): %d random runs, disagreeing with the run in %d' % (cnt, bad))
# (c) gathering and waiting only shifts the starting point and does not change the first opener (when P/T < θ_b − θ_a)
bad = cnt = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    m = rand_C('N3', cap=True, rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
    st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
    st['h'] = rq(0, 1)
    th = [tb.theta(m, st, x) for x in (0, 1)]
    if th[0] == th[1]:
        continue
    lo = 0 if th[0] < th[1] else 1
    P = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); T = R.choice([10, 20, 50])
    if not (P / T < abs(th[1] - th[0])):
        continue
    if st['h'] >= th[lo]:
        continue
    Y, t = tb.trial(m, st, T, P)
    if Y == frozenset():
        continue
    cnt += 1
    bad += (Y != frozenset((lo,)))
print('  (c) with store h < θ_min and P/T < |θ_a − θ_b|, the first opener is always the lower-threshold path: %d random trials, %d disagree' % (cnt, bad))
# (d) Tier A: q_N3 (n trials, P_lo, rest 0) — C16v0.2[bounded], N1, HOM, STF vs N3[ρ ≥ ρ_min]
g0min, cmax, Dmax3 = Fr(3, 4), Fr(1), Fr(2)
thfloor = g0min / (1 + cmax * Dmax3)
Plo = Fr(1, 5)
rho_min = Fr(7, 8)
assert Plo < thfloor and Plo / (1 - rho_min) > 1
jmax = next(j for j in range(1, 1000) if Plo * (1 - rho_min ** j) / (1 - rho_min) >= 1)
n3 = jmax + 1
print('  (d) bounded class: g0 ≥ %s, c ≤ %s, D̄ ≤ %s ⇒ θ ≥ θ_floor = %s; P_lo = %s, ρ_min = %s, j*_max = %d, protocol length n = %d' % (g0min, cmax, Dmax3, thfloor, Plo, rho_min, jmax, n3))

def rand_bounded(kind, **extra):
    g0s = (rq(g0min, 1), rq(g0min, 1)); cs = (rq(Fr(1, 4), cmax), rq(Fr(1, 4), cmax))
    caps = (rq(Fr(1, 2), Dmax3), rq(Fr(1, 2), Dmax3))
    return M(kind, g0=g0s, c=cs, alpha=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)), cap=caps, **extra)

stats = {}
for _ in range(1500):
    for kind in ('C', 'N1', 'HOM', 'STF', 'N3'):
        if kind == 'C':
            m = rand_bounded('C')
        elif kind == 'N1':
            m = rand_bounded('N1', phi=rq(0, 1), mu=rq(0, 1))
        elif kind == 'HOM':
            m = rand_bounded('HOM', kH=rq(0, 1), Hbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), nu=rq(0, 1))
        elif kind == 'STF':
            m = rand_bounded('STF', kF=rq(0, 1), Fbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1))
        else:
            m = rand_bounded('N3', rho=rq(rho_min, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
        st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m, top=Dmax3))
        out, _ = run(m, st, R.choice([4, 10, 20]), tr(n3, P=Plo))
        ys = [Y for (Y, _) in out]
        allempty = all(not Y for Y in ys)
        succ_then_fail = any(ys[k] and not ys[k + 1] for k in range(len(ys) - 1))
        s = stats.setdefault(kind, [0, 0, 0])
        s[0] += 1; s[1] += allempty; s[2] += succ_then_fail
for kind in ('C', 'N1', 'HOM', 'N3', 'STF'):
    s = stats[kind]
    print('      %-4s %d runs: all ∅ %d; “return to ∅ after an opening” in %d' % (kind, s[0], s[1], s[2]))
# (e) conjugacy with LIF (independent implementation)
same = tot = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    m = rand_C('N3', cap=True, rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
    d0 = rand_d(m); h0 = rq(0, 1)
    lif = M('LIF', g0=m['g0'], c=m['c'], alpha=m['alpha'], lam=m['lam'], cap=m['cap'], rhoV=m['rho'], muV=m['muh'])
    trials = rand_trials(allow_u=False)
    T = R.choice([3, 4, 7, 10])
    st = new_state(m, d=d0); st['h'] = h0
    o1, s1 = run(m, st, T, trials)
    o2, s2 = run(lif, tb.lif_state(V=h0, d=d0), T, trials)
    tot += 1
    same += (o1 == o2 and s1['d'] == s2['d'] and s1['h'] == s2['V'])
print('  (e) N3 vs entry LIF (independent implementation, renamed h↔V, ρ↔ρ_V, μ_h↔μ_V): %d random runs, readings and final states identical in %d' % (tot, same))
# (f) fast-leak blind spot and the interval–leak condition
m = M('N3', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2), rho=Fr(1, 2), muh=0)
out, _ = run(m, new_state(m), 10, tr(30, P=Fr(1, 3)))
print('  (f) fast-leak witness ρ = 1/2, P_lo = 1/3, θ = 1: P_lo/(1 − ρ) = 2/3 < 1, 30 trials: %s' % ('all ∅' if all(not Y for Y, _ in out) else seqstr(out)))
m = M('N3', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2), rho=Fr(9, 10), muh=Fr(1, 2))
for r in (0, 1, 3):
    out, _ = run(m, new_state(m), 10, tr(40, P=Fr(1, 4), r=r))
    first = next((k + 1 for k, (Y, _) in enumerate(out) if Y), None)
    q = Fr(9, 10) * (1 - Fr(1, 2)) ** r
    print('      ρ = 9/10, μ_h = 1/2, P_lo = 1/4, θ = 1, rest between trials r = %d (retention factor per interval q = %s, P_lo/(1 − q) = %s): first opening trial %s'
          % (r, q, Fr(1, 4) / (1 - q), first))
# (g) homeostasis (time-based) vs N3 (drive-based): subthreshold probe with long rests
mh = M('HOM', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2), kH=Fr(1, 4), Hbar=Fr(3, 4), nu=0)
mn = M('N3', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), cap=(2, 2), rho=Fr(9, 10), muh=Fr(1, 2))
for mm in (mh, mn):
    o1, _ = run(mm, new_state(mm), 10, tr(12, P=Fr(1, 2), r=0))
    o2, _ = run(mm, new_state(mm), 10, tr(12, P=Fr(1, 2), r=6))
    print('  (g) %-3s subthreshold drive P = 1/2: rest 0: %s | rest 6 beats after each trial: %s' % (mm.kind, seqstr(o1), seqstr(o2)))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R4
print('\nR4  Candidate N5 “Carrying the Trace into Depth” (two-arm protocol: training → delay R → non-reinforced test)')

def two_arm(m, j_tr, R_short, R_long, n_test, T):
    res = []
    for Rd in (R_short, R_long):
        trials = [(Fr(1), 1, A, 0)] * (j_tr - 1) + [(Fr(1), 1, A, Rd)] + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * n_test
        out, st = run(m, new_state(m), T, trials)
        res.append(out[j_tr:])
    return res

m5 = M('N5', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha_s=Fr(1, 2), sbar=2, sigma=Fr(1, 2), beta=Fr(1, 4), betaE=Fr(1, 4), mus=Fr(1, 4), muE=Fr(1, 2), muL=0)
sh, lo = two_arm(m5, 4, 2, 8, 10, 40)
print('  Witness N5 (α_s = 1/2, s̄ = 2, σ* = 1/2, β = β_E = 1/4, μ_s = 1/4, μ_E = 1/2, μ_L = 0), 4 training trials, T = 40:')
print('    Short arm R = 2: %s' % seqstr(sh, True))
print('    Long arm R = 8: %s' % seqstr(lo, True))
# spontaneous recovery
trials = [(Fr(1), 1, A, 0)] * 3 + [(Fr(1), 1, A, 8)] + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * 6 + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 12)] + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * 2
out, st = run(m5, new_state(m5), 400, trials)
print('    Long arm, rest 12 beats after extinction, then test again (T = 400): %s; final state s = %s, E = %s' % (seqstr(out[4:], True), st['s'][0], st['E'][0]))
# random check: signature of each class on the two-arm protocol
sig = {}
for _ in range(1200):
    T = 60; j_tr = 4; n_test = 12
    kinds = ['N5', 'C', 'TF3', 'RW', 'TWO']
    for kind in kinds:
        g0 = rq(Fr(3, 4), 1); c = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2)
        if kind == 'N5':
            m = M('N5', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), alpha_s=rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), sbar=rq(Fr(3, 2), 3), sigma=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)),
                  beta=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), betaE=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), mus=rq(Fr(1, 6), Fr(1, 3)), muE=rq(Fr(1, 3), Fr(2, 3)), muL=0)
            sR_short = min(j_tr * m['alpha_s'], m['sbar']) * (1 - m['mus']) ** 1
            if sR_short < m['sigma']:
                continue
        elif kind == 'C':
            m = M('C', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), alpha=(rq(Fr(1, 4), 1),) * 2, lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 3)),) * 2, cap=(None, None))
        elif kind == 'TF3':
            m = M('TF3', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), alpha=(rq(Fr(1, 4), 1),) * 2, lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 3)),) * 2, cap=(None, None))
        elif kind == 'RW':
            m = M('RW', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), eta=rq(Fr(1, 6), Fr(5, 6)), Vbar=rq(1, 3), lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 3)),) * 2)
        else:
            m = M('TWO', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), Bf=rq(Fr(1, 6), Fr(1, 2)), Bs=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 6)), Vbar=rq(1, 3),
                  lf=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), ls=rq(0, Fr(1, 12)))
        sh, lo = two_arm(m, j_tr, 1, 12, n_test, T)
        test_tr = [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * n_test
        drop = tb.viol_tau_nondecr(sh, test_tr, 0) is not None
        lose = (len(lo[0][0]) == 1 and tb.viol_I1(lo, test_tr) is not None)
        uniq_long = len(lo[0][0]) == 1
        below = True
        if kind == 'N5':
            below = min(j_tr * m['alpha_s'], m['sbar']) * (1 - m['mus']) ** 12 < m['sigma']
        s = sig.setdefault(kind, [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0])
        s[0] += 1; s[1] += drop; s[2] += uniq_long; s[3] += lose
        if below and uniq_long:
            s[4] += 1; s[5] += lose
for kind in ('N5', 'C', 'TF3', 'RW', 'TWO'):
    s = sig[kind]
    extra = ('; of these, s_R < σ* with a sole lead on the first test in %d runs, sole lead lost in %d' % (s[4], s[5])) if kind == 'N5' else ''
    print('  %-3s %4d runs: τ strictly decreasing in the short-arm test segment %4d; sole lead on the first long-arm test %4d, of which sole lead lost in the test segment %4d%s' % (kind, s[0], s[1], s[2], s[3], extra))
# u-flip two-arm: same initial state, same protocol, only u inverted on every trial
cnt = {}
for _ in range(300):
    for kind in ('N5', 'C', 'N1', 'N2', 'N3', 'STF', 'HOM', 'HS'):
        if kind == 'N5':
            m = M('N5', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha_s=rq(Fr(1, 4), 1), sbar=rq(1, 3), sigma=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)),
                  beta=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), betaE=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), mus=rq(Fr(1, 6), Fr(1, 3)), muE=rq(Fr(1, 3), Fr(2, 3)), muL=0)
        else:
            m = None
            if kind == 'C':
                m = rand_C('C')
            elif kind == 'N1':
                m = rand_C('N1', phi=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), mu=rq(0, 1))
            elif kind == 'N2':
                m = rand_C('N2')
            elif kind == 'N3':
                m = rand_C('N3', rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
            elif kind == 'STF':
                m = rand_C('STF', kF=rq(0, 1), Fbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1))
            elif kind == 'HOM':
                m = rand_C('HOM', kH=rq(0, 1), Hbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), nu=rq(0, 1))
            else:
                m = rand_C('HS', kappa=rq(0, 1))
        trials = [(Fr(1), 1, A, 0)] * 3 + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * 3
        flipped = [(P, 1 - u, pr, r) for (P, u, pr, r) in trials]
        o1, _ = run(m, new_state(m), 40, trials)
        o2, _ = run(m, new_state(m), 40, flipped)
        c = cnt.setdefault(kind, [0, 0])
        c[0] += 1; c[1] += (o1 != o2)
print('  Two arms with u flipped (zero initial state, 3 training trials presenting a only with u = 1, then 3 tests with both paths presented and u = 0; in the other arm every u is inverted): ' +
      '; '.join('%s %d/%d readings differ' % (k, cnt[k][1], cnt[k][0]) for k in ('N5', 'C', 'N1', 'N2', 'N3', 'STF', 'HOM', 'HS')))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R5
print('\nR5  Signature table on W16-E (finite verification on random protocols: violation count / number of runs)')

def rand_member(kind):
    if kind == 'C':
        return rand_C('C')
    if kind == 'N1':
        return rand_C('N1', phi=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), mu=rq(0, 1))
    if kind == 'N2':
        return rand_C('N2')
    if kind == 'N3':
        return rand_C('N3', rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
    if kind == 'STF':
        return rand_C('STF', kF=rq(0, 1), Fbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1))
    if kind == 'HOM':
        return rand_C('HOM', kH=rq(0, 1), Hbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), nu=rq(0, 1))
    if kind == 'HS':
        return rand_C('HS', kappa=rq(0, 1))
    if kind == 'TF3':
        return rand_C('TF3')
    if kind == 'RW':
        g0s, cs = rand_g()
        return M('RW', g0=g0s, c=cs, eta=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), Vbar=rq(0, 3), lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)))
    if kind == 'TWO':
        g0s, cs = rand_g()
        return M('TWO', g0=g0s, c=cs, Bf=rq(0, Fr(1, 2)), Bs=rq(0, Fr(1, 2)), Vbar=rq(0, 3), lf=rq(0, 1), ls=rq(0, 1))
    if kind == 'N5':
        g0s, cs = rand_g()
        return M('N5', g0=g0s, c=cs, alpha_s=rq(0, 1), sbar=rq(0, 3), sigma=rq(0, 1), beta=rq(0, 1), betaE=rq(0, 1),
                 mus=rq(0, 1), muE=rq(0, 1), muL=rq(0, 1))

kinds = ['C', 'N1', 'N2', 'N3', 'STF', 'HOM', 'HS', 'TF3', 'RW', 'TWO', 'N5']
print('  %-4s %10s %10s %10s %10s' % ('kind', 'I1', 'I2', '∅-absorb', 'u-depend'))
for kind in kinds:
    c1 = c2 = ca = cu = 0; N = 800
    for _ in range(N):
        m = rand_member(kind)
        if kind == 'TWO':
            st = new_state(m, df=(rq(-1, 1), rq(-1, 1)), ds=(rq(0, 2), rq(0, 2)))
        elif kind == 'N5':
            st = new_state(m, s=(rq(0, 2), rq(0, 2)), L=(rq(0, 2), rq(0, 2)), E=(rq(0, 2), rq(0, 2)))
        else:
            st = new_state(m, d=rand_d(m))
        trials = rand_trials()
        T = R.choice([3, 4, 7, 10])
        out, _ = run(m, st, T, trials)
        c1 += tb.viol_I1(out, trials) is not None
        c2 += tb.viol_I2(out, trials) is not None
        ca += tb.viol_absorb(out, trials) is not None
        flipped = [(P, 1 - u, pr, r) for (P, u, pr, r) in trials]
        out2, _ = run(m, st, T, flipped)
        cu += (out2 != out)
    print('  %-4s %6d/%d %6d/%d %6d/%d %6d/%d' % (kind, c1, N, c2, N, ca, N, cu, N))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ R6
print('\nR6  Tier B: upper bounds on the two kinds of misjudgment under the reading channel R_ε (exact rational arithmetic, then converted to decimal)')
print('  Statistic: S = number of trials, among the declared tail segment of n′ trials, whose report shows the specified pattern; decision rule S ≥ τ. On one side the true value never shows the pattern; on the other, the tail segment always shows it.')
print('  Upper bounds on misjudgment: α ≤ P(Bin(n′, ε) ≥ τ), β ≤ P(Bin(n′, 1 − ε) < τ); τ is chosen to minimize the larger of the two.')
for eps in (Fr(1, 20), Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 5)):
    for npr in (10, 20, 40):
        best = None
        for tau in range(0, npr + 2):
            a = tb.binom_tail_ge(npr, eps, tau)
            b = tb.binom_tail_lt(npr, 1 - eps, tau)
            mx = max(a, b)
            if best is None or mx < best[0]:
                best = (mx, tau, a, b)
        print('  ε = %-5s n′ = %-3d τ = %-3d α ≤ %.3e  β ≤ %.3e' % (eps, npr, best[1], float(best[2]), float(best[3])))
# N3 vs C16v0.2[bounded]: n = 8m trials; the true value of C is all ∅, that of N3 is non-∅ at least m times (at least one opening in every j*_max = 8 trials)
print('  N3 vs bounded C16 v0.2 (protocol q_N3, j*_max = 8, n = 8m, statistic S = number of trials reported non-∅): α ≤ P(Bin(n, ε) ≥ τ), β ≤ P(Bin(m, 1 − ε) < τ)')
for eps in (Fr(1, 20), Fr(1, 40)):
    for mm in (10, 20, 40):
        nn = 8 * mm
        best = None
        for tau in range(0, mm + 2):
            a = tb.binom_tail_ge(nn, eps, tau)
            b = tb.binom_tail_lt(mm, 1 - eps, tau)
            mx = max(a, b)
            if best is None or mx < best[0]:
                best = (mx, tau, a, b)
        print('  ε = %-5s m = %-3d n = %-4d τ = %-3d α ≤ %.3e  β ≤ %.3e' % (eps, mm, nn, best[1], float(best[2]), float(best[3])))
print('  Usage: N2 vs C16 v0.2 (event U taken as the class restriction “sole lead on the first trial”), pattern = both paths open simultaneously, tail segment = trials K to n; N3 vs C16, N1, HOM (bounded class), '
      'see the table above (openings of N3 are not consecutive, so they are counted as “at least once every j*_max trials”).')
print('\nDone.')
~~~

A.10  run16\_2.py  Run entry: sections Q0–Q7 (Theorem N3-6; the N5 horizon condition and Θ5; the same-cell example; the scope of Theorem N3-2; N11; N12)

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_2.py (sections Q1–Q7).
Run: python3 run16_2.py. The output is deterministic. The program provides only finite verification, explicit witnesses and contrasts; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, math
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import t16b_core as tb
import t16c_ext as tc
from t16b_core import M, new_state, run, tr, seqstr, ystr, BOTH, g
R = random.Random(16202)
A, B = frozenset((0,)), frozenset((1,))
here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def rq(lo, hi, den=12):
    a = math.ceil(Fr(lo) * den); b = math.floor(Fr(hi) * den)
    return Fr(R.randint(a, b), den)

def sha(p):
    with open(p, 'rb') as fh:
        return hashlib.sha256(fh.read()).hexdigest()

print('The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_2.py')
print('=' * 72)
print('\nQ0  Version')
for f in ('t16b_core.py', 't16c_ext.py'):
    print('  %s SHA-256: %s' % (f, sha(os.path.join(here, f))))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Q1
print('\nQ1  Theorem N3-6: the N3 group and the baseline union are disjoint; an overlap example between baselines')
p = dict(g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(2, 2))
mc = M('C', **p); ms = M('STF', **p, kF=Fr(1, 100), Fbar=Fr(1, 10), muF=0)
q = tr(9, P=Fr(1, 5))
oc, _ = run(mc, new_state(mc), 40, q); os_, _ = run(ms, new_state(ms), 40, q)
print('  Overlap example: C and STF (κ_F = 1/100, F̄ = 1/10) give identical readings under q_N3 (n = 9, P_lo = 1/5, T = 40): %s; %s' % (oc == os_, seqstr(oc, True)))
print('  No separability between baselines is claimed; this overlap serves as a contrast.')
g0min, cmax, Dmax3 = Fr(3, 4), Fr(1), Fr(2)
Plo, rho_min = Fr(1, 5), Fr(7, 8)
jmax = next(j for j in range(1, 1000) if Plo * (1 - rho_min ** j) / (1 - rho_min) >= 1)
n3 = jmax + 1

def rb(kind, **extra):
    g0s = (rq(g0min, 1), rq(g0min, 1)); cs = (rq(Fr(1, 4), cmax), rq(Fr(1, 4), cmax))
    caps = (rq(Fr(1, 2), Dmax3), rq(Fr(1, 2), Dmax3))
    return M(kind, g0=g0s, c=cs, alpha=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)), cap=caps, **extra)

def pattern(out):
    ys = [Y for Y, _ in out]
    return any(ys[k] and not ys[k + 1] for k in range(len(ys) - 1))

cnt = {}
base_kinds = ['C', 'N1', 'HOM', 'STF', 'TF3', 'RW', 'TWO', 'N2', 'HS', 'N5']
for _ in range(600):
    for kind in base_kinds + ['N3']:
        if kind == 'N1':
            m = rb('N1', phi=rq(0, 1), mu=rq(0, 1)); st = new_state(m, d=(rq(0, m['cap'][0]), rq(0, m['cap'][1])))
        elif kind == 'HOM':
            m = rb('HOM', kH=rq(0, 1), Hbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), nu=rq(0, 1)); st = new_state(m, d=(rq(0, m['cap'][0]), rq(0, m['cap'][1])))
        elif kind == 'STF':
            m = rb('STF', kF=rq(0, 1), Fbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1)); st = new_state(m, d=(rq(0, m['cap'][0]), rq(0, m['cap'][1])))
        elif kind == 'HS':
            m = rb('HS', kappa=rq(0, 1)); st = new_state(m, d=(rq(0, m['cap'][0]), rq(0, m['cap'][1])))
        elif kind == 'N3':
            m = rb('N3', rho=rq(rho_min, 1), muh=rq(0, 1)); st = new_state(m, d=(rq(0, m['cap'][0]), rq(0, m['cap'][1])))
        elif kind == 'RW':
            m = M('RW', g0=(rq(g0min, 1),) * 2, c=(rq(Fr(1, 4), cmax),) * 2, eta=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), Vbar=rq(0, 2), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2)
            st = new_state(m, d=(rq(0, 2), rq(0, 2)))
        elif kind == 'TWO':
            m = M('TWO', g0=(rq(g0min, 1),) * 2, c=(rq(Fr(1, 4), cmax),) * 2, Bf=rq(0, Fr(1, 2)), Bs=rq(0, Fr(1, 2)), Vbar=rq(0, 2), lf=rq(0, 1), ls=rq(0, 1))
            st = new_state(m, df=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), ds=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)))
        elif kind == 'N5':
            m = M('N5', g0=(rq(g0min, 1),) * 2, c=(rq(Fr(1, 4), cmax),) * 2, alpha_s=rq(0, 1), sbar=rq(0, 2), sigma=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1),
                  beta=rq(0, 1), betaE=rq(0, 1), mus=rq(0, 1), muE=rq(0, 1), muL=0)
            st = new_state(m, s=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), L=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)))
        else:
            m = rb(kind); st = new_state(m, d=(rq(0, m['cap'][0]), rq(0, m['cap'][1])))
        th0 = [tb.theta(m, st, x) for x in (0, 1)]
        if min(th0) <= Plo and kind != 'N3':
            continue   # class bound: initial thresholds ≥ θ_floor > P_lo
        out, _ = run(m, st, R.choice([10, 20, 40]), tr(n3, P=Plo))
        c = cnt.setdefault(kind, [0, 0])
        c[0] += 1; c[1] += pattern(out)
out, _ = run(ms, new_state(ms), 40, tr(n3, P=Plo))
print('  Occurrences of “return to ∅ after an opening” on q_N3 (n = %d) (must always be yes for the N3 group and always no for the baseline union):' % n3)
print('    ' + '; '.join('%s %d/%d' % (k, cnt[k][1], cnt[k][0]) for k in base_kinds + ['N3']))
print('    STF member of the overlap example: %s' % pattern(out))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Q2
print('\nQ2  Long-arm horizon condition of the N5 two-arm protocol: critical example and critical value')
m = M('N5', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha_s=Fr(1, 2), sbar=1, sigma=Fr(3, 8), beta=1, betaE=Fr(1, 1000), mus=Fr(1, 2), muE=Fr(3, 4), muL=0)

def arm(m, Rd, n, T, j=1):
    q = [(Fr(1), 1, A, 0)] * (j - 1) + [(Fr(1), 1, A, Rd)] + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * n
    o, _ = run(m, new_state(m), T, q)
    return o[j:]

s_long = min(Fr(1, 2), 1) * (1 - Fr(1, 2)) ** 1
n_req = 1 + math.ceil(s_long / Fr(1, 1000))
for n in (2, n_req - 1, n_req):
    o = arm(m, 1, n, 100)
    first_loss = next((k + 1 for k, (Y, _) in enumerate(o) if Y != A), None)
    print('  Critical example, long arm (R = 1, s_long = %s, β_E = 1/1000, T = 100), n = %d: sole lead throughout %s, sole lead first lost at test %s'
          % (s_long, n, all(Y == A for Y, _ in o), first_loss))
print('  Long-arm horizon condition n ≥ 1 + ⌈s_long,max/β_E,min⌉ = %d (the bound of Theorem N5-2; the grid makes the actual loss of lead come earlier)' % n_req)
o = arm(m, 0, 2, 100)
print('  Critical example, short arm (R = 0), n = 2: %s' % seqstr(o, True))
# Random check inside the Θ5 box: with all conditions written in, the reading pair of the N5 group is always (yes, yes), and the baseline union does not contain (yes, yes)
box = dict(alpha_s=(Fr(1, 2), 1), sbar=(Fr(3, 2), 2), sigma=(Fr(1, 3), Fr(1, 2)), beta=(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)),
           betaE=(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), mus=(Fr(1, 6), Fr(1, 4)), muE=(Fr(1, 3), Fr(2, 3)))
j_tr, Rs, Rl, T5 = 4, 1, 12, 200
s_short_min = min(j_tr * box['alpha_s'][0], box['sbar'][0]) * (1 - box['mus'][1]) ** Rs
s_long_max = box['sbar'][1] * (1 - box['mus'][0]) ** Rl
s_long_min = min(j_tr * box['alpha_s'][0], box['sbar'][0]) * (1 - box['mus'][1]) ** Rl
n5 = 1 + math.ceil(s_long_max / box['betaE'][0])
print('  Θ5 box: s_short,min = %s ≥ σ*_max = %s: %s; s_long,max = %s < σ*_min = %s: %s; s_long,min = %s; n = 1 + ⌈s_long,max/β_E,min⌉ = %d; T = %d'
~~~
