~~~python
      % (float(s_short_min), box['sigma'][1], s_short_min >= box['sigma'][1], float(s_long_max), box['sigma'][0],
         s_long_max < box['sigma'][0], float(s_long_min), n5, T5))
print('  First-test grid condition g(0) − g(s_long,min) > P/T (taking g0 = 1, c = 1, the lower end of the worst-case threshold family): %s' % (1 - 1 / (1 + s_long_min) > Fr(1, T5)))
sig = {}
for _ in range(300):
    for kind in ('N5', 'C', 'TF3', 'RW', 'TWO'):
        g0 = rq(Fr(3, 4), 1) if kind != 'N5' else Fr(1); c = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2) if kind != 'N5' else Fr(1)
        if kind == 'N5':
            m = M('N5', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), **{k: rq(*v) for k, v in box.items()}, muL=0)
        elif kind == 'C':
            m = M('C', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), alpha=(rq(Fr(1, 4), 1),) * 2, lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 3)),) * 2, cap=(None, None))
        elif kind == 'TF3':
            m = M('TF3', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), alpha=(rq(Fr(1, 4), 1),) * 2, lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 3)),) * 2, cap=(None, None))
        elif kind == 'RW':
            m = M('RW', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), eta=rq(Fr(1, 6), Fr(5, 6)), Vbar=rq(1, 3), lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 3)),) * 2)
        else:
            m = M('TWO', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), Bf=rq(Fr(1, 6), Fr(1, 2)), Bs=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 6)), Vbar=rq(1, 3), lf=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), ls=rq(0, Fr(1, 12)))
        sh = arm(m, Rs, n5, T5, j_tr); lo = arm(m, Rl, n5, T5, j_tr)
        test_tr = [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * n5
        drop = tb.viol_tau_nondecr(sh, test_tr, 0) is not None
        lose = len(lo[0][0]) == 1 and tb.viol_I1(lo, test_tr) is not None
        s = sig.setdefault(kind, {})
        s[(drop, lose)] = s.get((drop, lose), 0) + 1
for kind in ('N5', 'C', 'TF3', 'RW', 'TWO'):
    print('    %-3s distribution of reading pairs (short-arm drop, long-arm loss of lead): %s' % (kind, ' '.join('%s:%d' % (('yes' if a else 'no') + ('yes' if b else 'no'), v) for (a, b), v in sorted(sig[kind].items()))))
print('  Reading: the N5 group is always (yes, yes); (yes, yes) does not occur in the baseline union; overlaps between baselines (e.g. both C and RW can give (yes/no, no)) carry no discrimination claim.')
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Q3
print('\nQ3  Positive depth recovery and distinguishable sole-lead recovery are adjudicated separately: same-cell example')
m = M('N5', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha_s=Fr(1, 2), sbar=1, sigma=Fr(3, 8), beta=1, betaE=Fr(1, 1000), mus=Fr(1, 2), muE=Fr(3, 4), muL=0)
st0 = new_state(m, s=(Fr(1, 400), 0), E=(Fr(1, 200), 0))
pos = uniq = 0; maxdepth = Fr(0)
for Rr in range(1, 80):
    z = tb.copy_state(st0); tb.rest(m, z, Rr)
    dep = max(Fr(0), z['s'][0] - z['E'][0]); maxdepth = max(maxdepth, dep)
    Y, t = tb.trial(m, z, 40, 1)
    pos += dep > 0; uniq += Y == A
print('  Same-cell example s = 1/400, E = 1/200, μ_s = 1/2, μ_E = 3/4, T = 40: over R = 1..79, depth positive %d times, sole lead %d times; maximal recovery depth %s' % (pos, uniq, maxdepth))
print('  Analytic bound: for every R, δ_R ≤ s·(1 − μ_s)^R ≤ 1/800 < δ_need = 1/39 (with P = 1, T = 40, g = 1/(1 + d), the negation of the sufficient lower bound on the depth a sole lead needs: g(0) − g(δ) ≤ 1/40 ⇔ δ ≤ 1/39), so a distinguishable recovery never occurs.')
m2 = M('N5', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha_s=Fr(1, 2), sbar=2, sigma=Fr(1, 2), beta=Fr(1, 4), betaE=Fr(1, 4), mus=Fr(1, 4), muE=Fr(1, 2), muL=0)
trials = [(Fr(1), 1, A, 0)] * 3 + [(Fr(1), 1, A, 8)] + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0)] * 6
out, st = run(m2, new_state(m2), 400, trials)
rows = []
for Rr in (4, 8, 12, 16, 24):
    z = tb.copy_state(st); tb.rest(m2, z, Rr)
    dep = max(Fr(0), z['s'][0] + z['L'][0] - z['E'][0])
    margin = (1 - 1 / (1 + dep)) - Fr(1, 400)
    Y, t = tb.trial(m2, z, 400, 1)
    rows.append('R=%d depth %.5f margin g(0)−g(δ)−P/T=%.5f reading %s' % (Rr, float(dep), float(margin), ystr(Y)))
print('  Positive example (the witness member of Section R4 of run16_1; s and E after extinction are the actual final states, T = 400): ' + '; '.join(rows))
print('  Reading: a distinguishable sole-lead recovery is signed only at concrete R with margin > 0 (a sufficient condition); with margin ≤ 0 the reading can be a joint opening, even when the depth is positive.')
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Q4
print('\nQ4  Theorem N3-2 applies only when r = 0 and no earlier trial has opened; the same geometric sum may not be continued across a reset event')
m = M('N3', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 10), Fr(1, 10)), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(2, 2), rho=Fr(9, 10), muh=0)
out, _ = run(m, new_state(m), 10, tr(12, P=Fr(1, 4)))
opens = [k + 1 for k, (Y, _) in enumerate(out) if Y]
acc = Fr(0); naive = []
for j in range(1, 13):
    acc += Fr(1, 4) * Fr(9, 10) ** (j - 1)
    if acc >= 1:
        naive.append(j)
print('  Witness ρ = 9/10, α = 1/10, P_lo = 1/4: actual opening trials %s; trials judged “≥ θ_min = 1” by the single geometric sum from trial 1: %s (the first, j* = %d, agrees; the later ones do not)'
      % (opens, naive[:6], opens[0]))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Q6  N11
print('\nQ6  N11 “Selective Bias Maturing”')
m = M('N11', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4)), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(Fr(1), Fr(3)), ku=Fr(1, 4), kd=Fr(1, 8), ebar=1)
st = tc.n11_new(d=(1, 0))
out, s = tc.n11_run(m, st, 40, [(Fr(1), 1, BOTH, 0)] * 40)
print('  Witness (a: depth 1, capacity 1; b: depth 0, capacity 3; α = 1/4; κ_u = 1/4, κ_d = 1/8, ē = 1; target z = b, T = 40):')
print('    ' + seqstr(out))
print('    Final state d = (%s, %s), e_b = %s' % (s['d'][0], s['d'][1], s['e'][1]))
mdv = M('N11', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 16), Fr(1, 16)), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(Fr(1), Fr(3)), ku=Fr(1, 4), kd=Fr(1, 2), ebar=1)
print('  Target-withdrawal (devaluation-type) witness (as above, but α = 1/16, κ_d = 1/2):')
for k_tr in (6, 60):
    tr1 = [(Fr(1), 1, BOTH, 0)] * k_tr + [(Fr(1), None, BOTH, 0)] * 12
    o, s2 = tc.n11_run(mdv, tc.n11_new(d=(1, 0)), 40, tr1)
    print('    After %d target trainings (last 6: %s), target withdrawn for 12 trials: %s' % (k_tr, seqstr(o[k_tr - 6:k_tr]), seqstr(o[k_tr:])))
# Random check of the habit-formation theorem
bad = cnt = 0; worst = 0
for _ in range(800):
    g0 = rq(Fr(1, 2), 1); c = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); Da = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); dl = rq(Fr(1, 2), 1); Db = Da + dl + Fr(1, 2)
    ab = rq(Fr(1, 6), Fr(1, 2)); aa = rq(0, Fr(1, 2)); ku = rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2)); kd = rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2))
    T = 400
    if not (g(g0, c, Da) - g(g0, c, Da + dl) > Fr(1, T)):
        continue
    m = M('N11', g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), alpha=(aa, ab), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(Da, Db), ku=ku, kd=kd, ebar=g0)
    d0 = (rq(0, Da), Fr(0))
    K = (math.ceil(g0 / ku) + 1) * math.ceil((Da + dl) / ab) + 1
    nn = K + math.ceil(g0 / kd) + 3
    o, s = tc.n11_run(m, tc.n11_new(d=d0), T, [(Fr(1), 1, BOTH, 0)] * nn)
    cnt += 1
    ok = all(Y == B for Y, _ in o[K:]) and s['e'][1] == 0
    bad += not ok
print('  Random check of Theorem N11-1 (capacity D̄_b ≥ D̄_a + δ, grid g(D̄_a) − g(D̄_a + δ) > P/T): of %d runs, %d are not constantly {b} after trial K or end with nonzero effort' % (cnt, bad))
# W-instance layer: N11 vs an independent implementation of “X-3 integral feedback superposed via c1 on the C16 threshold”
same = tot = 0
for _ in range(800):
    g0s = (rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), rq(Fr(1, 2), 1)); cs = (rq(Fr(1, 2), 2), rq(Fr(1, 2), 2))
    par = dict(g0=g0s, c=cs, alpha=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1),) * 2, cap=(rq(1, 3), rq(1, 3)), ku=rq(0, 1), kd=rq(0, 1), ebar=rq(0, 1))
    m1 = M('N11', **par); m2 = M('IFBC', **par)
    tr2 = [(R.choice([Fr(1, 2), Fr(1)]), R.choice([0, 1, None]), R.choice([BOTH, A, B]), R.randint(0, 2)) for _ in range(R.randint(1, 15))]
    d0 = (rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1))
    o1, s1 = tc.n11_run(m1, tc.n11_new(d=d0), 10, tr2); o2, s2 = tc.n11_run(m2, tc.n11_new(d=d0), 10, tr2)
    tot += 1; same += (o1 == o2 and s1 == s2)
print('  N11 vs IFBC (X-3-type integral feedback superposed via c1 on the C16 v0.2 threshold, independent implementation): of %d random runs, readings and final states identical in %d' % (tot, same))
# Target-independent class: C16
diff = 0
for _ in range(300):
    mcz = M('C', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 4),) * 2, lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(1, 3))
    o1, _ = run(mcz, new_state(mcz, d=(1, 0)), 40, tr(20))
    diff += any(Y == B for Y, _ in o1)
print('  C16 v0.2 (does not read z), same initial state, 20 trials: runs in which a sole lead of b occurs: %d/300 (the target input has no effect on it)' % diff)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Q7  N12
print('\nQ7  N12 “Clog, Swell, Scour a Channel”')
par = dict(g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4)), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), rhoH=Fr(1, 2), muH=Fr(1, 4), ks=Fr(1), Bbar=(Fr(5, 2), Fr(5, 2)))
m12 = M('N12', **par); c12 = M('C12', **par)
mh = M('HOMB', **par, kH=Fr(1, 4), Hbar=Fr(1, 2), nu=Fr(1, 2))
def blk(m, n_block, rest_in_block, release, test, T=200):
    q = [(Fr(1), BOTH, rest_in_block, (0,) if k == 0 else (), ()) for k in range(n_block)]
    if release:
        q += [(Fr(1), BOTH, 0, (), (0,))]
    q += [(Fr(1), BOTH, 0, (), ())] * test
    return tc.n12_run(m, tc.n12_new(d=(0, 0)), T, q)
for mm in (m12, c12, mh):
    o, s = blk(mm, 12, 0, False, 2)
    print('  %-4s zero initial state, a blocked from trial 1, driven for 14 trials (rest 0, no release): %s; events %s' % (mm.kind, seqstr(o), [(k, e, x) for (k, e, x, _) in s['events']]))
print('  Closed form of the breakthrough time: with rest 0 and P = 1, H = j·ρ_H·P after the j-th blocked trial, and the breakthrough occurs at the start of trial 1 + ⌈B̄/(ρ_H·P)⌉ = %d' % (1 + math.ceil(Fr(5, 2) / Fr(1, 2))))
for m_ in (1, 2, 3, 4):
    res = []
    for mm in (m12, c12, mh):
        o, s = blk(mm, m_, 0, True, 1)
        res.append('%s %s' % (mm.kind, ystr(o[m_][0])))
    print('  a blocked for m = %d drive trials, then released by the protocol (rest 0); reading at the release trial: %s' % (m_, '; '.join(res)))
for mm in (m12, mh):
    o, s = blk(mm, 3, 4, True, 1)
    print('  %-4s a blocked, rest of 4 beats after each trial (3 trials), then released: reading at the release trial %s' % (mm.kind, ystr(o[3][0])))
# Tier A random check: zero initial state, a blocked for m drive trials and then released; read the release trial
bad = {'N12': 0, 'C12': 0, 'HOMB': 0}; tot = {'N12': 0, 'C12': 0, 'HOMB': 0}
for _ in range(600):
    g0 = rq(Fr(1, 2), 1); c = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); ab = rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2)); rho = rq(Fr(1, 4), 1); ks = rq(1, 2)
    gam = ks * rho - ab
    if gam < Fr(1, 6):
        continue
    mstar = 3; T = 400
    d_a, d_b = ks * rho * mstar, ab * mstar
    if not (g(g0, c, d_b) - g(g0, c, d_a) > Fr(1, T)):
        continue
    p2 = dict(g0=(g0, g0), c=(c, c), alpha=(rq(0, 1), ab), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), rhoH=rho, muH=rq(0, 1), ks=ks, Bbar=(Fr(100), Fr(100)))
    for kind in ('N12', 'C12', 'HOMB'):
        mm = M(kind, **p2, kH=rq(0, 1), Hbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), nu=rq(0, 1)) if kind == 'HOMB' else M(kind, **p2)
        o, s = blk(mm, mstar, 0, True, 0, T)
        Y = o[mstar][0]
        tot[kind] += 1
        if kind == 'N12':
            bad[kind] += Y != A
        else:
            bad[kind] += Y == A
print('  Tier A random check of Theorem N12-2 (κ_s·ρ_H − α_b ≥ 1/6, m = 3, sufficient grid margin): N12 release trials not {a}: %d/%d; C12 release trials equal to {a}: %d/%d; HOMB equal to {a}: %d/%d'
      % (bad['N12'], tot['N12'], bad['C12'], tot['C12'], bad['HOMB'], tot['HOMB']))
print('\nDone.')
~~~

A.11  run16\_3.py  Run entry: sections V0–V4 (Proposition N5-6; Theorem N12-1; Lemmas N12-L1 and N12-L2, and Theorem N12-4; Proposition N11-2)

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_3.py (sections V1–V4).
Run: python3 run16_3.py. The output is deterministic. The program provides only finite verification, explicit witnesses and contrasts; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, math
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import t16b_core as tb
import t16c_ext as tc
import t16d_ext as td
from t16b_core import M, new_state, seqstr, ystr, BOTH, g
R = random.Random(16303)
A, B = frozenset((0,)), frozenset((1,))
NOB = frozenset()
here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def rq(lo, hi, den=12):
    a = math.ceil(Fr(lo) * den); b = math.floor(Fr(hi) * den)
    return Fr(R.randint(a, b), den)

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()

print('The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_3.py')
print('=' * 72)
print('\nV0  Version')
for f in ('t16b_core.py', 't16c_ext.py', 't16d_ext.py'):
    print('  %s SHA-256: %s' % (f, sha(os.path.join(here, f))))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ V1
print('\nV1  Proposition N5-6: eventual sign with coefficients merged by decay factor; comparison of exact first-opening steps; same-grid-cell criterion')
m = M('N5', g0=(Fr(19, 20),) * 2, c=(1, 1), alpha_s=1, sbar=1, sigma=Fr(1, 2), beta=1, betaE=1, mus=Fr(1, 2), muE=Fr(3, 4), muL=0)
st = new_state(m, s=(Fr(2, 5), 0), E=(Fr(2, 5), 0)); tb.rest(m, st, 1)
dl = st['s'][0] - st['E'][0]
ta, tbb, uq = td.unique_exact(Fr(19, 20), 1, dl, 1, 10)
Y, _ = tb.trial(m, st, 10, 1)
print('  Example V1a: δ_1 = %s, threshold gap = %s < P/T = 1/10, exact first-opening steps τ_a = %d, τ_b = %d, sole lead %s; program reading %s'
      % (dl, Fr(19, 20) - Fr(19, 20) / (1 + dl), ta, tbb, uq, ystr(Y)))
print('  Example V1b: s₀ = 0, L₀ = 1, E₀ = 2, μ_L = μ_E = 1/2: eventual sign %d (same-rate merged coefficient 1 − 2 = −1); number of R in 0..100 with δ_R > 0: %d'
      % (td.eventual_sign(0, 1, 2, Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)), sum(td.delta_R(0, 1, 2, Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2), r) > 0 for r in range(101))))
bad = cnt = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    s0, L0, E0 = rq(0, 2), R.choice([Fr(0), rq(0, 2)]), rq(0, 2)
    mus, muL, muE = R.choice([rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(11, 12)), Fr(1, 2)]), R.choice([Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), rq(0, Fr(11, 12))]), R.choice([Fr(1, 2), rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)])
    sg = td.eventual_sign(s0, L0, E0, mus, muL, muE)
    tail = [td.delta_R(s0, L0, E0, mus, muL, muE, r) for r in range(400, 410)]
    cnt += 1
    if sg > 0 and not all(t > 0 for t in tail):
        bad += 1
    if sg <= 0 and any(t > 0 for t in tail):
        bad += 1
print('  Random check of the eventual-sign criterion of Proposition N5-6 (1) (against the actual signs at R = 400..409): %d runs, %d mismatches' % (cnt, bad))
# Two examples for the same-grid-cell criterion
for (lab, g0, s0, E0, mus, muE, T) in (('Example V1c', Fr(1), Fr(1, 400), Fr(1, 200), Fr(1, 2), Fr(3, 4), 40),
                                       ('Example V1d', Fr(19, 20), Fr(2, 5), Fr(2, 5), Fr(1, 2), Fr(3, 4), 10)):
    kb = math.ceil(g0 * T)
    dmax = max(td.delta_R(s0, 0, E0, mus, 0, muE, r) for r in range(0, 200))
    tailbound = s0 * (1 - mus) ** 200
    cell = g(g0, 1, max(dmax, tailbound)) > Fr(kb - 1, T)
    rec = [r for r in range(0, 200) if td.unique_exact(g0, 1, td.delta_R(s0, 0, E0, mus, 0, muE, r), 1, T)[2]]
    print('  %s: grid cell of b (%s, %s]; sup δ_R ≤ %s (computed for R < 200, bounded for R ≥ 200 by s₀(1 − μ_s)^200 = %.3e); whole trajectory in the same cell %s; R with an actual sole lead: %s'
          % (lab, Fr(kb - 1, T), Fr(kb, T), dmax, float(tailbound), cell, rec[:6]))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ V2
print('\nV2  Theorem N12-1 with an initial head H₀')
m12 = M('N12', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), Bbar=(Fr(5, 2),) * 2, rhoH=Fr(1, 2), ks=1, muH=0)
s = tc.n12_new(); s['H'][0] = Fr(5, 2)
o, z = tc.n12_run(m12, s, 10, [(1, BOTH, 0, (0,), ())])
print('  Example V2a: H₀ = B̄ = 5/2, breakthrough at trial %d; closed form 1 + max(0, ⌈(B̄ − H₀)/(ρ_H·P)⌉) = %d' % (z['events'][0][0], td.breach_trial(Fr(5, 2), Fr(5, 2), Fr(1, 2), 1)))
bad = cnt = 0
for _ in range(1500):
    Bb = rq(Fr(1, 4), 4); H0 = rq(0, 5); rho = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1); P = R.choice([Fr(1), Fr(3, 2), Fr(2)])
    mm = M('N12', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 4),) * 2, lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), Bbar=(Bb, Bb), rhoH=rho, ks=1, muH=Fr(1, 3))
    s = tc.n12_new(); s['H'][0] = H0
    n = td.breach_trial(Bb, H0, rho, P) + 2
    o, z = tc.n12_run(mm, s, 10, [(P, BOTH, 0, (0,) if k == 0 else (), ()) for k in range(n)])
    ev = [e for e in z['events'] if e[1] == 'breach']
    cnt += 1
    bad += not (ev and ev[0][0] == td.breach_trial(Bb, H0, rho, P))
print('  %d random runs (one-time setting, rest 0, a always presented, ρ_H·P > 0): breakthrough trial disagrees with the closed form in %d' % (cnt, bad))
mm = M('N12', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 4),) * 2, lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), Bbar=(Fr(1),) * 2, rhoH=Fr(1, 2), ks=1, muH=0)
o, z = tc.n12_run(mm, tc.n12_new(), 10, [(1, B, 0, (0,) if k == 0 else (), ()) for k in range(12)])
print('  Zero-accumulation branch (a blocked and not presented): breakthrough events over 12 trials %s, readings %s' % (z['events'], seqstr(o)))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ V3
print('\nV3  N12 relative to the comparison class C_cmp v0.1: two invariants and their violation by N12')
print('  I_B (a blockade does not release itself): no component of the component library L writes Brk; hence on every member of C_cmp, a path blocked by the protocol and not yet released never opens.')
P_hi = Fr(5)

def blk_trials(Pblk, mblk, ntest, pres_a=True):
    q = [(Pblk, 0, BOTH if pres_a else B, 0, A)] * mblk
    q += [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0, NOB)] * ntest
    return q

def mk(kind):
    g0s = (rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), rq(Fr(1, 2), 1)); cs = (rq(Fr(1, 2), 2), rq(Fr(1, 2), 2))
    base = dict(g0=g0s, c=cs, alpha=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1),) * 2, cap=(rq(1, 3), rq(1, 3)))
    if kind == 'C': return M('C', **base), None
    if kind == 'N1': return M('N1', **base, phi=rq(0, 1), mu=rq(0, 1)), None
    if kind == 'STF': return M('STF', **base, kF=rq(0, 1), Fbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1)), None
    if kind == 'HOM': return M('HOM', **base, kH=rq(0, 1), Hbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), nu=rq(0, 1)), None
    if kind == 'HS': return M('HS', **base, kappa=rq(0, 1)), None
    if kind == 'HSP': return M('HSP', **base, kappa=rq(0, 1)), None
    if kind == 'TF3': return M('TF3', **base), None
    if kind == 'RW': return M('RW', g0=g0s, c=cs, eta=rq(0, 1), Vbar=rq(0, 2), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2), None
    if kind == 'TWO': return M('TWO', g0=g0s, c=cs, Bf=rq(0, Fr(1, 2)), Bs=rq(0, Fr(1, 2)), Vbar=rq(0, 2), lf=rq(0, 1), ls=rq(0, 1)), None
    if kind == 'FS': return M('FS', g0=g0s, c=cs, V=base['alpha'], Cap=base['cap'], leak=base['lam']), 'FS'
    if kind == 'LIF': return M('LIF', **base, rhoV=rq(0, 1), muV=rq(0, 1)), 'LIF'

def init(m, tag):
    if tag == 'FS': return tb.fs_state(S=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)))
    if tag == 'LIF': return tb.lif_state(V=0, d=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)))
    if m.kind == 'TWO': return new_state(m, df=(rq(0, 1),) * 2, ds=(rq(0, 1),) * 2)
    return new_state(m, d=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)))

kinds = ['C', 'N1', 'STF', 'HOM', 'HS', 'HSP', 'TF3', 'RW', 'TWO', 'FS', 'LIF']
res = {}
for _ in range(300):
    for kind in kinds:
        m, tag = mk(kind); st0 = init(m, tag)
        mblk = R.randint(1, 6); T = R.choice([10, 40, 200])
        P1 = Fr(1); P2 = rq(1, 3)
        o1, _ = td.run_blk(m, st0, T, blk_trials(P1, mblk, 3))
        o2, _ = td.run_blk(m, st0, T, blk_trials(P2, mblk, 3))
        opened_a = any(0 in Y for Y, _ in o1[:mblk] + o2[:mblk])
        same_blk = [Y for Y, _ in o1[:mblk]] == [Y for Y, _ in o2[:mblk]] and all(Y for Y, _ in o1[:mblk])
        r = res.setdefault(kind, [0, 0, 0])
        r[2] += opened_a
        if same_blk:
            r[0] += 1
            r[1] += [Y for Y, _ in o1[mblk:]] != [Y for Y, _ in o2[mblk:]]
print('  Random check of I_PD (invariance to drive strength during the blockade): two arms with P = 1 and P ∈ [1, 3] during the blockade; where the blockade-period readings are identical and all non-∅, compare the readings of the 3 trials after release:')
print('    ' + '; '.join('%s: premise holds %d, different after release %d, a opened during the blockade %d' % (k, res[k][0], res[k][1], res[k][2]) for k in kinds))
# Presentation dependence can be reproduced by a valid combination (HSP), so “drive or no drive” does not separate; but strength is invariant
m = M('HSP', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 4),) * 2, lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), kappa=Fr(1, 2))
o1, _ = td.run_blk(m, new_state(m), 200, blk_trials(Fr(1), 4, 1, True))
o2, _ = td.run_blk(m, new_state(m), 200, blk_trials(Fr(1), 4, 1, False))
o3, _ = td.run_blk(m, new_state(m), 200, blk_trials(P_hi, 4, 1, True))
print('  HSP (c2: crosstalk increment gated by 1[a ∈ Pres], valid under the strict reading), zero initial state, a blocked for 4 trials, then released: a presented %s; a not presented %s; a presented and P = 5 %s'
      % (ystr(o1[4][0]), ystr(o2[4][0]), ystr(o3[4][0])))
for kind, extra in (('N1', dict(phi=Fr(1, 2), mu=0)), ('HS', dict(kappa=Fr(1, 2)))):
    m = M(kind, g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 4),) * 2, lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), **extra)
    o, _ = td.run_blk(m, new_state(m), 200, blk_trials(Fr(1), 4, 1, True))
    print('  %s, zero initial state, a blocked for 4 trials, then released: %s (“bias toward the blocked path after release” is not by itself a signature exclusive to N12)' % (kind, ystr(o[4][0])))
# Subclass on which N12 violates I_PD
bad = cnt = 0
for _ in range(500):
    ab = rq(Fr(3, 10), Fr(1, 2), 60); rho = rq(Fr(1, 8), Fr(1, 4), 48)
    mm = M('N12', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(rq(0, Fr(1, 2)), ab), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), Bbar=(Fr(100),) * 2, rhoH=rho, ks=1, muH=rq(0, 1))
    def nq(Pb):
        return [(Pb, BOTH, 0, (0,) if k == 0 else (), ()) for k in range(4)] + [(Fr(1), BOTH, 0, (), (0,))]
    o1, _ = tc.n12_run(mm, tc.n12_new(), 400, nq(Fr(1)))
    o2, _ = tc.n12_run(mm, tc.n12_new(), 400, nq(P_hi))
    cnt += 1
    ok = [Y for Y, _ in o1[:4]] == [Y for Y, _ in o2[:4]] == [B] * 4 and o1[4][0] == B and o2[4][0] == A
    bad += not ok
print('  N12 subclass (α_b ∈ [3/10, 1/2], ρ_H ∈ [1/8, 1/4], κ_s = 1, m = 4, T = 400, B̄ = 100), two arms (P = 1 vs P = 5 during the blockade): '
      'blockade-period readings both {b}^4 while the release trials give {b} and {a} respectively: %d/%d (mismatches %d)' % (cnt - bad, cnt, bad))
# Breakthrough: violation of I_B
mm = M('N12', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 4),) * 2, lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), Bbar=(Fr(1),) * 2, rhoH=Fr(1, 2), ks=1, muH=0)
o, z = tc.n12_run(mm, tc.n12_new(), 10, [(1, BOTH, 0, (0,) if k == 0 else (), ()) for k in range(3)] + [(1, A, 0, (), ())])
print('  N12 breakthrough witness (B̄ = 1, ρ_H = 1/2): a blocked, 3 trials, then only a presented, protocol not released: %s; events %s' % (seqstr(o), [(k, e) for (k, e, x, _) in z['events']]))
for kind in kinds:
    m, tag = mk(kind); st0 = init(m, tag)
    o, _ = td.run_blk(m, st0, 10, [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0, A)] * 3 + [(Fr(1), 0, A, 0, A)])
    assert not o[-1][0]
print('  The 11 kinds in L under the same protocol (blockade not released), last trial presenting only a: all ∅ (program cross-check of I_B)')
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ V4
print('\nV4  Proposition N11-2: beat-by-beat sufficient criterion and the two conditional predictions')
mdv = M('N11', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 16), Fr(1, 16)), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(Fr(1), Fr(3)), ku=Fr(1, 4), kd=Fr(1, 2), ebar=1)
for k_tr in (6, 60):
    o, s = tc.n11_run(mdv, tc.n11_new(d=(1, 0)), 40, [(Fr(1), 1, BOTH, 0)] * k_tr)
    db, da, eb = s['d'][1], s['d'][0], s['e'][1]
    nl = math.ceil(eb / Fr(1, 2)) if eb > 0 else 0
    short_cond = db + nl * Fr(1, 16) < da and g(1, 1, db + nl * Fr(1, 16)) - g(1, 1, da) > Fr(1, 40)
    long_cond = g(1, 1, da) - g(1, 1, db) > Fr(1, 40) and db > da
    o2, _ = tc.n11_run(mdv, s, 40, [(Fr(1), None, BOTH, 0)] * 12)
    print('  Target withdrawn after %d trainings with d_a = %s, d_b = %s, e_b = %s: short-training condition (b, deepening by at most ⌈e_b/κ_d⌉·α_b more before the effort is exhausted, still stays below a, and the threshold gap exceeds one grid step) %s; long-training condition (b already deeper than a, and the threshold gap exceeds one grid step) %s; 12 trials after withdrawal %s'
          % (k_tr, da, db, eb, short_cond, long_cond, seqstr(o2)))
print('\nDone.')
~~~

A.12  run16\_4.py  Run entry: sections E0–E5 (Proposition N5-6 (4) and (5); Proposition 4-4; Theorem N12-5; Proposition N12-6; N13)

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_4.py (sections E1–E5).
Run: python3 run16_4.py. The output is deterministic. The program provides only finite verification, explicit witnesses and contrasts; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, math
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import t16b_core as tb
import t16c_ext as tc
import t16d_ext as td
import t16e_ext as te
from t16b_core import M, BOTH, ystr, g
R = random.Random(16404)
A, B = frozenset((0,)), frozenset((1,))
here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def rq(lo, hi, den=12):
    a = math.ceil(Fr(lo) * den); b = math.floor(Fr(hi) * den)
    return Fr(R.randint(a, b), den)

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()

def seq(out):
    return ' '.join(ystr(Y) for Y, _ in out)

print('The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_4.py')
print('=' * 72)
print('\nE0  Version')
for f in ('t16b_core.py', 't16c_ext.py', 't16d_ext.py', 't16e_ext.py'):
    print('  %s SHA-256: %s' % (f, sha(os.path.join(here, f))))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ E1
print('\nE1  Proposition N5-6 (4): persistent coefficient; continued search when the merged coefficient at rate 1 is 0; all-time conclusion from a failed starting point')
args = (1, 1, 3, 0, 0, Fr(1, 2))
Acoef = 1 * 1 + 1 * 1 - 3 * 0
print('  Boundary case s₀ = L₀ = 1, E₀ = 3, μ_s = μ_L = 0, μ_E = 1/2: persistent coefficient A = %s; δ_0..δ_3 = %s; difference between δ_200 and max(0, A) = %s'
      % (Acoef, ' '.join(str(td.delta_R(*args, r)) for r in range(4)), float(Fr(2) - td.delta_R(*args, 200))))
args2 = (1, Fr(1, 2), 1, 0, Fr(1, 2), 0)
print('  Case with merged coefficient 0 at rate 1, s₀ = E₀ = 1 (μ_s = μ_E = 0), L₀ = 1/2 (μ_L = 1/2): A = 0, search continues to the next nonzero merged rate 1/2, coefficient +1/2, eventual sign %d; δ_1, δ_10 = %s, %s'
      % (td.eventual_sign(*args2), td.delta_R(*args2, 1), td.delta_R(*args2, 10)))
bad = cnt = 0; badfull = cntfull = 0
for _ in range(4000):
    s0, L0, E0 = rq(0, 2), rq(0, 2), rq(0, 3)
    mus, muL, muE = R.choice([Fr(0), rq(0, 1)]), R.choice([Fr(0), rq(0, 1)]), R.choice([Fr(0), rq(0, 1)])
    Ac = s0 * (mus == 0) + L0 * (muL == 0) - E0 * (muE == 0)
    lim = max(Fr(0), Ac)
    far = td.delta_R(s0, L0, E0, mus, muL, muE, 300)
    cnt += 1
    if abs(far - lim) > Fr(1, 10 ** 6):
        bad += 1
    if s0 + L0 - E0 <= 0 and td.eventual_sign(s0, L0, E0, mus, muL, muE) <= 0:
        cntfull += 1
        badfull += any(td.delta_R(s0, L0, E0, mus, muL, muE, r) > 0 for r in range(0, 120))
print('  Random check of the limit max(0, A) (including the μ = 0 boundary): %d runs, %d with |δ_300 − max(0, A)| > 10⁻⁶' % (cnt, bad))
print('  Proposition N5-6 (5) (already failed at the start, u_0 ≤ 0, and eventual sign ≤ 0 ⇒ no recovery over all time): %d runs, %d with a positive depth for some R = 0..119' % (cntfull, badfull))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ E2
print('\nE2  Effect of the components new in C_cmp v0.2 on the two invariants of v0.1')
base = dict(g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4)), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None))
comps = {
    'VST': M('VST', **base, Ap=Fr(1, 8), thp=Fr(1, 2), thm=Fr(1, 4), muu=Fr(1, 2)),
    'BCM': M('BCM', **base, eta=Fr(1, 4), muM=Fr(1, 2)),
    'PF': M('PF', **base, Bbar=(Fr(2),) * 2, rhoH=Fr(1, 2), muH=0),
    'ERO': M('ERO', **base, Kb=3),
    'DIS': M('DIS', **base, dis=1),
    'THR': M('THR', **base, Prel=Fr(3, 2)),
    'DAM': M('DAM', **base, Bbar=(Fr(100),) * 2, rhoH=Fr(1, 4), muH=0, ks=1),
}

def q_pd(Pb, m=4):
    return [(Pb, 0, BOTH, 0, (0,) if k == 0 else (), ()) for k in range(m)] + [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0, (), (0,))]

for name in ('VST', 'BCM', 'DAM'):
    m = comps[name]
    o1, z1 = te.run(m, te.new(), 400, q_pd(Fr(1))); o2, z2 = te.run(m, te.new(), 400, q_pd(Fr(5)))
    qa1 = q_pd(Fr(1))[:-1] + [(Fr(1), 0, A, 0, (), (0,))]; qa2 = q_pd(Fr(5))[:-1] + [(Fr(1), 0, A, 0, (), (0,))]
    r1, _ = te.run(m, te.new(), 400, qa1); r2, _ = te.run(m, te.new(), 400, qa2)
    print('  I_PD two arms (blockade period P = 1 vs P = 5, m = 4) %s: blockade period %s | %s; release trial (both paths presented) %s | %s; release trial (only a presented) τ = %s | %s'
          % (name, seq(o1[:4]), seq(o2[:4]), ystr(o1[4][0]), ystr(o2[4][0]), r1[4][1], r2[4][1]))
for name, u in (('PF', 0), ('ERO', 0), ('DIS', 1), ('THR', 0), ('DAM', 0)):
    m = comps[name] if name != 'DAM' else M('DAM', **base, Bbar=(Fr(1),) * 2, rhoH=Fr(1, 2), muH=0, ks=1)
    Pl = Fr(2) if name == 'THR' else Fr(1)
    q = [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0, (0,) if k == 0 else (), ()) for k in range(4)] + [(Pl, u, A, 0, (), ())]
    o, st = te.run(m, te.new(), 40, q)
    print('  I_B (blockade not released, only a presented on the last trial%s) %s: %s; events %s' % (', u = 1' if u else (', P = 2' if name == 'THR' else ''), name, seq(o), st['events']))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ E3
print('\nE3  N12 under C_cmp v0.2: trial-by-trial equality with the dam-break component DAM (C16 ⊕ DAM, c1)')
same = tot = 0
for _ in range(1500):
    p = dict(g0=(rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), rq(Fr(1, 2), 1)), c=(rq(Fr(1, 2), 2), rq(Fr(1, 2), 2)), alpha=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)),
             lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)), cap=(rq(1, 4), rq(1, 4)), Bbar=(rq(Fr(1, 4), 3), rq(Fr(1, 4), 3)),
             rhoH=rq(0, 1), muH=rq(0, 1), ks=rq(0, 2))
    m12 = M('N12', **p); md = M('DAM', **p)
    H0 = (rq(0, 2), rq(0, 2)); d0 = (rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1))
    q12 = []; qd = []
    for k in range(R.randint(1, 12)):
        P = R.choice([Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)]); pres = R.choice([BOTH, A, B]); r = R.randint(0, 2)
        blk = tuple(x for x in (0, 1) if R.random() < 0.2); ub = tuple(x for x in (0, 1) if R.random() < 0.15)
        q12.append((P, pres, r, blk, ub)); qd.append((P, 0, pres, r, blk, ub))
    s12 = tc.n12_new(d=d0); s12['H'] = [Fr(H0[0]), Fr(H0[1])]
    o1, z1 = tc.n12_run(m12, s12, 10, q12)
    o2, z2 = te.run(md, te.new(d=d0, H=H0), 10, qd)
    tot += 1
    same += (o1 == o2 and z1['d'] == z2['d'] and z1['H'] == z2['H'] and z1['brk'] == z2['brk'])
print('  %d random runs (arbitrary setting and release of blockades, presentation, rest, initial accumulated head): readings and final state (d, H, Brk) all identical in %d' % (tot, same))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ E4
print('\nE4  Reproduction of the two N12 signatures by a depth-2 combination without DAM (VST gated by 1[blocked] ⊕ PF)')
mvp = M('VSTPF', **base, Ap=Fr(1, 8), thp=0, thm=0, muu=1, vst_blk_only=1, Bbar=(Fr(100),) * 2, rhoH=Fr(1, 4), muH=0)
o1, _ = te.run(mvp, te.new(), 400, q_pd(Fr(1))); o2, _ = te.run(mvp, te.new(), 400, q_pd(Fr(5)))
print('  I_PD two arms: blockade period %s | %s; release trial %s | %s' % (seq(o1[:4]), seq(o2[:4]), ystr(o1[4][0]), ystr(o2[4][0])))
mvp2 = M('VSTPF', **base, Ap=Fr(1, 8), thp=0, thm=0, muu=1, vst_blk_only=1, Bbar=(Fr(1),) * 2, rhoH=Fr(1, 2), muH=0)
o, st = te.run(mvp2, te.new(), 40, [(Fr(1), 0, BOTH, 0, (0,) if k == 0 else (), ()) for k in range(4)] + [(Fr(1), 0, A, 0, (), ())])
print('  I_B: %s; events %s' % (seq(o), st['events']))
# Where it is inexact: N12 defers its write until release, whereas VSTPF writes into d at once during the blockade period; the two separate when rest is inserted in the blockade period and μ_H ≠ λ
mn = M('N12', **base, Bbar=(Fr(100),) * 2, rhoH=Fr(1, 4), muH=Fr(1, 2), ks=1)
mv = M('VSTPF', **base, Ap=Fr(1, 4), thp=0, thm=0, muu=1, vst_blk_only=1, Bbar=(Fr(100),) * 2, rhoH=Fr(1, 4), muH=Fr(1, 2))
qa = [(Fr(1), BOTH, 6 if k == 3 else 0, (0,) if k == 0 else (), ()) for k in range(4)] + [(Fr(1), BOTH, 0, (), (0,))]
o1, z1 = tc.n12_run(mn, tc.n12_new(), 400, qa)
o2, z2 = te.run(mv, te.new(), 400, [(P, 0, pr, r, b, ub) for (P, pr, r, b, ub) in qa])
print('  Rest of 6 beats after the last blockade beat (μ_H = 1/2, λ = 1/10), then release: at release N12 d_a = %s, VSTPF d_a = %s; release trial %s | %s'
      % (z1['d'][0], z2['d'][0], ystr(o1[4][0]), ystr(o2[4][0])))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ E5
print('\nE5  N13 “Mutual Construction of Tendency and Shape” (the direction of two-way mutual control)')
m13 = M('N13', nu=2, th0=Fr(1, 2), alpha=Fr(1, 2), lam=Fr(1, 10))
o, d = te.n13_run(m13, (Fr(1, 2), 0), 40, [(Fr(1), BOTH, 0)] * 8)
print('  Witness (ν = 2, θ₀ = 1/2, α = 1/2, initial state (1/2, 0), P = 1, T = 40): %s; final state d = (%s, %s)'
      % (' '.join('%s@%s' % (ystr(Y), t) for Y, t in o), float(d[0]), float(d[1])))
o, d = te.n13_run(m13, (Fr(3), 0), 40, [(Fr(1), A, 0)] * 3)
print('  Proposition N13-1 witness (only a presented, three trials each from initial d_a = 3 and 0): %s | %s'
      % (' '.join('%s@%s' % (ystr(Y), t) for Y, t in o),
         ' '.join('%s@%s' % (ystr(Y), t) for Y, t in te.n13_run(m13, (0, 0), 40, [(Fr(1), A, 0)] * 3)[0])))
# Four-arm protocol q13(m, d_b0, P, T): common initial state (0, d_b0); arm 1 is tested directly; arm 2 is first presented with a alone m times (rest 0), then tested; there are two tests: both paths presented (read τ_min and Y) and only b presented (read τ)
# Signature S13: in arm 1 the two-path test has b ∈ Y; in arm 2 the two-path test has τ_min strictly greater than in arm 1; the two arms have the same τ in the b-only test.
def s13_generic(runner, mm, P, T):
    tr_both = [(P, BOTH)]; tr_b = [(P, B)]
    pre = [(Fr(1), A)] * mm
    r1 = runner([] + tr_both)[-1]; r2 = runner(pre + tr_both)[-1]
    b1 = runner([] + tr_b)[-1]; b2 = runner(pre + tr_b)[-1]
    return r1, r2, b1, b2

def is_s13(r1, r2, b1, b2):
    return (1 in r1[0]) and r1[1] is not None and r2[1] is not None and r2[1] > r1[1] and b1[1] == b2[1]

def runner_v01(m, st0, T):
    return lambda q: td.run_blk(m, st0, T, [(P, 0, pr, 0, frozenset()) for (P, pr) in q])[0]

def runner_v02(m, st0, T):
    return lambda q: te.run(m, st0, T, [(P, 0, pr, 0, (), ()) for (P, pr) in q])[0]

def runner_n13(m, d0, T):
    return lambda q: te.n13_run(m, d0, T, [(P, pr, 0) for (P, pr) in q])[0]

mC = M('C', **base)
for name, rn in (('C', runner_v01(mC, tb.new_state(mC, d=(0, 2)), 400)),
                 ('HS', runner_v01(M('HS', **base, kappa=Fr(1, 4)), tb.new_state(mC, d=(0, 2)), 400)),
                 ('CMP', runner_v02(M('CMP', **base, kc=Fr(1, 4)), te.new(d=(0, 2)), 400)),
                 ('N13', runner_n13(M('N13', nu=2, th0=Fr(1, 4), alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10)), (0, Fr(2)), 400))):
    r1, r2, b1, b2 = s13_generic(rn, 2, Fr(1), 400)
    print('  Four-arm witness %-4s (initial state (0, 2), arm 2 first presented with a alone 2 times): two-path test arm 1 %s@%s | arm 2 %s@%s; only b presented arm 1 τ = %s | arm 2 τ = %s; S13 %s'
          % (name, ystr(r1[0]), r1[1], ystr(r2[0]), r2[1], b1[1], b2[1], 'yes' if is_s13(r1, r2, b1, b2) else 'no'))

def mk(kind):
    g0s = (rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), rq(Fr(1, 2), 1)); cs = (rq(Fr(1, 2), 2), rq(Fr(1, 2), 2))
    bs = dict(g0=g0s, c=cs, alpha=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1),) * 2, cap=(rq(1, 4), rq(2, 4)))
    if kind == 'C': return M('C', **bs), 'v01'
    if kind == 'N1': return M('N1', **bs, phi=rq(0, 1), mu=rq(0, 1)), 'v01'
    if kind == 'STF': return M('STF', **bs, kF=rq(0, 1), Fbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1)), 'v01'
    if kind == 'HOM': return M('HOM', **bs, kH=rq(0, 1), Hbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), nu=rq(0, 1)), 'v01'
    if kind == 'HS': return M('HS', **bs, kappa=rq(0, 1)), 'v01'
    if kind == 'HSP': return M('HSP', **bs, kappa=rq(0, 1)), 'v01'
    if kind == 'TF3': return M('TF3', **bs), 'v01'
    if kind == 'RW': return M('RW', g0=g0s, c=cs, eta=rq(0, 1), Vbar=rq(0, 2), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2), 'v01'
    if kind == 'TWO': return M('TWO', g0=g0s, c=cs, Bf=rq(0, Fr(1, 2)), Bs=rq(0, Fr(1, 2)), Vbar=rq(0, 2), lf=rq(0, 1), ls=rq(0, 1)), 'v01'
    if kind == 'FS': return M('FS', g0=g0s, c=cs, V=bs['alpha'], Cap=bs['cap'], leak=bs['lam']), 'FS'
    if kind == 'LIF': return M('LIF', **bs, rhoV=rq(0, 1), muV=rq(0, 1)), 'LIF'
    if kind == 'CMP': return M('CMP', **bs, kc=rq(0, 1)), 'v02'
    if kind == 'VST': return M('VST', **bs, Ap=rq(0, 1), thp=rq(0, 1), thm=rq(0, 1), muu=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)), 'v02'
    if kind == 'BCM': return M('BCM', **bs, eta=rq(0, Fr(1, 2)), muM=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)), 'v02'
    if kind == 'DAM': return M('DAM', **bs, Bbar=(rq(Fr(1, 4), 3),) * 2, rhoH=rq(0, 1), muH=rq(0, 1), ks=rq(0, 2)), 'v02'
    if kind == 'PFERO': return M('PFERO', **bs, Bbar=(rq(Fr(1, 4), 3),) * 2, rhoH=rq(0, 1), muH=rq(0, 1), Kb=R.randint(1, 8), Prel=rq(1, 3)), 'v02'
    if kind == 'VSTCMP': return M('VSTCMP', **bs, kc=rq(0, 1), Ap=rq(0, 1), thp=rq(0, 1), thm=rq(0, 1), muu=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)), 'v02'

def mk_runner(m, tag, db0, T):
    if tag == 'FS': return runner_v01(m, tb.fs_state(S=(0, db0)), T)
    if tag == 'LIF': return runner_v01(m, tb.lif_state(V=0, d=(0, db0)), T)
    if tag == 'v02': return runner_v02(m, te.new(d=(0, db0)), T)
    if m.kind == 'TWO': return runner_v01(m, tb.new_state(m, df=(0, db0), ds=(0, 0)), T)
    return runner_v01(m, tb.new_state(m, d=(0, db0)), T)

print('  Random check of S13 (300 runs per kind; initial state (0, d_b0), d_b0 ∈ [0, 3], m ∈ 1..4, P ∈ {1, 2}, T = 200):')
line = []
for kind in ('C', 'N1', 'STF', 'HOM', 'HS', 'HSP', 'TF3', 'RW', 'TWO', 'FS', 'LIF', 'CMP', 'VST', 'BCM', 'DAM', 'PFERO', 'VSTCMP'):
    hit = 0
    for _ in range(300):
        m, tag = mk(kind)
        rn = mk_runner(m, tag, rq(0, 3), 200)
        hit += is_s13(*s13_generic(rn, R.randint(1, 4), R.choice([Fr(1), Fr(2)]), 200))
    line.append('%s %d' % (kind, hit))
print('    Runs showing S13: ' + ', '.join(line))
hit = cond = 0
for _ in range(600):
    nu = R.randint(1, 3); th0 = rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2)); al = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1); mm = R.randint(1, 4)
    db0 = mm * al + rq(0, 2); P = R.choice([Fr(1), Fr(2)]); T = 200
    m = M('N13', nu=nu, th0=th0, alpha=al, lam=Fr(1, 10))
    ok = th0 * T * ((1 + mm * al) ** nu - 1) / ((1 + db0) ** nu * P) >= 1 and th0 * T * 2 / P <= T
    res = is_s13(*s13_generic(runner_n13(m, (0, db0), T), mm, P, T))
    cond += ok; hit += (ok and res)
print('    N13 (ν ∈ {1, 2, 3}, d_b0 ≥ m·α, 600 random runs): the conditions of Theorem N13-3 hold in %d runs, of which %d show S13' % (cond, hit))
# Illustrative construction outside the contract (the threshold term reads this trial's presentation set, which oversteps step ① of the C_cmp contract, “the threshold is computed only from the pre-trial state”): θ_x = g(d_x) + κ·φ(d_o)·1[o ∈ Pres], φ(d) = min(1, d/D_φ)
def run_x3(kap, Dphi, d0, T, q):
    d = [tb.F(d0[0]), tb.F(d0[1])]; out = []
    for (P, pres) in q:
        P = tb.F(P); taus = []
        for x in (0, 1):
            if x not in pres:
                taus.append(None); continue
            th = g(1, 1, d[x]) + (kap * min(Fr(1), d[1 - x] / Dphi) if (1 - x) in pres else 0)
            taus.append(tb.open_step(th, Fr(0), P, T))
        fin = [t for t in taus if t is not None]
        Y = frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if fin and taus[x] == min(fin)) if fin else frozenset()
        for x in Y: d[x] += Fr(1, 4)
        out.append((Y, min(fin) if fin else None))
    return out
r1, r2, b1, b2 = s13_generic(lambda q: run_x3(Fr(1, 4), Fr(2), (0, 2), 400, q), 2, Fr(1), 400)
print('  Illustrative construction outside the contract (threshold term reads this trial’s presentation set: θ_x = g(d_x) + κ·min(1, d_o/D_φ)·1[o ∈ Pres], κ = 1/4, D_φ = 2; not a member of C_cmp): two-path test %s@%s | %s@%s; only b presented τ = %s | %s; S13 %s'
      % (ystr(r1[0]), r1[1], ystr(r2[0]), r2[1], b1[1], b2[1], 'yes' if is_s13(r1, r2, b1, b2) else 'no'))
print('\nDone.')
~~~

A.13  run16\_5.py  Run entry: sections F0–F4 (Proposition N13-2; Theorem N13-5 and Proposition 4-6; witnesses of N14 and S14)

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_5.py (sections F1–F4).
Run: python3 run16_5.py. The output is deterministic. The program provides only finite verification, explicit witnesses and contrasts; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, math
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import t16b_core as tb
import t16e_ext as te
import t16f_ext as tf
from t16b_core import M, BOTH, ystr, g
R = random.Random(16505)
A, B = frozenset((0,)), frozenset((1,))
here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
INF = float('inf')

def rq(lo, hi, den=12):
    a = math.ceil(Fr(lo) * den); b = math.floor(Fr(hi) * den)
    return Fr(R.randint(a, b), den)

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()

def tv(t):
    return INF if t is None else t

print('The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_5.py')
print('=' * 72)
print('\nF0  Version')
for f in ('t16b_core.py', 't16e_ext.py', 't16f_ext.py'):
    print('  %s SHA-256: %s' % (f, sha(os.path.join(here, f))))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ F1
print('\nF1  Direction of Proposition N13-2 (continuous first-arrival quantity kept apart from the discrete first-opening step)')

def taus13(nu, th0, da, db, P, T):
    wa = (1 + da) ** nu / ((1 + da) ** nu + (1 + db) ** nu); wb = 1 - wa
    qa, qb = th0 * T / (wa * P), th0 * T / (wb * P)
    ta, tb_ = math.ceil(qa), math.ceil(qb)
    return qa, qb, (ta if ta <= T else None), (tb_ if tb_ <= T else None)

m13 = M('N13', nu=1, th0=Fr(13, 50), alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10))
for (da, db) in ((Fr(0), Fr(1, 100)), (Fr(0), Fr(1))):
    qa, qb, ta, tb_ = taus13(1, Fr(13, 50), da, db, Fr(1), 10)
    o, _ = te.n13_run(m13, (da, db), 10, [(1, BOTH, 0)])
    print('  ν = 1, θ₀ = 13/50, P = 1, T = 10, d = (%s, %s): continuous first-arrival quantities q_a = %s, q_b = %s; first-opening steps τ_a = %s, τ_b = %s; n13_run reading %s@%s'
          % (da, db, qa, qb, ta, tb_, ystr(o[0][0]), o[0][1]))
fw = strict = conv = gridv = reach = cnt = 0
for _ in range(20000):
    nu = R.randint(1, 3); th0 = rq(Fr(1, 50), Fr(1, 2), 50); T = R.choice([5, 10, 20, 40]); P = R.choice([Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)])
    da, db = rq(0, 2, 100), rq(0, 2, 100)
    qa, qb, ta, tb_ = taus13(nu, th0, da, db, P, T)
    cnt += 1
    if da >= db and not (tv(ta) <= tv(tb_)):
        fw += 1
    if tv(ta) < tv(tb_) and not (da > db):
        strict += 1
    if tv(ta) <= tv(tb_) and da < db:
        conv += 1
    if qb - qa >= 1 and ta is not None and not (ta < tv(tb_)):
        gridv += 1
    if P >= 2 * th0 and da >= db and ta is None:
        reach += 1
print('  %d random pairs: counterexamples to “d_a ≥ d_b ⇒ τ_a ≤ τ_b” (unreachable recorded as ∞) %d; to “τ_a < τ_b ⇒ d_a > d_b” %d; to “q_b − q_a ≥ 1 and τ_a reachable ⇒ τ_a < τ_b” %d; to “P ≥ 2θ₀ and d_a ≥ d_b ⇒ τ_a reachable” %d; converse case “τ_a ≤ τ_b while d_a < d_b” occurs in %d'
      % (cnt, fw, strict, gridv, reach, conv))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ F2
print('\nF2  C_cmp v0.3: trial-by-trial equality of N13 and c2(KIR, 1[x ∈ Y]); contrast of the original KIR (every path in the pool reinforced) with DNC')
same = tot = 0
for _ in range(1500):
    p = dict(nu=R.randint(1, 3), th0=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2)), alpha=rq(0, 1), lam=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1))
    mN = M('N13', **p); mK = M('KIR', **p, mu=1)
    d0 = (rq(0, 2), rq(0, 2)); T = R.choice([10, 40, 200])
    qN = []; qK = []
    for k in range(R.randint(1, 12)):
        P = R.choice([Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)]); pres = R.choice([BOTH, A, B]); r = R.randint(0, 2)
        qN.append((P, pres, r)); qK.append((P, pres, r, frozenset()))
    o1, z1 = te.n13_run(mN, d0, T, qN)
    o2, z2 = tf.kir_run(mK, d0, T, qK, gateY=1)
    tot += 1; same += (o1 == o2 and z1 == z2)
print('  %d random runs (nonempty presentation set, arbitrary P, rest and initial state): readings and final state all identical in %d' % (tot, same))
mK = M('KIR', nu=2, th0=Fr(1, 4), alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10), mu=1)
q = [(Fr(1), BOTH, 0, frozenset())] * 4
o1, z1 = tf.kir_run(mK, (Fr(1, 2), 0), 40, q, gateY=1); o2, z2 = tf.kir_run(mK, (Fr(1, 2), 0), 40, q, gateY=0)
print('  KIR gated (= N13) vs original KIR (every path in the pool reinforced): %s | %s; final state d ≈ (%.6f, %.6f) | (%.6f, %.6f)'
      % (' '.join('%s@%s' % (ystr(Y), t) for Y, t in o1), ' '.join('%s@%s' % (ystr(Y), t) for Y, t in o2),
         float(z1[0]), float(z1[1]), float(z2[0]), float(z2[1])))

def s13(runner, mm, P):
    pre = [(Fr(1), A)] * mm
    r1 = runner([(P, BOTH)])[-1]; r2 = runner(pre + [(P, BOTH)])[-1]
    b1 = runner([(P, B)])[-1]; b2 = runner(pre + [(P, B)])[-1]
    ok = (1 in r1[0]) and r1[1] is not None and r2[1] is not None and r2[1] > r1[1] and b1[1] == b2[1]
    return ok, r1, r2, b1, b2

for name, rn in (('KIR gated', lambda qq: tf.kir_run(mK, (0, 2), 400, [(P, pr, 0, frozenset()) for P, pr in qq], 1)[0]),
                 ('KIR original', lambda qq: tf.kir_run(mK, (0, 2), 400, [(P, pr, 0, frozenset()) for P, pr in qq], 0)[0]),
                 ('DNC', lambda qq: tf.dnc_run(M('DNC', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(Fr(1, 4),) * 2, lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, n=2, sigma=0, thN=Fr(1, 8)),
                                               (0, 2), 400, [(P, pr, 0) for P, pr in qq])[0])):
    ok, r1, r2, b1, b2 = s13(rn, 2, Fr(1))
    print('  Four-arm S13 (initial state (0, 2), arm 2 first presented with a alone 2 times) %s: two-path test %s@%s | %s@%s; only b presented τ = %s | %s; S13 %s'
          % (name, ystr(r1[0]), r1[1], ystr(r2[0]), r2[1], b1[1], b2[1], 'yes' if ok else 'no'))
hit = {'KIR original': 0, 'DNC': 0}; n_ = 300
for _ in range(n_):
    p = dict(nu=R.randint(1, 3), th0=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 4)), alpha=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), lam=Fr(1, 10), mu=1)
    mm = R.randint(1, 4); db0 = mm * p['alpha'] + rq(0, 2)
    mk = M('KIR', **p)
    ok = s13(lambda qq: tf.kir_run(mk, (0, db0), 200, [(P, pr, 0, frozenset()) for P, pr in qq], 0)[0], mm, Fr(1))[0]
    hit['KIR original'] += ok
    md = M('DNC', g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(p['alpha'],) * 2, lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, n=p['nu'], sigma=rq(0, Fr(1, 2)), thN=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 4)))
    ok = s13(lambda qq: tf.dnc_run(md, (0, db0), 400, [(P, pr, 0) for P, pr in qq])[0], mm, Fr(1))[0]
    hit['DNC'] += ok
print('  %d random runs each: original KIR shows S13 in %d; DNC shows S13 in %d (S13 is no longer exclusive to N13)' % (n_, hit['KIR original'], hit['DNC']))
# Proposition 4-5 fails in v0.3: in the same state, the threshold of b changes with this trial's presentation set
o1, _ = tf.kir_run(mK, (Fr(1), Fr(1)), 400, [(Fr(1), B, 0, frozenset())], 1)
o2, _ = tf.kir_run(mK, (Fr(1), Fr(1)), 400, [(Fr(1), BOTH, 0, frozenset())], 1)
print('  Witness that Proposition 4-5 fails in v0.3 (KIR, state (1, 1)): only b presented τ = %s; both paths presented τ_min = %s' % (o1[0][1], o2[0][1]))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ F3
print('\nF3  N14 “Coloring Biased-Flow”: witness, transfer, avoidance reversal')
m14 = M('N14', g0=1, cc=1, alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10), kappa=Fr(1, 2), beta=Fr(1, 4), cbar=2, muc=Fr(1, 10))

def show(o):
    return ' '.join('%s@%s' % (ystr(Y), t) for Y, t in o)

for sg in (1, 0, -1):
    q = [(Fr(1), A, 0, sg)] * 4 + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * 6
    o, st = tf.e_run(m14, {'d': (0, Fr(1))}, 40, q)
    print('  e = %+d, pretraining on a 4 times, then only b tested 6 times (initial state (0, 1)): pretraining %s | tests %s; final state c = %s, d_b = %s' % (sg, show(o[:4]), show(o[4:]), st['c'], st['d'][1]))
# Two-arm signature S14
def s14(model, db0, mm, n, P, T, sg):
    qa = [(P, A, 0, sg)] * mm + [(P, B, 0, 0)] * n
    q0 = [(P, A, 0, 0)] * mm + [(P, B, 0, 0)] * n
    o1, _ = tf.e_run(model, {'d': (0, db0)}, T, qa)
    o0, _ = tf.e_run(model, {'d': (0, db0)}, T, q0)
    pre_ok = all(o1[k][0] == o0[k][0] and o1[k][0] for k in range(mm))
    test1 = o1[mm:]; test0 = o0[mm:]
    return pre_ok and test1[0] == test0[0] and test1 != test0

def base14():
    return dict(g0=rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), cc=rq(Fr(1, 2), 2), alpha=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), lam=Fr(1, 10))

def mk14(kind):
    b = base14()
    if kind == 'N14': return M('N14', **b, kappa=rq(Fr(1, 4), 2), beta=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), cbar=rq(1, 3), muc=rq(0, 1))
    if kind == 'C16': return M('C', **b)
    if kind == 'TF3e': return M('TF3e', **b, alpha_e=rq(0, 1))
    if kind == 'RWe': return M('RWe', **b, eta=rq(0, 1), Vbar=rq(0, 2))
    if kind == 'HS': return M('HS', **b, kh=rq(0, 1))
    if kind == 'STF': return M('STF', **b, kF=rq(0, 1), Fbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1))
    if kind == 'TF3e+HS+STF': return M('L3', **b, alpha_e=rq(0, 1), kh=rq(0, 1), kF=rq(0, 1), Fbar=rq(0, Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1))
    if kind == 'X1(HS·1[e=+1])': return M('X1', **b, kh=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), hs_gate=1)
    if kind == 'X2(STF·1[e=+1])': return M('X2', **b, kF=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), Fbar=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(11, 12)), muF=rq(0, 1), stf_gate=1)
    if kind == 'X3(K1·φ(V_oth))': return M('X3', **b, a0=rq(0, Fr(1, 2)), b1=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), eta_v=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1))

print('  Signature S14 (pretraining with only a presented m times, arm A e = +1, arm B e = 0, the two arms having the same nonempty first-opened set in pretraining; then only b presented n times, e = 0): the first test of b agrees and some later test differs')
line = []
for kind in ('N14', 'C16', 'TF3e', 'RWe', 'HS', 'STF', 'TF3e+HS+STF', 'X1(HS·1[e=+1])', 'X2(STF·1[e=+1])', 'X3(K1·φ(V_oth))'):
    hit = 0
    for _ in range(300):
        mdl = mk14(kind)
        hit += s14(mdl, rq(0, 2), R.randint(1, 4), R.randint(2, 6), R.choice([Fr(1), Fr(2)]), 200, 1)
    line.append('%s %d' % (kind, hit))
print('    300 random runs each, runs showing S14: ' + ', '.join(line))
# Under the hypotheses of Theorem N14-3
cond = hit = 0
for _ in range(600):
    b = base14(); kap = rq(Fr(1, 4), 2); be = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1); cb = rq(1, 3); mm = R.randint(1, 4); db0 = rq(0, 2); P = R.choice([Fr(1), Fr(2)]); T = 200
    mdl = M('N14', **b, kappa=kap, beta=be, cbar=cb, muc=Fr(0))
    cpos = min(mm * be, cb)
    gg = lambda d: g(b['g0'], b['cc'], d)
    ok = Fr(T) / P * (gg(db0 + b['alpha']) - gg(db0 + b['alpha'] * (1 + kap * cpos))) >= 1
    cond += ok
    hit += ok and s14(mdl, db0, mm, 2, P, T, 1)
print('    Hypotheses of Theorem N14-3 (margin inequality holds) in %d runs, of which %d show S14' % (cond, hit))
# Avoidance reversal: after pretraining with e = −1, use of b makes it shallower (first-opening step grows), while in the e = 0 arm the step does not grow
cond = hit = 0
for _ in range(600):
    b = base14(); kap = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); be = rq(Fr(1, 4), 1); cb = rq(1, 3); mm = R.randint(2, 4); db0 = rq(1, 3); P = Fr(1); T = 200
    mdl = M('N14', **b, kappa=kap, beta=be, cbar=cb, muc=Fr(0))
    cneg = min(mm * be, cb); fac = 1 - kap * cneg
    gg = lambda d: g(b['g0'], b['cc'], d)
    ok = fac < 0 and db0 + b['alpha'] * fac >= 0 and Fr(T) / P * (gg(db0 + b['alpha'] * fac) - gg(db0)) >= 1
    if not ok:
        continue
    cond += 1
    o, _ = tf.e_run(mdl, {'d': (0, db0)}, T, [(P, A, 0, -1)] * mm + [(P, B, 0, 0)] * 2)
    o0, _ = tf.e_run(mdl, {'d': (0, db0)}, T, [(P, A, 0, 0)] * mm + [(P, B, 0, 0)] * 2)
    hit += (o[mm + 1][1] > o[mm][1]) and (o0[mm + 1][1] <= o0[mm][1])
print('    Hypotheses of Proposition N14-2 in %d runs, of which %d have “in the e = −1 arm the second test of b has a larger first-opening step than the first, in the e = 0 arm not larger”' % (cond, hit))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ F4
print('\nF4  Contrast witnesses for the rate type (N14, X3) and the offset type (X1, X2) (zero initial state, pretraining with only a presented 4 times, arm A e = +1, arm B e = 0, then only b tested 8 times)')
mods = (('N14', M('N14', g0=1, cc=1, alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10), kappa=Fr(1, 2), beta=Fr(1, 4), cbar=2, muc=0)),
        ('X1', M('X1', g0=1, cc=1, alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10), kh=Fr(1, 2), hs_gate=1)),
        ('X2', M('X2', g0=1, cc=1, alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10), kF=Fr(1, 4), Fbar=Fr(1, 2), muF=0, stf_gate=1)),
        ('X3', M('X3', g0=1, cc=1, alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10), a0=Fr(1, 2), b1=Fr(1, 2), eta_v=Fr(1, 2))))
for name, mdl in mods:
    o1, s1 = tf.e_run(mdl, {'d': (0, 0)}, 400, [(Fr(1), A, 0, 1)] * 4 + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * 8)
    o0, s0 = tf.e_run(mdl, {'d': (0, 0)}, 400, [(Fr(1), A, 0, 0)] * 4 + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * 8)
    print('  %-3s first-opening steps of the b-only tests arm A %s | arm B %s; final state d_b %s | %s, difference %s'
          % (name, ' '.join(str(t) for _, t in o1[4:]), ' '.join(str(t) for _, t in o0[4:]), s1['d'][1], s0['d'][1], s1['d'][1] - s0['d'][1]))
print('\nDone.')
~~~

A.14  run16\_6.py  Run entry: sections G0–G3 (out-of-class controls; Theorem N14-5; S15 of N15 and Proposition N15-5)

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_6.py (sections G1–G3).
Run: python3 run16_6.py. The output is deterministic. The program provides only finite verification, explicit witnesses and contrasts; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, math
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import t16b_core as tb
import t16f_ext as tf
import t16g_ext as tg
from t16b_core import M, ystr, g
R = random.Random(16606)
A, B, BOTH = frozenset((0,)), frozenset((1,)), frozenset((0, 1))
here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def rq(lo, hi, den=12):
    a = math.ceil(Fr(lo) * den); b = math.floor(Fr(hi) * den)
    return Fr(R.randint(a, b), den)

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()

def ys(Y):
    return '{' + ','.join({0: 'a', 1: 'b', 2: 'ℓ'}[x] for x in sorted(Y)) + '}' if Y else '∅'

print('The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_6.py')
print('=' * 72)
print('\nG0  Version')
for f in ('t16b_core.py', 't16f_ext.py', 't16g_ext.py'):
    print('  %s SHA-256: %s' % (f, sha(os.path.join(here, f))))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ G1
print('\nG1  Control model for the c4 clipping switch (outside the non-interference class)')
for e in (1, 0):
    Yp, o = tg.c4_example(e)
    print('  e = %+d: pretraining first-opened sets %s; first-opening steps when only b is tested %s' % (e, ystr(Yp), o))
print('  Reading: in pretraining both arms have Y = {a}, and the update that reads e writes only a; the difference enters through “clipping event of a → c4 switches the write law of b”. By condition (N3) of the non-interference class Λ_e^S, an event that switches a law writing b must not be an event on a, so this construction lies outside the class.')
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ G2
print('\nG2  C_cmp v0.4: trial-by-trial equality of N14 and c1(C16, IZH); S14 for the IZH variant closer to the literature, TON and PIT')
same = tot = 0
for _ in range(1500):
    al = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1); kap = rq(Fr(1, 4), 2); be = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1); cb = rq(1, 3); mu = rq(0, 1)
    base = dict(g0=rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), cc=rq(Fr(1, 2), 2), alpha=al, lam=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1))
    mN = M('N14', **base, kappa=kap, beta=be, cbar=cb, muc=mu)
    mI = M('IZH', **base, alphaI=al * kap, epsD=be, Dbar=cb, muD=mu)
    d0 = (rq(0, 2), rq(0, 2)); T = R.choice([10, 40, 200])
    q = [(R.choice([Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)]), R.choice([BOTH, A, B]), R.randint(0, 2), R.choice([-1, 0, 1])) for _ in range(R.randint(1, 12))]
    o1, z1 = tf.e_run(mN, {'d': d0}, T, q)
    o2, z2 = tg.m_run(mI, d0, T, q)
    tot += 1; same += (o1 == o2 and z1['d'] == z2['d'] and z1['c'] == z2['D'])
print('  %d random runs (arbitrary presentation, rest, e ∈ {−1, 0, +1}, initial state): readings and final state (d; Coloring = modulator level) all identical in %d' % (tot, same))

def s14(run, db0, mm, n, P, T):
    o1, _ = run([(P, A, 0, 1)] * mm + [(P, B, 0, 0)] * n, db0)
    o0, _ = run([(P, A, 0, 0)] * mm + [(P, B, 0, 0)] * n, db0)
    pre_ok = all(o1[k][0] == o0[k][0] and o1[k][0] for k in range(mm))
    return pre_ok and o1[mm] == o0[mm] and o1[mm:] != o0[mm:]

def mkv(kind):
    base = dict(g0=rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), cc=rq(Fr(1, 2), 2), alpha=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), lam=Fr(1, 10))
    if kind == 'IZH (immediate eligibility, decay at rest)': return M('IZH', **base, alphaI=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), epsD=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), Dbar=rq(1, 3), muD=rq(0, 1))
    if kind == 'IZHc (eligibility trace, trial-by-trial decay)': return M('IZHc', **base, alphaI=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), epsD=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), Dbar=rq(1, 3), muD=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2)), mu_el=rq(Fr(1, 4), 1), da_per_trial=1)
    if kind == 'TON': return M('TON', **base, etaR=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), psiT=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2)))
    if kind == 'PIT': return M('PIT', **base, etaV=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), psiV=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2)))
    if kind == 'TON⊕PIT': return M('TP', **base, etaR=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), psiT=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2)), etaV=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), psiV=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2)))

line = []
for kind in ('IZH (immediate eligibility, decay at rest)', 'IZHc (eligibility trace, trial-by-trial decay)', 'TON', 'PIT', 'TON⊕PIT'):
    hit = 0
    for _ in range(300):
        mdl = mkv(kind)
        hit += s14(lambda q, db0: tg.m_run(mdl, (0, db0), 200, q), rq(0, 2), R.randint(1, 4), R.randint(2, 6), R.choice([Fr(1), Fr(2)]), 200)
    line.append('%s %d' % (kind, hit))
print('  S14, 300 random runs each: ' + ', '.join(line))
mN = M('N14', g0=1, cc=1, alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10), kappa=Fr(1, 2), beta=Fr(1, 4), cbar=2, muc=0)
mC = M('IZHc', g0=1, cc=1, alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10), alphaI=Fr(1, 8), epsD=Fr(1, 4), Dbar=2, muD=Fr(1, 4), mu_el=Fr(1, 2), da_per_trial=1)
q = [(Fr(1), A, 0, 1)] * 4 + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * 8
o1, _ = tf.e_run(mN, {'d': (0, 0)}, 400, q); o2, z2 = tg.m_run(mC, (0, 0), 400, q)
print('  Witness that N14 and IZHc are not equal (pretraining: a paired with good 4 times; then only b tested 8 times): first-opening steps of b N14 %s | IZHc %s (the modulator level of IZHc decays trial by trial during testing, and the rate of b falls back)'
      % (' '.join(str(t) for _, t in o1[4:]), ' '.join(str(t) for _, t in o2[4:])))
mT = M('TON', g0=1, cc=1, alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10), etaR=Fr(1, 2), psiT=Fr(1, 4))
o1, _ = tg.m_run(mT, (0, 0), 400, q); o0, _ = tg.m_run(mT, (0, 0), 400, [(Fr(1), A, 0, 0)] * 4 + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * 8)
print('  Performance-type witness for TON: first-opening steps of b, good arm %s | neutral arm %s (already different at the first test: acts on performance, does not change writes)'
      % (' '.join(str(t) for _, t in o1[4:]), ' '.join(str(t) for _, t in o0[4:])))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ G3
print('\nG3  W16-F and N15 “Overflow Position Generation”: trigger and detectability of generation, and the signature against GNG, MSP, c4(GNG@full) and GWR')
base3 = dict(g0=1, cc=1, alpha=(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4)), lam=Fr(1, 10), capa=1, capb=4, capl=4)
mods = {
    'N15': M('N15', **base3, sigma=Fr(3, 4), mus=0),
    'GNG': M('GNG', **base3, lamG=4, alphaG=Fr(1, 2), dG=Fr(199, 200)),
    'c4(GNG@full)': M('GNGf', **base3, lamG=4, alphaG=Fr(1, 2), dG=Fr(199, 200), gng_after_full=1),
    'MSP': M('MSP', **base3, nuM=Fr(1, 4), muA=Fr(1, 2), epsM=Fr(1, 2)),
    'GWR': M('GWR', **base3, hT=Fr(1, 2), tauh=Fr(4), alh=Fr(1, 2)),
    'C16 (no generation)': M('C', **base3),
}

def q15(model, arm, n, da0, capa=None):
    mm = model if capa is None else model
    pres = A if arm in ('A', 'b') else B
    tr = [(Fr(1), pres, 0, frozenset())] * n + [(Fr(1), A, 0, frozenset((0,)))]
    o, st = tg.s_run(mm, {'d': (da0, 0)}, 200, tr)
    return o[-1][0], st['gen_at']

for name, mdl in mods.items():
    ya, ga = q15(mdl, 'A', 8, Fr(1))
    yb, gb = q15(mdl, 'B', 8, Fr(1))
    mdl2 = M(mdl.kind, **{k: v for k, v in mdl.p.items() if k != 'capa'}, capa=20)
    yc, gc = tg.s_run(mdl2, {'d': (0, 0)}, 200, [(Fr(1), A, 0, frozenset())] * 8 + [(Fr(1), A, 0, frozenset((0,)))])[0][-1][0], None
    print('  %-12s q15a arm A (a already full, only a presented 8 times), test with a blocked: %s (generated in trial %s); arm B (only b presented 8 times): %s (%s); q15b (a far from full, only a presented 8 times): %s'
          % (name, ys(ya), ga, ys(yb), gb, ys(yc)))

def is_s15(mdl, n, cap_big):
    ya, _ = q15(mdl, 'A', n, mdl['capa'])
    yb, _ = q15(mdl, 'B', n, mdl['capa'])
    mdl2 = M(mdl.kind, **{k: v for k, v in mdl.p.items() if k != 'capa'}, capa=cap_big)
    yc = tg.s_run(mdl2, {'d': (0, 0)}, 200, [(Fr(1), A, 0, frozenset())] * n + [(Fr(1), A, 0, frozenset((0,)))])[0][-1][0]
    return (2 in ya) and (2 not in yb) and (2 not in yc)

def mk3(kind):
    al = rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2))
    b = dict(g0=rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), cc=rq(Fr(1, 2), 2), alpha=(al, rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2)), rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2))), lam=Fr(1, 10), capa=rq(1, 2), capb=4, capl=4)
    if kind == 'N15': return M('N15', **b, sigma=rq(Fr(1, 12), 2), mus=0)
    if kind == 'GNG': return M('GNG', **b, lamG=R.randint(1, 8), alphaG=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(3, 4)), dG=Fr(199, 200))
    if kind == 'MSP': return M('MSP', **b, nuM=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(1, 2)), muA=rq(Fr(1, 4), 1), epsM=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(3, 4)))
    if kind == 'c4(GNG@full)': return M('GNGf', **b, lamG=R.randint(1, 8), alphaG=rq(Fr(1, 4), Fr(3, 4)), dG=Fr(199, 200), gng_after_full=1)
    if kind == 'GWR': return M('GWR', **b, hT=rq(Fr(1, 12), Fr(11, 12)), tauh=rq(1, 8), alh=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1))
    if kind == 'C16': return M('C', **b)

line = []
for kind in ('N15', 'GNG', 'MSP', 'c4(GNG@full)', 'GWR', 'C16'):
    hit = 0
    for _ in range(300):
        mdl = mk3(kind)
        n = 40
        hit += is_s15(mdl, n, 100)
    line.append('%s %d' % (kind, hit))
print('  S15, 300 random runs each (n = 40): ' + ', '.join(line))
# Dependence of the generation time on α_a (Proposition N15-5): N15 gives ⌈σ/α_a⌉ (counted from fullness), c4(GNG@full) gives λ (counted from the bottom-reaching event, independent of α_a)
print('  Dependence of the generation time on α_a (a initially full, only a presented; the trial of generation is recorded; Proposition N15-5):')
for name in ('N15', 'c4(GNG@full)', 'GWR'):
    row = []
    for al in (Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 8)):
        b = dict(g0=1, cc=1, alpha=(al, Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4)), lam=Fr(1, 10), capa=1, capb=4, capl=4)
        mdl = {'N15': lambda: M('N15', **b, sigma=Fr(3, 4), mus=0),
               'c4(GNG@full)': lambda: M('GNGf', **b, lamG=4, alphaG=Fr(1, 2), dG=Fr(199, 200), gng_after_full=1),
               'GWR': lambda: M('GWR', **b, hT=Fr(1, 2), tauh=Fr(4), alh=Fr(1, 2))}[name]()
        _, st = tg.s_run(mdl, {'d': (1, 0)}, 200, [(Fr(1), A, 0, frozenset())] * 20)
        row.append('α_a = %s: trial %s' % (al, st['gen_at']))
    print('    %-12s %s' % (name, '; '.join(row)))
print('\nDone.')
~~~

A.15  run16\_7.py  Run entry: sections H0–H6 (Theorem N15-1, Proposition N15-2; out-of-class controls; Theorem N14-6; IZD; Propositions N14-7, N14-8)

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_7.py (sections H1–H6).
Run: python3 run16_7.py. The output is deterministic. The program provides only finite verification, explicit witnesses and contrasts; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, math
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import t16b_core as tb
import t16f_ext as tf
import t16g_ext as tg
import t16h_ext as th
from t16b_core import M, g
R = random.Random(16707)
A, B, BOTH, NONE = frozenset((0,)), frozenset((1,)), frozenset((0, 1)), frozenset()
here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def rq(lo, hi, den=12):
    a = math.ceil(Fr(lo) * den); b = math.floor(Fr(hi) * den)
    return Fr(R.randint(a, b), den)

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()

def ys(Y):
    return '{' + ','.join({0: 'a', 1: 'b', 2: 'ℓ'}[x] for x in sorted(Y)) + '}' if Y else '∅'

def tau(theta, P, T):
    t = math.ceil(theta * T / P)
    return t if t <= T else None

print('The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_7.py')
print('=' * 72)
print('\nH0  Version')
for f in ('t16b_core.py', 't16f_ext.py', 't16g_ext.py', 't16h_ext.py'):
    print('  %s SHA-256: %s' % (f, sha(os.path.join(here, f))))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ H1
print('\nH1  Discrete reading of Proposition N15-2 (rounding tie, strict step difference, outside the window, third path, a blocked)')
mR = M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=2, alpha=(1, 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=Fr(1, 100), mus=0)
o, st = tg.s_run(mR, {'d': (1, 0)}, 1, [(1, A, 0, ()), (1, A, 0, ())])
print('  Rounding-tie example (s_run): T = P = 1, generated in trial %s, d_ℓ = %s; second trial Y = %s, τ = %s (g_ℓ = 100/199 > g_a = 1/2; both ceilings are 1)'
      % (st['gen_at'], st['d'][2], ys(o[1][0]), o[1][1]))
S0 = {'d': (1, 0, Fr(99, 100)), 'E': 1, 's': 0}
cases = [
    ('rounding tie', 1, 1, [(1, A, 0, ())]),
    ('strict step difference (T = 100)', 100, 1, [(1, A, 0, ())] * 20),
    ('outside the window (T = 10, P = 1/2)', 10, Fr(1, 2), [(Fr(1, 2), A, 0, ())] * 5),
    ('third path (both paths presented, d_b = 2)', 100, 1, [(1, BOTH, 0, ())]),
    ('a blocked, only a presented', 100, 1, [(1, A, 0, frozenset((0,)))]),
    ('a blocked, both paths presented (d_b = 2)', 100, 1, [(1, BOTH, 0, frozenset((0,)))]),
]
for name, T, P, q in cases:
    s0 = dict(S0)
    if 'third path' in name or 'both paths presented' in name:
        s0 = {'d': (1, 2, Fr(99, 100)), 'E': 1, 's': 0}
    o, st = th.n15_run(mR, s0, T, q)
    ta = tau(g(1, 1, s0['d'][0]), P, T); tl = tau(g(1, 1, s0['d'][2]), P, T); tbv = tau(g(1, 1, s0['d'][1]), P, T)
    print('  %-24s τ_a = %s, τ_ℓ = %s, τ_b = %s; readings %s; first opening of ℓ: %s' % (name, ta, tl, tbv, ' '.join(ys(Y) for Y, _ in o[:3]) + (' …' if len(o) > 3 else ''), st['open']))
# Random: characterization and persistent hiding
ch = cnt = 0; hid_tot = hid_bad = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    g0 = rq(Fr(1, 2), 1); cc = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); capa = rq(1, 2)
    m = M('N15', g0=g0, cc=cc, capa=capa, capb=4, capl=4, alpha=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=Fr(1), mus=0)
    s0 = {'d': (capa, rq(0, 3), rq(0, capa)), 'E': 1, 's': 0}
    T = R.choice([1, 5, 10, 40, 100]); P = R.choice([Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)]); pres = R.choice([A, BOTH])
    blk = R.choice([(), (0,)])
    o, st = th.n15_run(m, s0, T, [(P, pres, 0, frozenset(blk))])
    pool = [x for x in (0, 1, 2) if (x in pres or (x == 2 and 0 in pres)) and x not in blk]
    ts = {x: tau(g(g0, cc, s0['d'][x]), P, T) for x in pool}
    fin = [t for t in ts.values() if t is not None]
    pred = (2 in ts and ts[2] is not None and ts[2] == min(fin))
    cnt += 1; ch += (pred == (2 in o[0][0]))
    # Persistent hiding: rest 0, P and presentation unchanged, no blockade; if the first trial has Y = {a} (strictly), ℓ does not open in the next 20 trials
    if not blk and o[0][0] == A:
        o2, _ = th.n15_run(m, s0, T, [(P, pres, 0, frozenset())] * 20)
        hid_tot += 1; hid_bad += any(2 in Y for Y, _ in o2)
print('  %d random states: “ℓ ∈ Y ⇔ τ_ℓ is finite and equals the least first-opening step in the pool” holds in %d; first trial has Y = {a} strictly in %d, of which ℓ opens in the next 20 trials (rest 0, protocol unchanged) in %d'
      % (cnt, ch, hid_tot, hid_bad))
o, st = th.n15_run(M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=2, alpha=(0, 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=Fr(1, 2), mus=0), {'d': (1, 0, 0)}, 10, [(1, A, 0, ())] * 30)
print('  Case α_a = 0: α_a = 0, σ = 1/2, a already full, only a presented 30 times: birth %s' % st['birth'])
for T, name in ((1, 'rounding tie'), (100, 'strict step difference')):
    o, st = th.n15_run(mR, {'d': (1, 0, 0)}, T, [(1, A, 0, ())] * 5 + [(1, A, 0, frozenset((0,)))])
    print('  The three events birth, pool entry and first opening (%s, T = %d; only a presented 5 times, a blocked the 6th time): birth in trial %s, first entry into the readable pool in trial %s, first opening in trial %s' % (name, T, st['birth'], st['pool'], st['open']))
same = 0
for _ in range(1000):
    capa = rq(1, 2)
    m = M('N15', g0=rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), cc=rq(Fr(1, 2), 2), capa=capa, capb=4, capl=4, alpha=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), lam=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), sigma=rq(Fr(1, 12), 2), mus=rq(0, 1))
    q = [(R.choice([Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)]), R.choice([A, B, BOTH]), R.randint(0, 2), frozenset(x for x in (0, 1) if R.random() < 0.2)) for _ in range(R.randint(1, 15))]
    da = R.choice([capa, rq(0, capa)])
    o1, s1 = tg.s_run(m, {'d': (da, 0)}, 40, q)
    o2, s2 = th.n15_run(m, {'d': (da, 0, 0)}, 40, q)
    same += (o1 == o2 and s1['d'] == s2['d'] and s1['gen_at'] == s2['birth'])
print('  n15_run vs t16g_ext.s_run (ℓ initially absent), 1000 random runs: readings, final state and birth trial all identical in %d' % same)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ H2
print('\nH2  Two controls outside the non-interference class: delayed-transfer gating and the c4 clipping switch')
for e in (1, 0):
    pb, o = th.delayed_gate(e)
    print('  Delayed transfer e = %+d: after pretraining d_b = %s (b and the shared state unchanged); first-opening steps when only b is tested %s' % (e, pb, o))
for e in (1, 0):
    Yp, o = tg.c4_example(e)
    print('  c4 control e = %+d: first-opening steps when only b is tested %s' % (e, o))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ H3
print('\nH3  Broadcast-integrator reparameterization of N14, M = 1 + κc (Theorem N14-6)')
same = 0
for _ in range(1500):
    p = dict(g0=rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), cc=rq(Fr(1, 2), 2), alpha=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), lam=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), kappa=rq(Fr(1, 4), 2), beta=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), cbar=rq(1, 3), muc=rq(0, 1))
    m = M('N14', **p)
    d0 = (rq(0, 2), rq(0, 2)); T = R.choice([10, 40, 200])
    q = [(R.choice([Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)]), R.choice([BOTH, A, B, NONE]), R.randint(0, 2), R.choice([-1, 0, 1])) for _ in range(R.randint(1, 15))]
    o1, z1 = tf.e_run(m, {'d': d0}, T, q)
    o2, z2 = th.bim_run(m, d0, T, q)
    o3, z3 = th.n14_run(m, d0, T, q)
    same += (o1 == o2 == o3 and z1['d'] == z2['d'] == z3['d'] and z2['M'] == 1 + p['kappa'] * z1['c'])
print('  1500 random runs (including empty presentations, rest, e ∈ {−1, 0, +1}, negative M and the depth lower bound): readings and final states of N14 (e_run), BIM (M form) and the independent N14 implementation all identical, with M = 1 + κc, in %d' % same)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ H4
print('\nH4  IZD, the fixed discretization of Izhikevich 2007, and N14: S14 on q14')

def s14(run, db0, mm, n, P, T):
    o1, _ = run([(P, A, 0, 1)] * mm + [(P, B, 0, 0)] * n, db0)
    o0, _ = run([(P, A, 0, 0)] * mm + [(P, B, 0, 0)] * n, db0)
    pre = all(o1[k][0] == o0[k][0] and o1[k][0] for k in range(mm))
    return pre and o1[mm] == o0[mm] and o1[mm:] != o0[mm:]

hit = 0
for _ in range(300):
    mz = M('IZD', g0=rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), cc=rq(Fr(1, 2), 2))
    hit += s14(lambda q, db0: th.izd_run(mz, (0, db0), 200, q), rq(0, 2), R.randint(1, 4), R.randint(2, 6), R.choice([Fr(1), Fr(2)]), 200)
print('  IZD, 300 random runs (g0, c, m, n, P, d_b0 random; the other IZD parameters fixed), S14 occurs in %d' % hit)
mz = M('IZD', g0=1, cc=1)
q = [(Fr(1), A, 0, 1)] * 4 + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * 8
o1, z1 = th.izd_run(mz, (0, 0), 400, q); o0, z0 = th.izd_run(mz, (0, 0), 400, [(Fr(1), A, 0, 0)] * 4 + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * 8)
print('  Witness (zero initial state; a paired with good 4 times, then only b tested 8 times): first-opening steps of b, good arm %s | neutral arm %s; final w_b ≈ %.5f | %.5f'
      % (' '.join(str(t) for _, t in o1[4:]), ' '.join(str(t) for _, t in o0[4:]), float(z1['w'][1]), float(z0['w'][1])))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ H5
print('\nH5  Protocol B (waiting segment without local activity): Proposition N14-7')
m14 = M('N14', g0=1, cc=1, alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10), kappa=Fr(1, 2), beta=Fr(1, 4), cbar=2, muc=0)
for K in (0, 3, 10):
    qB = [(Fr(1), B, 0, 1)] + [(Fr(1), NONE, 0, 0)] * K + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)]
    o1, z1 = th.n14_run(m14, (0, 0), 400, qB)
    o2, z2 = th.izd_run(mz, (0, 0), 400, qB)
    print('  Waiting K = %2d empty-presentation trials: N14 final-test τ_b = %s (final d_b = %s); IZD final-test τ_b = %s (final w_b ≈ %.6f)' % (K, o1[-1][1], z1['d'][1], o2[-1][1], float(z2['w'][1])))
inv = cnt = diff = cond = 0
for _ in range(500):
    g0 = rq(Fr(1, 2), 1); cc = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); K = R.randint(1, 12); T = R.choice([100, 400, 1000])
    mN = M('N14', g0=g0, cc=cc, alpha=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), lam=Fr(1, 10), kappa=rq(Fr(1, 4), 2), beta=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), cbar=rq(1, 3), muc=rq(0, 1))
    mZ = M('IZD', g0=g0, cc=cc)
    q0 = [(Fr(1), B, 0, 1), (Fr(1), B, 0, 0)]
    qK = [(Fr(1), B, 0, 1)] + [(Fr(1), NONE, 0, 0)] * K + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)]
    a0, _ = th.n14_run(mN, (0, 0), T, q0); aK, _ = th.n14_run(mN, (0, 0), T, qK)
    cnt += 1; inv += (a0[-1] == aK[-1])
    b0, s0_ = th.izd_run(mZ, (0, 0), T, q0[:1]); bK, sK = th.izd_run(mZ, (0, 0), T, qK[:-1])
    w0, wK = s0_['w'][1], sK['w'][1]
    margin = Fr(T) * (g(g0, cc, w0) - g(g0, cc, wK)) >= 1
    z0, _ = th.izd_run(mZ, (0, 0), T, q0); zK, _ = th.izd_run(mZ, (0, 0), T, qK)
    cond += margin; diff += margin and (zK[-1][1] < z0[-1][1])
print('  500 random runs: N14 final-test reading same as without waiting %d/%d; IZD runs satisfying the grid margin %d, of which the final-test first-opening step is strictly earlier %d' % (inv, cnt, cond, diff))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ H6
print('\nH6  Protocol C (selective reset of the shared state): Proposition N14-8')

def armC(run, e, reset):
    q = [(Fr(1), A, 0, e)] + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * 3
    o, _ = run(q, 2 if reset else None)
    return [t for _, t in o[1:]]

runs = (('N14', lambda q, ra: th.n14_run(M('N14', g0=1, cc=1, alpha=Fr(1, 4), lam=Fr(1, 10), kappa=Fr(1, 2), beta=Fr(1, 2), cbar=2, muc=0), (0, 0), 100, q, ra)),
        ('IZD', lambda q, ra: th.izd_run(mz, (0, 0), 100, q, ra)),
        ('c4 mode', lambda q, ra: th.c4_run((0, 0), 100, q, ra)))
for name, run in runs:
    r1 = armC(run, 1, False); r0 = armC(run, 0, False); s1 = armC(run, 1, True); s0 = armC(run, 0, True)
    print('  %-6s no reset: good arm %s | neutral arm %s; shared state reset before testing: good arm %s | neutral arm %s' % (name, r1, r0, s1, s0))
eqN = eqZ = dc4 = 0
for _ in range(300):
    g0 = rq(Fr(1, 2), 1); cc = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); mm = R.randint(1, 4); n = R.randint(2, 6)
    mN = M('N14', g0=g0, cc=cc, alpha=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), lam=Fr(1, 10), kappa=rq(Fr(1, 4), 2), beta=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), cbar=rq(1, 3), muc=rq(0, 1))
    mZ = M('IZD', g0=g0, cc=cc)
    qa = [(Fr(1), A, 0, 1)] * mm + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * n; q0 = [(Fr(1), A, 0, 0)] * mm + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * n
    eqN += th.n14_run(mN, (0, 0), 200, qa, mm + 1)[0][mm:] == th.n14_run(mN, (0, 0), 200, q0, mm + 1)[0][mm:]
    eqZ += th.izd_run(mZ, (0, 0), 200, qa, mm + 1)[0][mm:] == th.izd_run(mZ, (0, 0), 200, q0, mm + 1)[0][mm:]
    dc4 += th.c4_run((0, 0), 200, qa, mm + 1)[0][mm:] != th.c4_run((0, 0), 200, q0, mm + 1)[0][mm:]
print('  300 random runs (reset before the b tests): b-test readings identical in both arms: N14 %d, IZD %d; different in the c4 mode model: %d' % (eqN, eqZ, dc4))
print('\nDone.')
~~~

A.16  run16\_8.py  Run entry: sections I0–I3 (the resolvability conditions of Proposition N14-8; the scope of Proposition N15-2 (2) and (4))
