# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_8.py (sections I1–I3).
Run: python3 run16_8.py. The output is deterministic. The program provides only finite verification, explicit witnesses and contrasts; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, math
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import t16b_core as tb
import t16h_ext as th
from t16b_core import M, g
R = random.Random(16808)
A, B, BOTH = frozenset((0,)), frozenset((1,)), frozenset((0, 1))
here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def rq(lo, hi, den=12):
    a = math.ceil(Fr(lo) * den); b = math.floor(Fr(hi) * den)
    return Fr(R.randint(a, b), den)

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()

def tau(theta, P, T):
    t = math.ceil(theta * T / P)
    return t if t <= T else None

def ys(Y):
    return '{' + ','.join({0: 'a', 1: 'b', 2: 'ℓ'}[x] for x in sorted(Y)) + '}' if Y else '∅'

print('The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_8.py')
print('=' * 72)
print('\nI0  Version')
for f in ('t16b_core.py', 't16h_ext.py'):
    print('  %s SHA-256: %s' % (f, sha(os.path.join(here, f))))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ I1
print('\nI1  The c4 branch of Proposition N14-8: internal mode differences kept apart from finite reading differences')

def c4_arms(T, P, db0, aon, n=2):
    qa = [(P, A, 0, 1)] + [(P, B, 0, 0)] * n
    q0 = [(P, A, 0, 0)] + [(P, B, 0, 0)] * n
    oa, sa = th.c4_run((0, db0), T, qa, reset_at=2, alpha_on=aon)
    o0, s0 = th.c4_run((0, db0), T, q0, reset_at=2, alpha_on=aon)
    return [t for _, t in oa[1:]], [t for _, t in o0[1:]], sa['mode'], s0['mode']

for T in (1, 100):
    ra, r0, ma, m0 = c4_arms(T, Fr(1), Fr(0), Fr(1, 4))
    pred = (tau(g(Fr(1), Fr(1), Fr(1, 4)), 1, T), tau(g(Fr(1), Fr(1), Fr(0)), 1, T))
    print('  T = %3d, P = 1, d_b0 = 0, α_on = 1/4: mode good arm %s, neutral arm %s; b readings good arm %s | neutral arm %s; second-test prediction ⌈T·g(d_b0 + α_on)/P⌉ = %s vs ⌈T·g(d_b0)/P⌉ = %s; margin T/P·[g(d_b0) − g(d_b0 + α_on)] = %s'
          % (T, ma, m0, ra, r0, pred[0], pred[1], Fr(T) * (g(Fr(1), Fr(1), Fr(0)) - g(Fr(1), Fr(1), Fr(1, 4)))))
ok = cnt = mar_yes = mar_no = diff_yes = diff_no = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    T = R.choice([1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 40, 100]); P = R.choice([Fr(1), Fr(2)]); db0 = rq(0, 2); aon = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)
    ra, r0, ma, m0 = c4_arms(T, P, db0, aon)
    t_on = tau(g(1, 1, db0 + aon), P, T); t_off = tau(g(1, 1, db0), P, T)
    pred_diff = (t_on != t_off)
    cnt += 1; ok += (pred_diff == (ra[1] != r0[1])) and ra[0] == r0[0] and ma and not m0
    margin = Fr(T) / P * (g(1, 1, db0) - g(1, 1, db0 + aon)) >= 1
    if margin:
        mar_yes += 1; diff_yes += (ra[1] != r0[1])
    else:
        mar_no += 1; diff_no += (ra[1] != r0[1])
print('  %d random runs: the modes of the two arms always differ (the good arm has switched); “second test differs ⇔ the two ceilings differ” together with an identical first test, holds in %d; margin holds in %d, of which the second test differs in %d; margin fails in %d, of which the second test differs in %d (when it fails the second tests may or may not differ)'
      % (cnt, ok, mar_yes, diff_yes, mar_no, diff_no))
eqN = eqZ = 0
for _ in range(300):
    g0 = rq(Fr(1, 2), 1); cc = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); mm = R.randint(1, 4); n = R.randint(2, 6); T = R.choice([1, 10, 200])
    mN = M('N14', g0=g0, cc=cc, alpha=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), lam=Fr(1, 10), kappa=rq(Fr(1, 4), 2), beta=rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), cbar=rq(1, 3), muc=rq(0, 1))
    mZ = M('IZD', g0=g0, cc=cc)
    qa = [(Fr(1), A, 0, 1)] * mm + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * n; q0 = [(Fr(1), A, 0, 0)] * mm + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * n
    eqN += th.n14_run(mN, (0, 0), T, qa, mm + 1)[0][mm:] == th.n14_run(mN, (0, 0), T, q0, mm + 1)[0][mm:]
    eqZ += th.izd_run(mZ, (0, 0), T, qa, mm + 1)[0][mm:] == th.izd_run(mZ, (0, 0), T, q0, mm + 1)[0][mm:]
print('  Reset branch of N14 and IZD (T ∈ {1, 10, 200}, 300 random runs): b readings identical in both arms: N14 %d, IZD %d (grid-independent; retained)' % (eqN, eqZ))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ I2
print('\nI2  Scope of Proposition N15-2 (2) and (4)')
m = M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=2, alpha=(1, 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=Fr(1, 100), mus=0)
st = {'d': (1, 2, Fr(99, 100)), 'E': 1, 's': 0}
o, _ = th.n15_run(m, st, 3, [(1, BOTH, 0, ())])
ts = [tau(g(1, 1, F), 1, 3) for F in st['d']]
print('  (2) Third path earlier: E = 1, (d_a, d_b, d_ℓ) = (1, 2, 99/100), T = 3, P = 1, both paths presented: first-opening steps (a, b, ℓ) = %s, Y = %s — a and ℓ tie, yet neither opens' % (tuple(ts), ys(o[0][0])))
o, s = th.n15_run(m, {'d': (1, 0, 0), 'E': 0}, 1, [(1, A, 0, ())] * 2)
print('  (4) Birth changes the pool: E = 0, (1, 0, 0), T = P = 1, only a presented twice: Y in turn %s, birth in trial %s — trial 1 has Y = {a} strictly, but ℓ is born within that trial, enters the pool in trial 2 and opens on a tie' % (' '.join(ys(Y) for Y, _ in o), s['birth']))
tie_ok = tie_cnt = hid_tot = hid_bad = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    g0 = rq(Fr(1, 2), 1); cc = rq(Fr(1, 2), 2); capa = rq(1, 2)
    mm_ = M('N15', g0=g0, cc=cc, capa=capa, capb=4, capl=4, alpha=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1), rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=Fr(1), mus=0)
    s0 = {'d': (capa, rq(0, 3), rq(0, capa)), 'E': 1, 's': 0}
    T = R.choice([1, 3, 5, 10, 40, 100]); P = R.choice([Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)]); pres = R.choice([A, BOTH])
    o, _ = th.n15_run(mm_, s0, T, [(P, pres, 0, frozenset())])
    pool = [0, 2] + ([1] if 1 in pres else [])
    tt = {x: tau(g(g0, cc, s0['d'][x]), P, T) for x in pool}
    fin = [t for t in tt.values() if t is not None]
    if tt[0] is not None and tt[0] == tt[2]:
        tie_cnt += 1
        both_min = fin and tt[0] == min(fin)
        tie_ok += (both_min == ({0, 2} <= set(o[0][0])))
    if o[0][0] == A:
        o2, _ = th.n15_run(mm_, s0, T, [(P, pres, 0, frozenset())] * 20)
        hid_tot += 1; hid_bad += any(2 in Y for Y, _ in o2)
print('  3000 random states (E = 1): a and ℓ tied in %d, “both open ⇔ the tied step equals the minimum over the whole pool” holds in %d; first trial has Y = {a} strictly in %d, of which ℓ opens in the next 20 trials in %d (ℓ already exists, no new position, protocol unchanged)'
      % (tie_cnt, tie_ok, hid_tot, hid_bad))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ I3
print('\nI3  Values of the coarse-grid example (T = P = 1)')
xa, sa = th.c4_run((0, 0), 1, [(Fr(1), A, 0, 1)] + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * 2, reset_at=2)
x0, s0 = th.c4_run((0, 0), 1, [(Fr(1), A, 0, 0)] + [(Fr(1), B, 0, 0)] * 2, reset_at=2)
print('  Coarse-grid example: modes %s, %s; b readings %s | %s' % (sa['mode'], s0['mode'], [t for _, t in xa[1:]], [t for _, t in x0[1:]]))
print('\nDone.')
~~~

A.17  run16\_9.py  Run entry: sections J0–J5 (current and residual terms of the depression branch of s1; absolute flux and share; the first-grid-cell construction of Theorem 16-4; the two branches of I1′ for N3; the generation count and valid domain of birth for N15)

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_9.py (sections J1–J5).
Run: python3 run16_9.py. The output is deterministic. The program provides only finite verification, explicit witnesses, and boundary and out-of-domain contrasts; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.
J1 uses snn_core.py (identical verbatim to the frozen program in Appendix A.1 of The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts v1.0).
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, math
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import snn_core as sn
import t16b_core as tb
import t16h_ext as th
from t16b_core import M
R = random.Random(16909)
A, B, BOTH = frozenset((0,)), frozenset((1,)), frozenset((0, 1))
here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def rq(lo, hi, den=12):
    a = math.ceil(Fr(lo) * den); b = math.floor(Fr(hi) * den)
    return Fr(R.randint(a, b), den)

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()

print('The Discrimination Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.0, appendix program run16_9.py')
print('=' * 72)
print('\nJ0  Version')
for f in ('snn_core.py', 't16b_core.py', 't16h_ext.py'):
    print('  %s SHA-256: %s' % (f, sha(os.path.join(here, f))))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ J1
print('\nJ1  The depression branch Φ1b of s1: current and residual terms of the postsynaptic trace (DEC = %s, A₋ = %s)' % (sn.DEC, sn.A_MINUS))
h = [('A', (1, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('A', (0, 0))]
_, ev = sn.run(h)
print('  Example J1a (zero initial state: A with teaching current (1, 0) → none → A): firing of output 0 at each beat %s; depression component at the last beat MINUS00 = %s (nonzero after 1 rest beat)'
      % ([e['s_out'][0] for e in ev], ev[-1]['comp'][(0, 0)][1]))
h2 = [('A', (1, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6 + [('none', (0, 0))] * 8 + [('A', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('A', (0, 0))]
_, e2 = sn.run(h2)
print('  Example J1b (frozen formation prefix (A+teach, none, none) × 6, rest 8 beats, then A@26, none@27, A@28): firing of output 0 at beats 26, 27, 28 %s; MINUS00@28 = %s (nonzero; when beat 28 has a current postsynaptic firing, it contains the current term −A₋)'
      % ([e2[k]['s_out'][0] for k in (26, 27, 28)], e2[28]['comp'][(0, 0)][1]))
# Upper bound of the residual term: t0 is the beat of the last postsynaptic firing; no postsynaptic firing in the r beats after it; at a probe beat with g_j = 0 and s_i = 1, MINUS_ij = −A₋·DEC^(r+1)·y_j(t0), |MINUS| < 2^−(r+4)
cnt = ok = cur = 0
pats = ['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']
for _ in range(3000):
    n0 = R.randint(1, 12)
    hist = [(R.choice(pats), (R.choice((0, 1)), 0)) for _ in range(n0)]
    st, log = sn.run(hist)
    fired = [k for k, e in enumerate(log) if e['s_out'][0]]
    if not fired:
        continue
    t0 = fired[-1]
    st2, log2 = sn.run(hist[:t0 + 1])
    y_t0 = st2['y'][0]
    r = len(hist) - 1 - t0
    st3, e3 = sn.step(st, 'A', (0, 0))
    if e3['s_out'][0]:
        cur += 1
        continue
    cnt += 1
    neg = e3['comp'][(0, 0)][1]
    ok += (neg == -sn.A_MINUS * sn.DEC ** (r + 1) * y_t0 and abs(neg) < Fr(1, 2 ** (r + 4)))
print('  3000 random histories, each followed by one probe beat A (no teaching): no postsynaptic firing at the probe beat in %d, of which “MINUS00 = −A₋·DEC^(r+1)·y₀(t₀) and |MINUS00| < 2^−(r+4)” in %d; postsynaptic firing at the probe beat (current term included) in %d, counted separately'
      % (cnt, ok, cur))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ J2
print('\nJ2  Two parallel channels of Family A type: absolute flux and share under constant head and under constant total flow')
G0, G1 = (Fr(1), Fr(1)), (Fr(2), Fr(1))
sh = lambda G: G[1] / (G[0] + G[1])
print('  Example J2a: conductances (G_a, G_b) change from (1, 1) to (2, 1). Constant head ΔP = 1: Q_b %s → %s, share %s → %s; constant total flow I = 2: Q_b %s → %s, share %s → %s'
      % (G0[1], G1[1], sh(G0), sh(G1), 2 * sh(G0), 2 * sh(G1), sh(G0), sh(G1)))
same_share = head_const = flow_drop = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    Ga, Gb, dG = rq(Fr(1, 12), 3), rq(Fr(1, 12), 3), rq(Fr(1, 12), 2)
    dP, I = rq(Fr(1, 12), 2), rq(Fr(1, 12), 2)
    s0, s1 = Gb / (Ga + Gb), Gb / (Ga + dG + Gb)
    qh0, qh1 = Gb * dP, Gb * dP
    qf0, qf1 = I * s0, I * s1
    same_share += (qh0 / (Ga * dP + qh0) == s0 and qh1 / ((Ga + dG) * dP + qh1) == s1)
    head_const += (qh0 == qh1)
    flow_drop += (qf1 < qf0)
print('  2000 random sets (G_a increased by ΔG > 0): share the same under both boundary conditions %d; Q_b unchanged under constant head %d; Q_b decreases under constant total flow %d' % (same_share, head_const, flow_drop))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ J3
print('\nJ3  Blind spot of Theorem 16-4: the same-parameter witness when only Y is read, and the first-grid-cell construction under the full reading')
com = dict(g0=(1, 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(1, 1), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None))
c = M('C', **com); n1 = M('N1', **com, phi=Fr(1, 100), mu=1)
oC, _ = tb.run(c, tb.new_state(c), 2, tb.tr(2, P=1))
oN, _ = tb.run(n1, tb.new_state(n1), 2, tb.tr(2, P=1))
print('  Example J3a (g = 1/(1 + d), α = 1, φ = 1/100, n = 2, P = 1, T = 2): Y sequences identical %s; first-opening steps C16 %s, N1 %s (full readings differ)'
      % ([Y for Y, _ in oC] == [Y for Y, _ in oN], [t for _, t in oC], [t for _, t in oN]))
ok = tot = 0
for T in (1, 2, 10, 100):
    for P in (Fr(1, 10), Fr(1), Fr(3)):
        for ntr in (1, 2, 20):
            th_ = min(Fr(1, 2), P / (2 * T)); ph = (P / T - th_) / (2 * ntr)
            cm = M('C', g0=(th_, th_), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None))
            nm = M('N1', g0=(th_, th_), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), phi=ph, mu=0)
            q = [(P, 0, BOTH, k % 3) for k in range(ntr)]
            ca, _ = tb.run(cm, tb.new_state(cm), T, q); na, _ = tb.run(nm, tb.new_state(nm), T, q)
            tot += 1
            ok += (ca == na and all(Y == BOTH and t == 1 for Y, t in ca))
print('  First-grid-cell construction (α = 0, θ* = min(1/2, P/(2T)), g0 = θ*, c = 1, zero initial state, μ = 0, φ = (P/T − θ*)/(2n)): T ∈ {1, 2, 10, 100}, P ∈ {1/10, 1, 3}, n ∈ {1, 2, 20}, rest by k mod 3: of %d sets, full readings of the two sides all identical and constantly ({a,b}, 1) in %d' % (tot, ok))
ok = tot = 0
for _ in range(1000):
    T = R.choice((1, 3, 7, 40, 400)); P = rq(Fr(1, 12), 3); ntr = R.randint(1, 30)
    th_ = min(Fr(1, 2), P / (2 * T)); ph = (P / T - th_) / (2 * ntr)
    lam = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1)
    cm = M('C', g0=(th_, th_), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(lam, lam), cap=(None, None))
    nm = M('N1', g0=(th_, th_), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(lam, lam), cap=(None, None), phi=ph, mu=0)
    q = [(P, 0, BOTH, R.randint(0, 5)) for _ in range(ntr)]
    ca, _ = tb.run(cm, tb.new_state(cm), T, q); na, _ = tb.run(nm, tb.new_state(nm), T, q)
    tot += 1
    ok += (ca == na and all(Y == BOTH and t == 1 for Y, t in ca))
print('  1000 random finite Q₀ protocols (T, P, n, λ and the per-trial rest random): full readings all identical and constantly ({a,b}, 1) in %d/%d' % (ok, tot))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ J4
print('\nJ4  I1′ of N3: the two branches of zero and nonzero store at the first trial')
m = M('N3', g0=(Fr(19, 20), 1), c=(1, 1), alpha=(0, 0), lam=(Fr(1, 10),) * 2, cap=(None, None), rho=Fr(1, 2), muh=0)
q = tb.tr(2, P=1)
out, _ = tb.run(m, tb.new_state(m, h=Fr(3, 50)), 10, q)
print('  Example J4a (θ = (19/20, 1), α = 0, h₀ = 3/50, P = 1, T = 10): Y sequence %s; the first pair has h₀ ≠ 0 and lies outside the declared domain of I1′: checking with h₀ returns %s; checking wrongly with h₀ = 0 reports a violation at pair %s'
      % (tb.seqstr(out), tb.viol_I1p(out, q, h0=Fr(3, 50)), tb.viol_I1p(out, q)))
out0, _ = tb.run(m, tb.new_state(m), 10, q)
print('  Same parameters, zero initial store: Y sequence %s; checking with h₀ = 0 returns %s' % (tb.seqstr(out0), tb.viol_I1p(out0, q)))
v0 = v1 = first_fail = n1c = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    g0 = (rq(Fr(1, 2), 1), rq(Fr(1, 2), 1))
    mm = M('N3', g0=g0, c=(rq(Fr(1, 2), 2), rq(Fr(1, 2), 2)), alpha=(rq(0, 1), rq(0, 1)), lam=(rq(Fr(1, 12), 1),) * 2,
           cap=(None, None), rho=rq(0, 1), muh=rq(0, 1))
    trials = [(Fr(R.choice((1, 1, Fr(3, 2)))), 0, BOTH, R.choice((0, 0, 0, 1))) for _ in range(R.randint(2, 8))]
    T = R.choice((3, 4, 7, 10))
    o0, _ = tb.run(mm, tb.new_state(mm), T, trials)
    v0 += tb.viol_I1p(o0, trials) is not None
    hh = rq(Fr(1, 12), min(g0) - Fr(1, 12)) if min(g0) > Fr(1, 6) else Fr(0)
    if hh > 0:
        n1c += 1
        o1, _ = tb.run(mm, tb.new_state(mm, h=hh), T, trials)
        v1 += tb.viol_I1p(o1, trials, h0=hh) is not None
        first_fail += tb.viol_I1p(o1, trials) == 0
print('  3000 random runs, zero initial store: I1′ violations %d; %d of them rerun with a nonzero initial store h₀ ∈ (0, min θ): violations within the declared domain (pairs other than the first whose preceding trial is not ∅) %d, I1 fails at the first pair %d'
      % (v0, n1c, v1, first_fail))
# ------------------------------------------------------------------ J5
print('\nJ5  Generation count and valid domain of birth for N15')
m5 = M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=2, alpha=(Fr(1, 10), 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=1, mus=0)
o, s = th.n15_run(m5, {'d': (1, 0, 0), 'E': 0, 's': Fr(9, 10)}, 10, [(1, A, 0, ())])
print('  Example J5a (initial generation amount s₀ = 9/10, σ = 1, α_a = 1/10, μ_s = 0, a initially full): born in trial %s; the zero-initial-amount count ⌈σ/α_a⌉ = %d does not apply; the nonzero-branch count is ⌈(σ − s₀)/α_a⌉ = %d'
      % (s['birth'], math.ceil(m5['sigma'] / m5['alpha'][0]), math.ceil((m5['sigma'] - Fr(9, 10)) / m5['alpha'][0])))
m6 = M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=1, alpha=(100, 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=1, mus=0)
o, s = th.n15_run(m6, {'d': (1, 0, 0), 'E': 0, 's': 0}, 10, [(1, A, 0, ())])
print('  Example J5b (outside the domain: s₀ = 0, α_a = 100 > D̄_ℓ = 1, σ = 1): birth depth d_ℓ = %s > D̄_ℓ = %s (violates the antecedent D̄_ℓ ≥ α_a; the birth state is not valid)' % (s['d'][2], m6['capl']))
legal = lower = bound = 0
for _ in range(2000):
    al = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1); capl = R.choice((al, al + rq(0, 2), None))
    sig = rq(Fr(1, 12), 3); mus = R.choice((0, rq(0, 1)))
    s0 = R.choice((Fr(0), rq(0, sig - Fr(1, 12)) if sig > Fr(1, 12) else Fr(0)))
    mm = M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=capl if capl is not None else 10 ** 9, alpha=(al, 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=sig, mus=mus)
    rests = R.choice((0, 1))
    tr_ = [(1, A, rests if R.random() < 0.3 else 0, ()) for _ in range(80)]
    o, s = th.n15_run(mm, {'d': (1, 0, 0), 'E': 0, 's': s0}, 10, tr_)
    if s['birth'] is None:
        continue
    legal += 1
    bound += (0 <= s['d'][2] < al)
    need = math.ceil((sig - s0) / al)
    lower += s['birth'] >= need
print('  2000 random runs in the valid domain (σ > 0, μ_s ∈ [0, 1], D̄_a = 1, D̄_ℓ ≥ α_a or ∞, 0 ≤ s₀ < σ, a initially full, only a presented): births %d; birth depth in [0, α_a) %d; birth trial ≥ ⌈(σ − s₀)/α_a⌉ %d'
      % (legal, bound, lower))
ex_tot = ex_ok = 0
for _ in range(1000):
    al = rq(Fr(1, 12), 1); sig = rq(Fr(1, 12), 3); s0 = R.choice((Fr(0), rq(0, sig - Fr(1, 12)) if sig > Fr(1, 12) else Fr(0)))
    mm = M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=al, alpha=(al, 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=sig, mus=0)
    o, s = th.n15_run(mm, {'d': (1, 0, 0), 'E': 0, 's': s0}, 10, [(1, A, 0, ())] * 80)
    ex_tot += 1
    ex_ok += (s['birth'] == math.ceil((sig - s0) / al))
print('  1000 random runs with μ_s = 0 and no rest: birth trial exactly ⌈(σ − s₀)/α_a⌉ in %d/%d' % (ex_ok, ex_tot))
rows = []
for al in (Fr(1, 4), Fr(3, 10), Fr(1, 3), Fr(3, 8), Fr(1, 2)):
    mm = M('N15', g0=1, cc=1, capa=1, capb=2, capl=1, alpha=(al, 0, 0), lam=Fr(1, 10), sigma=1, mus=0)
    o, s = th.n15_run(mm, {'d': (1, 0, 0), 'E': 0, 's': 0}, 10, [(1, A, 0, ())] * 10)
    rows.append('α_a = %s: trial %s' % (al, s['birth']))
print('  Stepwise dependence (σ = 1, s₀ = 0): ' + '; '.join(rows) + ' (values of α_a with the same ⌈1/α_a⌉ give the same generation trial; the plateaus are not counterexamples)')
print('\nDone.')
~~~

## Appendix B  Run Results

&nbsp;&nbsp;Statistic: S = number of trials, among the declared tail segment of n′ trials, whose report shows the specified pattern; decision rule S ≥ τ. On one side the true value never shows the pattern; on the other, the tail segment always shows it.

Q3  Positive depth recovery and distinguishable sole-lead recovery are adjudicated separately: same-cell example  
&nbsp;&nbsp;Same-cell example s = 1/400, E = 1/200, μ\_s = 1/2, μ\_E = 3/4, T = 40: over R = 1..79, depth positive 78 times, sole lead 0 times; maximal recovery depth 1/3200  
&nbsp;&nbsp;Analytic bound: for every R, δ\_R ≤ s·(1 − μ\_s)^R ≤ 1/800 \< δ\_need = 1/39 (with P = 1, T = 40, g = 1/(1 + d), the negation of the sufficient lower bound on the depth a sole lead needs: g(0) − g(δ) ≤ 1/40 ⇔ δ ≤ 1/39), so a distinguishable recovery never occurs.  
&nbsp;&nbsp;Positive example (the witness member of Section R4 of run16\_1; s and E after extinction are the actual final states, T = 400): R=4 depth 0.00000 margin g(0)−g(δ)−P/T=-0.00250 reading {a,b}; R=8 depth 0.01419 margin g(0)−g(δ)−P/T=0.01149 reading {a}; R=12 depth 0.00598 margin g(0)−g(δ)−P/T=0.00344 reading {a}; R=16 depth 0.00198 margin g(0)−g(δ)−P/T=-0.00052 reading {a,b}; R=24 depth 0.00020 margin g(0)−g(δ)−P/T=-0.00230 reading {a,b}  
&nbsp;&nbsp;Reading: a distinguishable sole-lead recovery is signed only at concrete R with margin \> 0 (a sufficient condition); with margin ≤ 0 the reading can be a joint opening, even when the depth is positive.
